We attribute this gap to a computation-difficulty mismatch: within a partially observed sequence, some unknown tokens are easy to predict, while others require substantially more computation. Existing DLMs nevertheless apply uniform computational depth to all unknown positions at each denoising step. We introduce ALoDLM, which replaces uniform computation with token-adaptive latent recurrence. At each denoising step, ALoDLM iteratively refines latent representations and allocates computation according to token difficulty. Tokens ready to commit are fed back as discrete context, while unresolved tokens retain and further refine their latent states through additional recurrent passes. To learn token prediction and computation allocation jointly, we formulate token-wise computation schedules as latent variables and derive a conditional negative evidence lower bound (NELBO). We train ALoDLM at 1.7B and 8B parameter scales. Across eleven benchmarks, ALoDLM outperforms all evaluated DLMs and the corresponding AR baselines in average benchmark score at both scales. ALoDLM also retains fast parallel decoding, yielding a strong quality-efficiency trade-off among evaluated autoregressive and diffusion models under optimized inference engines.

</details>


### [34] [Fine-Tuning VLM for Enhancing AI's Spatial Intelligence: Understanding 3D and 2D Rotations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04206)
*Uttamasha Monjoree,Wei Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过在多套旋转数据集上微调 Gemma-4 MoE 模型，作者显著提升了VLM在2D/3D旋转轴与角度预测的能力，且发现线性特征比物体可识别性更有助于角度检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空间智能在STEM、医学、建筑等领域至关重要，而现有视觉-语言模型在空间推理与旋转理解上仍存在限制，研究旨在提升VLMs在实际空间任务中的性能与适用性。

Method: 构建并/或使用多套物体旋转数据集作为训练与评估集，对比微调的 Google DeepMind Gemma-4 MoE（混合专家）与 Gemma-4 通用体模型在2D与3D旋转检测上的表现，测量轴与角度预测精度；分析微调前后性能差异，并检验对象可识别性与线性特征对角度检测的影响。

Result: 在多套旋转数据集上取得了良好改善：微调后模型在旋转轴与角度预测上性能提升明显；MoE 架构优于通用架构；2D角度估计在无显式坐标系统下亦显著改善；物体是否易识别与角度检测无显著正相关，线性特征强的物体检测效果更好。

Conclusion: 经验证，微调的 Gemma-4 MoE 模型在2D和3D物体旋转检测任务上显著优于微调的 Gemma-4 通用模型；微调显著提升了2D角度估计能力，且不依赖显式坐标系；物体可识别性并未提升角度检测精度，但具有明显线性特征的物体表现更好。

Abstract: Spatial intelligence is a fundamental skill in multiple domains, such as Science, Technology, Engineering, and Mathematics (STEM), Medicine, Architecture, and Construction. Recent studies indicate that Vision-Language Models (VLMs) still face limitations in spatial reasoning, which inhibits artificial intelligence (AI) from performing practical spatial tasks. Using multiple object-rotation datasets developed for training and evaluation, our experiments demonstrated promising improvements in both 2D and 3D rotation detection. Fine-tuned Google DeepMind-built Gemma-4 mixture-of-experts (MoE) models significantly outperformed fine-tuned Gemma-4 generalist models in predicting rotations defined by both their axes and angles. Fine-tuning also substantially improved angle estimation for 2D representation without requiring an explicit coordinate system. Furthermore, identifiable objects did not improve angle-detection accuracy; instead, objects with prominent linear features showed improved performance.

</details>


### [35] [TCMClinicalReason-Bench: Can Language Models Reason from Pathogenesis to Prescription over Real-World Clinical Cases?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04215)
*Jirui Dai,Chenkai Zhang,Yan Jia,Yukai Wang,Ruiyang He,Changyong Luo,Zhi Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 构建了以证据为约束的中医临床推理评测基线，揭示LLM在结构完整但证据支撑不足、处方相关环节最大风险、通用模型优于小型中医模型、跨块一致性中等且与内容相关，并通过扰动测试表明遗漏型错误最难被自动/人工检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM能生成流畅但缺乏病例证据支撑的临床叙述；在中医语境下，病因病机误判会纵向传导至证候、治则和处方，存在安全与质量隐患，因此需要一个能区分‘表面流畅’与‘基于个案证据’回答的基准。

Method: 使用2000例多中心EHR病例、制定证据约束的评分量表，评估7个诊疗组件和3个跨块关系（允许基于案例的合理备选项）；在零样本设置下测试5个通用LLM和2个中医专用LLM；以Qwen3.7-Plus+中医检索作为自动评判器，并由5位高级中医师对600例进行盲审；对100例进行扰动压力测试以评估对矛盾与遗漏的检测能力。

Result: 结构完整率接近饱和（99.3–100%），归一化内容得分仅40.7–54.1%；5个通用模型平均50.0%，2个较小中医模型平均41.3%；跨块逻辑一致性60.8–66.8%，与内容得分呈中等相关（Pearson r=0.515–0.656）。表现最差的环节为处方生成、处方分析和症状驱动的处方修改。扰动检测（100例）在三种关系上的检出率分别约为57%、56%和31%，对矛盾的检测优于对遗漏的检测。

Conclusion: 基于2000例多中心电子病历构建的TCMClinicalReason-Bench有效揭示了大语言模型在中医临床推理中的落地短板：尽管结构完整性接近饱和，生成内容的病例证据支撑率仍然偏低，且处方相关环节错误最多。将组件质量与跨块逻辑一致性分离，有助于定位难以被终点或完整性指标发现的失败点，从而明确需要人工审查的环节。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can generate clinical narratives that are insufficiently grounded in patient-specific evidence. In traditional Chinese medicine (TCM), errors can propagate from etiology and pathogenesis through syndrome diagnosis and treatment principles to prescription generation. We developed TCMClinicalReason-Bench using 2,000 multicenter electronic health record cases to distinguish case-grounded responses from fluent but unsupported diagnostic and therapeutic conclusions. Five general-purpose and two TCM-specific LLMs were evaluated in zero-shot settings. An evidence-constrained rubric assessed seven diagnostic and therapeutic components and three cross-block relations, allowing case-supported alternatives. Qwen3.7-Plus with TCM retrieval served as the automated judge, alongside parallel blinded ratings by five senior TCM clinicians on a 600-case subset. Structural completeness was nearly saturated (99.3-100.0%), but normalized content scores ranged from 40.7% to 54.1%. The five general-purpose models averaged 50.0%, versus 41.3% for the two smaller TCM-specific models. Cross-block logic consistency ranged from 60.8% to 66.8% and correlated moderately with content across cases (Pearson's r = 0.515-0.656). Deficits were greatest in prescription generation, prescription analysis, and symptom-guided modification. In judge stress testing on 100 independent cases, perturbation detection rates across the three relations were 57%, 56%, and 31%, with contradictions detected more reliably than omissions. Separating component quality from cross-block consistency localizes failures missed by endpoint and completeness metrics and identifies where clinician oversight remains necessary.

</details>


### [36] [On the Steering Dimensionality of Refusal in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04245)
*Han Wang,Erik Miehling,Dennis Wei,Karthikeyan Natesan Ramamurthy,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: The paper defines steering dimensionality and shows that safety-triggered refusal is 1-D steerable but general refusal requires much higher-dimensional subspaces, implying single-vector steering is often inadequate.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Challenge the common assumption that a single steering direction mediates high-level concepts; determine how many directions are required to control different refusal behaviors and whether a single vector suffices.

Method: Formalize steering subspaces and introduce steering dimensionality as minimal subspace dimension that reliably controls a behavior; characterize sufficient subspaces by observing monotonic improvement up to a threshold dimension and saturation beyond it; perform empirical experiments measuring additive steering and directional ablation for two types of refusal behaviors across steering subspaces of increasing dimensionality.

Result: Refusal behavior triggered by safety alignment is effectively 1-dimensional (many distinct single directions can control it), whereas refusal in general contexts has substantially richer activation geometry that is not captured by up to 5-dimensional subspaces; steering dimensionality is behavior- and context-dependent.

Conclusion: Activation geometry underlying complex behaviors (like refusal) can be higher-dimensional and context-dependent; single linear steering vectors are insufficient in general.

Abstract: Existing activation steering methods often assume that a high-level concept can be mediated by a single steering direction. To support this, two complementary interventions should be achieved: additive steering should induce the target behavior, while directional ablation should suppress it. Yet behaviors may occupy richer activation geometries beyond a single direction, and semantically similar behaviors may be represented by distinct directions. In this work, we study how many directions can reliably control two different types of refusal behaviors: refusal triggered by the safety alignment and refusal in general contexts. Given the limited expressive capability of a single steering vector, we study the general setting of steering subspaces and introduce the notion of steering dimensionality as the minimum subspace dimensionality required to reliably control a behavior. We characterize sufficient steering subspaces that cover the full extent of the target behavior through both the (monotonic) improvement before the sufficient dimensionality, and the saturation beyond it. Empirically, we find that refusal triggered by safety alignment is 1-dim steerable, while multiple distinct steering directions can achieve comparable control. In contrast, refusal in general contexts exhibits substantially richer activation geometry where even 5-dim steering subspaces fail to reliably capture its full steerable variation. Our results reveal that the activation geometry underlying refusal is highly context-dependent and can be substantially more complex than a single linear steering direction.

</details>


### [37] [Spec2Game: Can LLMs Generate Complete Playable Games from Detailed Specifications?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04253)
*Yixue Cai,Yuzhe Zhao,Hanxiang Chao,Qingsen Ma,Ziheng Xiong,Jinhu Qi,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Spec2Game基准揭示了LLM在从自然语言规范生成交互式程序时的核心短板：能跑不代表正确，尤其难以可靠地实现规则与终止条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM能生成可运行代码，但缺乏对复杂行为规范的忠实实现能力；需要一个细粒度、可测量的基准来量化模型在把自然语言规范实现为完整交互式程序方面的能力。

Method: 提出并构建Spec2Game基准：包含15个游戏家族、150个任务（每个家族1个规范任务+9个受控规则变体），覆盖三种实现复杂度。对14个LLM生成的3,330个Pygame项目，基于源码、运行时与视觉证据，从可执行性、规范实现、代码质量与用户体验四个维度进行评估，并在组件层面细分为“游戏元素建模”“规则与机制建模”“目标与终止建模”。

Result: 实验发现：高可执行率并不保证规范实现的忠实性；模型在‘游戏元素建模’上的表现显著好于‘规则与机制建模’和‘目标与终止建模’；实现游戏规则与终止逻辑仍是主要挑战。

Conclusion: 本研究表明：仅生成可执行程序并不等同于满足自然语言行为规范；当前大规模语言模型在实现详细交互式程序规范时存在明显缺陷，尤其是关于规则与终止条件的忠实实现。

Abstract: Generating an executable program does not necessarily mean that it correctly implements the behavioral requirements specified in natural language. To evaluate large language models' ability to realize detailed specifications as complete interactive programs, we introduce Spec2Game, a benchmark that requires models to generate complete Pygame projects from detailed natural-language game specifications. Spec2Game comprises 15 game families and 150 task instances, with one canonical task and nine controlled rule variants per family, spanning three levels of implementation complexity. Using source-code, runtime, and visual evidence, we evaluate generated projects along four dimensions---Executability, Specification Realization, Code Quality, and User-Facing Quality. Across 14 LLMs and 3,330 generated projects, we find that high executability does not imply faithful specification realization. Component-level analysis further shows that models perform substantially better on Game Element Modeling than on Rule and Mechanism Modeling or Goal and Termination Modeling, indicating that faithfully implementing game rules and termination logic remains a major challenge.

</details>


### [38] [Functionally Equivalent or Not? Graph-Grounded Differential Surrogate Execution for Code Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04371)
*Amit Kachroo,Like Hui,Haitao Mao,Yuhao Zhang,Nguyen Vo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FEAgent is a selective, evidence-led equivalence assessor combining typed program-graph queries and differential surrogate execution via blinded LLMs; it outputs EQUIVALENT/INEQUIVALENT/UNCLEAR with an evidence ledger and reconciler, and uncovers nontrivial label/test gaps in benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing signals for program equivalence (tests, textual similarity, unconstrained LLM judgments) are incomplete or unauditable, and direct execution can be impossible due to unavailable/unsafe environments. There is a need for an auditable, uncertainty-aware assessor that can operate when execution is constrained.

Method: Align public interfaces and behaviorally relevant program-graph anchors; issue bounded graph-queries across call/control/data-flow, types, imports, and effects; use a branch-aware generator to propose discriminating inputs; run two blinded LLM surrogates to predict observables for source and target; record claims in an evidence ledger; use a deterministic reconciler to return a final judgment or UNCLEAR when evidence conflicts or paths are uncovered.

Result: On EquiBench, FEAgent's disagreements with published labels were confirmed by execution for 216 of 1,200 pairs (18.0%). On SWE-bench Verified, 94 of 331 test-passing agent patches (28.4%) diverged from the reference patch. FEAgent finds benchmark-label errors and divergences missed by unit-test-only scoring.

Conclusion: FEAgent provides a middle ground between testing and formal verification by producing reviewable, evidence-backed equivalence judgments (EQUIVALENT, INEQUIVALENT, or UNCLEAR) without claiming formal proof.

Abstract: Determining whether two programs are functionally equivalent is central to code modernization, patch validation, refactoring, and code-generation evaluation. Yet the usual signals are incomplete: tests cover only finite inputs, textual similarity confuses implementation with behavior, and unconstrained LLM judgments are difficult to audit. Direct execution is often impossible when a program depends on an obsolete, licensed, unavailable, or unsafe environment. We introduce FEAgent, a selective equivalence assessor agent that combines typed program-graph evidence with differential surrogate execution. FEAgent first aligns public interfaces and behaviorally relevant graph anchors, then issues bounded queries over call-flow, control-flow, data-flow, type, import, and effect relations. Next, a branch-aware generator agent proposes discriminating inputs, and two blinded LLM surrogates independently predict source and target observables. Every claim and predicted divergence is recorded in an evidence ledger. A deterministic reconciler then returns EQUIVALENT, INEQUIVALENT, or UNCLEAR rather than forcing a verdict when paths are uncovered or evidence conflicts. We evaluate FEAgent on function-level equivalence and repository-level bug patches, where the existing oracle is a benchmark label or a passing test suite. Every disagreement with that oracle is adjudicated by direct execution, revealing errors in benchmark labels and behavioral divergences missed by unit-test-only scoring. On EquiBench, execution confirms FEAgent's disagreements with published labels on 216 of 1,200 evaluated pairs (18.0%); on SWE-bench Verified, 94 of 331 test-passing agent patches (28.4%) diverge from the reference patch. FEAgent thus serves as an audit layer between testing and formal verification, keeping its evidence reviewable and its uncertainty explicit without claiming a proof of equivalence.

</details>


### [39] [CADForge: Agentic Single-View CAD Reconstruction with Explicit Geometry Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04262)
*Keyang Lu,Zhifei Yang,Tianao Dong,Mingzhe Xing,Zhen Xiao,Yikai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CADForge通过组件化的几何推理+代码化参数映射+评审反馈循环，将单视图图像转为可执行的CadQuery参数化程序，显著提升单视图CAD重建的可编辑性与保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单视图重建可直接产出可编辑的参数化CAD模型，对制造与后续修改极有价值，但受观测不全与部件间复杂几何关系限制，需显式几何推理和可执行程序化建模能力以获得可编辑、高保真的结果。

Method: CADForge是一个多代理流水线：先将目标对象分解为CAD语义组件；为每个组件识别CAD相关约束（例如几何关系、尺寸约束等）；通过数学代码将这些约束翻译为精确的建模参数；基于推断出的参数合成可执行的CadQuery脚本；引入评审(agent)对生成几何体进行检测与打分并反馈用于迭代精化；在此基础上采用失败引导的工具集构建（将常见失败模式总结成可复用工具）和紧凑的参数化CAD记忆用于按需检索建模上下文以提高鲁棒性与效率。

Result: 在多种单部件与多部件物体上，CADForge在重建几何保真度和主观感知质量上均显著优于现有基线方法；系统性组件（分解、评审、工具学习、记忆检索）提升了重建稳定性与效率。

Conclusion: CADForge提出了一套将单视图图像逐步转换为可编辑参数化CAD（CadQuery）程序的智能代理框架，并通过组件分解、显式几何推理、基于代码的参数化映射、逐步合成与评审-反馈循环，以及失败引导的工具提取与参数化CAD记忆机制，实现了相比先前方法更高的重建保真度与感知质量。实验表明该方法在单/多部件对象上稳健优于基线。

Abstract: Reconstructing editable parametric CAD models from a single-view image is of great practical value for modern manufacturing, yet remains challenging due to incomplete geometric observations and complex inter-part relationships. To address it, we propose CADForge, an agentic framework that progressively converts a single image into CadQuery programs. CADForge decomposes an object into CAD-meaningful components and performs explicit geometric reasoning for each component, a process that first identifies CAD-relevant constraints and then translates them into precise modeling parameters through mathematical code. The inferred parameters then drive component-wise synthesis of executable CadQuery programs, with a review agent evaluating the resulting geometry and providing targeted feedback for iterative refinement. To further improve robustness and efficiency, CADForge incorporates a failure-guided toolkit construction mechanism to distill accumulated experience into tools, and maintains a compact parametric CAD memory for retrieving modeling context on demand. Experiments on diverse single- and multi-part objects show that CADForge consistently outperforms existing baselines in reconstruction fidelity and perceptual quality, demonstrating an effective approach to accurate single-view CAD reconstruction.

</details>


### [40] [Do Tool Calls Execute as Intended? Measuring and Repairing Intent-Execution Correspondence in LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04375)
*Boyang Yang,Zhenhao Li,Ziyao Yang,Kanghui Jia,Xin Yin,Mingmou Liu,Haoye Tian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Calls traverse multiple hops that can silently change them; define intent-execution correspondence (IEC) and instrument per-hop observation. IntAct enforces IEC and recovers most changed-call failures; benchmarks should test and design harnesses hop-by-hop.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM agents make tool calls that pass through multiple software hops; any hop can silently transform calls causing incorrect execution. Standard benchmarks and failure analyses observe only emitted call and result, missing intermediate transformations so they undercount and misattribute failures.

Method: Define IEC formally. Instrument the call path to record what each hop receives (without executing). Identify the first hop that changes the call per the receiver's own parser. Implement IntAct to either deliver the call in an unmodifiable form or refuse it. Build IEC-Bench from real-world observed changes across four harnesses and measure in production sessions and benchmarks.

Result: In 47,828 production shell calls, Claude Code's Bash tool altered 12.0% of calls carrying code/escapes/long text. 80.7% of calls with modified backslashes ran wrong actions without error. All 10 measured harnesses change calls. Trajectory-based judgment attributes 95.1% of production failures to the LLM, but more than half were caused by the path. On IEC-Bench, path increases token cost per passed task by 2.4x on average (up to 12.3x). A modifying hop hides its changes, so 55.1% of failures on one path appear only after repairing first hop. IntAct recovers 79.2% of failures with changed calls when deployed commercially.

Conclusion: Benchmarks must account for per-hop transformations: ensuring intent-execution correspondence (IEC) prevents silent changes and failures. IntAct, a protocol that observes what each hop receives and fixes or refuses altered calls, recovers most changed-call failures and reduces wasted retries.

Abstract: Agents built on large language models (LLMs) build and run software through tool calls. A call reaches its program through several hops, and any hop can change the call without notice. When the changed call fails, the agent retries a correct call, which costs users time and money. Benchmarks and failure analyses do not see the change, because they read the call and its result but not what a hop received. We define intent-execution correspondence (IEC) as the property that the executed action matches the action the emitted call denotes under the tool contract. Our protocol observes what each hop received without executing the call, and names the first hop that changed it by the receiver's own parser. IntAct then delivers the call in a form that this hop cannot alter, or refuses the call. We build IEC-Bench from the changes observed in real-world use, with chains of dependent calls under the execution paths of 4 widely-used harnesses.
  In 47,828 shell calls within production sessions, Claude Code's Bash tool changes 12.0% of the calls that carry code, escape sequences, or long text. For 80.7% of the calls whose backslashes are changed, the wrong action runs without any reported error. All 10 measured harnesses change a call. Trajectory-based judgment attributes 95.1% of the production failures to the LLM, although the path caused more than half of them. On IEC-Bench, the path raises the token cost per passed task 2.4 times (up to 12.3 times). A hop that changes a call also hides the changes after it, so 55.1% of the failures on one path appear only after its first hop is repaired. IntAct, deployed in a commercial product, recovers 79.2% of the failures with a changed call. Harnesses should therefore be designed and tested hop-by-hop to ensure a correct call executes as intended or is refused.

</details>


### [41] [Dense Neuro-Symbolic Reasoning in a Unified Geometry State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04280)
*Ruoran Xu,Wending Gao,Haoyu Cheng,Xiaoqiang Kang,Qiufeng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种在有类型状态和可执行动作上密集耦合神经与符号模块的几何推理框架，并在OmniGeo中实现，显著提升了多类几何题的求解率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 几何推理天然是有状态的、交替进行的搜索—需要同时处理结构选择（启发）与精确推导（演绎）。单纯神经或单纯符号方法难以兼顾启发性探索与精确性验证，因此需要紧耦合的混合方案。

Method: 提出“密集神经-符号耦合”框架：用一个共享的有类型状态表示搜索过程，神经模块提出定理实例、构造步骤与代数桥接，符号运行时执行注册规则、传播精确约束并记录溯源；采用嵌套控制器先在神经/符号提议源间分配计算预算，再在被接纳的动作间分配；在此框架下实现统一求解器OmniGeo，涵盖平面、解析与立体几何，结合Claude Sonnet 4.6作为神经提议器。

Result: OmniGeo在三个数据集上分别达到94.2%（FormalGeo7K）、88.5%（Conic10K）、89.8%（SolidFGeo），宏平均90.8%；在IMO-AG-30基准上解决21/30道题，显示强实用能力。

Conclusion: 将神经引导与符号执行在“有类型状态 + 可执行动作”的框架下进行密集耦合，能有效捕捉几何推理中“结构提议 ↔ 精确演绎”的交替过程，从而显著提升几何题求解性能。实例系统OmniGeo在多种几何任务上取得了高精度，并能攻克多数IMO风格难题。

Abstract: Geometry reasoning is naturally stateful: solving a problem repeatedly alternates between structural proposals and exact deductions. We formulate this process as dense neural-symbolic coupling, in which neural guidance and symbolic execution share a typed state and communicate through executable actions at every search step. Neural proposals contribute theorem instances, constructions, and algebraic bridges; the symbolic runtime applies registered rules, propagates exact constraints, and records provenance. A nested controller allocates computation first between neural and symbolic proposal sources and then among admitted actions. We instantiate the framework in OmniGeo, a single solver for plane, analytic, and solid geometry. With Claude Sonnet 4.6, OmniGeo reaches 94.2%, 88.5%, and 89.8% on FormalGeo7K, Conic10K, and SolidFGeo, respectively (90.8% macro average), and solves 21/30 IMO-AG-30 problems.

</details>


### [42] [What Does a Harness Buy? Tokens, Mostly](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04433)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在固定模型下，harness对通过率的多数影响被运行间噪声掩盖；真正显著的负面效应源于实现缺陷（如输出截断）；但harness对成本的影响很大，且由每次调用的开销主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 业界和排行榜常混合或夸大harness改动带来的提升，但很少有研究把模型固定时harness本身对得分和成本的影响量化；目的是衡量harness更改带来的真实效应及其可检测性。

Method: 在SWE-bench Verified上，将五种模型通过三个生产harness（Claude Code、mini-SWE-agent、OpenCode）运行；使用447项任务的全量池和45项难题子集；对相同配置进行重复运行以量化运行间噪声；比较不同harness之间的得分差异、任务翻转率和成本构成，并进行统计功效评估。

Result: 在447任务与两个被反复运行的模型上，Claude Code与mini-SWE-agent在±5分内等效；在45任务的困难子集上，不同harness间任务翻转比例（≈13%）与同一harness重跑的翻转比例相当；OpenCode在大池上落后最多9分，其中一半可归因于输出截断；相同模型与任务下成本在不同harness间可达3倍差异，差异主要由每次调用附带的系统提示与工具schema长度决定；统计功效分析表明45任务对13分差异的检测力仅约50%，447任务能分辨约5分但仍比许多声称的改进粗糙。

Conclusion: 在固定模型下，大多数生产级harness对SWE-bench指标的影响与单次重跑的随机波动相当，显著影响主要来自于个别实现（如OpenCode的输出截断导致的损失）以及预设的系统提示和工具schema带来的调用成本，后者会把费用放大至最多3倍。

Abstract: A coding agent is a language model wrapped in a harness: the system prompt, the tool set, and the context management that turn a chat model into something that can work inside a repository. Production harnesses ship releases daily, vendors advertise pass-rate gains from harness changes, and leaderboards mix harnesses freely. What is rarely measured is how much the harness itself moves the score when the model is held fixed. We run five models through three production harnesses, Claude Code, mini-SWE-agent, and OpenCode, on SWE-bench Verified, and rerun the same configurations to calibrate how much a score moves when nothing changes but the run. On 447 tasks and the two models we ran there, Claude Code and mini-SWE-agent, the heaviest and the lightest harness, are equivalent within five points. On a 45-task hard subset and five models, swapping the harness flips as many tasks as rerunning the same harness, 13% in both cases, and the tasks a harness wins in one run are not the tasks it wins in the next. The one harness effect that clears the noise is a loss, not a gain: OpenCode trails by up to 9 points on the large pool, and on one model half of that gap sits in runs its output cap cut short. What the harness does decide is the bill. With the same model, the same tasks, and one price list, cost per task differs by up to 3x across harnesses. The gap is set at the first call, by the preamble of system prompt and tool schemas each harness sends with every step, and scaled by the number of steps; per-step growth and per-call tool output differ far less. The provider's price for cached input scales the bill and does not reorder it. The rerun data also give the resolution a harness comparison needs: at the discordance we observe, 45 tasks catch a 13-point gap only half the time and no gap with 80% power, and 447 tasks resolve 5 points, still coarser than the gains many harness changes claim.

</details>


### [43] [VIGIL: Verifier-Informed Gated Improvement Loop for Spreadsheet Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04287)
*Kang Li,Lu He,Sandarsita Guntupalli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Enterprise agents should improve from delayed feedback without allowing every correction to rewrite system behavior. We study continual harness learning for corpus-level spreadsheet question answering. Building on FiCo (Find-then-Compute), a static retrieval-and-execution backbone, we introduce VIGIL (Verifier-Informed Gated Improvement Loop). Within a question, VIGIL verifies and repairs diversely prompted Structured Query Language (SQL) candidates. Across episodes, delayed labels update only a contract calibrator and query/result-column selector. The base model, prompts, retriever, and recorded candidate pool remain fixed in the continual-learning protocol. With the gold workbook and expected type supplied, the ungated and dual-gated full-replay variants reach 82.4% and 82.0% forward accuracy over 79 documents, from a 75.8% static baseline. The dual gate has the larger retrospective gain (2.7 versus 2.3 points), and its 6.3-point forward gain has a 95% t-interval of 5.6-6.9. In a separate stricter split that excludes 16 documents and 380 questions from fitting and online promotion, the accuracy-only gate raises mean held-out accuracy across ten final harnesses from 80.3% to 86.7%. Calibrator-only adaptation gains 5.9 points, close to the combined 6.4-point gain. Yet three of 26 gate-approved updates reduce held-out accuracy relative to their incumbents, so replay-buffer non-regression does not imply held-out non-regression. MiMoTable and external-task case studies test within-task verification and reuse the same promote-or-retain discipline. Overall, the results support bounded, auditable harness adaptation while revealing where finite replay gates fail to generalize beyond their promotion buffers.

</details>


### [44] [LMBuild: Evaluating LLM Agents for Generating Buildable and Functional Structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04292)
*Jiateng Liu,Rushi Wang,Cheng Qian,Xuejun Zhang,Sun Li,Jiayu Liu,Yifan Shen,Xu Cao,Jiarui Yao,Bingxuan Li,Ruhi Sarikaya,Heng Ji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LMBuild 提供了一个面向“可构建且具功能性结构”的评测基准与环境，揭示了当前 LLM 代理在功能推理、机械性与新零件设计方面的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 驱动三维生成研究侧重几何品质，而忽视生成物体是否可建造并可实现预期功能——作者旨在填补“几何到可构建功能物体”这一评价空白。

Method: 作者提出 LMBuild 基准与统一框架，包含：一个交互式环境（代理可用工具检索、创建、放置零件以构建物体）；一个从既有 CAD 数据集重新整理并以维基百科知识增强的标注基准；及一套从结构健全性、功能可用性、设计质量到物理实现的评估管线。并在 30 个系统上进行横向评测。

Result: 跨 30 个系统的评测揭示：(a) 对齐与结构健全性已经不是前沿闭源模型的主要瓶颈；(b) 功能可用性与物理可操作性仍然显著困难；(c) 强模型更倾向于创建新零件，弱模型更多依赖检索；(d) 给出功能规范能显著提升零件完整性、运动学合理性与物理可操作性。

Conclusion: LMBuild 的核心结论是：评估生成可构建且具功能性的三维结构，比仅评估几何优雅更具挑战性；现有前沿闭源模型在结构健全性和对齐上表现良好，但在功能性可用性与物理可操作性方面仍有显著不足。

Abstract: LLM-based agents are increasingly capable of generating complex 3D structures, with the potential to reshape how objects are designed and realized in the physical world. Yet, producing elegant geometry is fundamentally different from producing objects that can be built and perform their intended functions. Existing evaluations largely focus on geometric quality while overlooking physical realizability. We introduce LMBuild, a benchmark for evaluating LLM agents on generating buildable and functional structures. LMBuild represents generated objects as assembled structures comprising part decompositions, joints, materials, and sequences. To support reproducible evaluation, we provide a unified framework consisting of: (1) an interactive environment in which agents can use tools to retrieve, create, and place components to construct objects; (2) a curated benchmark that repurposes established CAD datasets and augments them with knowledge from Wikipedia; and (3) a evaluation framework covering structural soundness, functional affordance, design quality, and physical realization. Evaluations across 30 systems reveal several intriguing findings: (a) Soundness and alignment are no longer the primary bottlenecks for frontier closed-source models, while functional affordance and physical operability remain substantially more challenging; (b) stronger models more effectively create new components, whereas weaker models tend to rely on retrieval; and (c) providing functional specifications substantially improves part completeness, kinematics, and physical operability. These results show that generating real-world structures requires deeper reasoning about functional affordances, mechanics, and designing and creating novel components. We expect LMBuild to provide a foundation for measuring progress and incentivizing research toward agents that generate buildable and functional structures.

</details>


### [45] [Language-Conditioned Token and Reasoning Efficiency in Large Language Models: A Paired Cross-Lingual Study Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04295)
*Genliang Zhu,Chu Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A carefully controlled, pre-registered paired study design separates language-of-input, trace-language, and realization effects across many runs and conditions; methods and controls are rigorous but no confirmatory outcomes are yet released.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To disentangle costs attributable to input language, assigned observable trace language, and answer realization in large language models, preventing conflation present in prior comparisons.

Method: A prospective paired experimental design with 240 templated items in 8 languages, three open-weight checkpoints, three trace-token budgets, 22 input/trace-language conditions, fixed answer oracle and delimiter, 47,520 core runs prior to sample-size selection; intention-to-treat estimation with failure-inclusive terminal accounting; sealed-prefix cloning and runtime-native KV state for answer-realization testing; Holm-family multiple-comparison control and a secondary randomized policy experiment.

Result: No confirmatory model outcomes are reported; result fields are disabled pending independent verification of the frozen evidence ledger.

Conclusion: The protocol rigorously separates input-language, observable-trace, and answer-realization interfaces and is designed to attribute language-dependent representation and inference costs without conflating interfaces; no confirmatory results are released until independent verification.

Abstract: Large language models incur language-dependent representation and inference costs, but existing comparisons often conflate input language, assigned observable-trace language, and answer realization. We specify a prospective paired study that separates these interfaces while holding the semantic item, checkpoint, and answer oracle fixed. The initial design instantiates 240 exactly scored items rendered from templates in English and seven non-English languages, three distinct-lineage open-weight checkpoints, three trace-token budgets, 22 input- and trace-language conditions, a fixed answer reserve, and a separately counted delimiter: 47,520 initial core runs before prospective sample-size selection. RQ1-RQ3 estimate input and trace effects by intention-to-treat with failure-inclusive terminal accounting and test answer realization by cloning a sealed prefix and runtime-native KV state into eight crossed branches. Pre-freeze independent language review, fixed-form ASCII selectors, and code/surface/solver agreement constrain the realization test. H1-H5 share one Holm family and a global simultaneous component band. A secondary randomized experiment compares one-long-attempt and complete K-short-attempt policies at equal trace allowance under frozen seeds and oracle-blind aggregation; it is a full-policy contrast because answer capacity differs. Outcomes include exact token spans, correctness, latency, runtime-exposed memory, and qualified same-host operating-system-reported energy over prespecified hardware rails. The protocol separates tokenizer expansion, observable-trace cost, and answer-realization cost without treating visible traces as internal cognition or operating-system estimates as physical cross-device energy. No confirmatory model outcome is reported; result fields remain disabled until the frozen evidence ledger passes independent verification.

</details>


### [46] [EnvDreamer: Large-Scale Multimodal-to-Environment Generation for Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04301)
*Kabir Swain,Sijie Han,Antonio Torralba*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过LLM/VLM驱动的UE5自动化生成与验证流水线，EnvDreamer提供大规模、多样、可交互的仿真环境并释放20k场景数据集，使得无监督或弱监督策略在多任务具身基准上取得有竞争力的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希望把大规模数据与高容量模型带来的进步扩展到具身学习、世界模型和机器人领域，解决真实世界数据难采集、场景多样性与交互复杂性的瓶颈。

Method: 通过大语言模型/视觉语言模型生成场景描述与构建指令，基于Unreal Engine 5的程序化管线合成场景与物理交互要素；引入自动化校验器（validator）保证环境可交互与任务可达；导出任务程序、场景图、轨迹与元数据；支持图像条件的实景到仿真重构流程。

Result: 构建并展示大量生成场景和基线实验；训练的策略在导航、重排与操控等多个基准上表现竞争力；发布EnvDreamer-20k以支持可复现基准测试与后续研究。

Conclusion: EnvDreamer使用大语言模型与视觉语言模型驱动的流程，自动生成大量通过验证的Unreal Engine 5虚拟环境，从而为训练和评估具身智能与机器人策略提供可扩展的数据基础；在多个导航、重排与操控基准上，直接在EnvDreamer生成环境中训练的策略在无需显式建图或人工任务监督的情况下取得了具有竞争力的结果，并发布了包含20k验证通过场景的数据集EnvDreamer-20k。

Abstract: Large datasets and high capacity models have accelerated progress in vision and language. This work introduces a platform aimed at bringing comparable gains to embodied learning, world models, and robotics. We present EnvDreamer, a framework that uses large language and vision language models to generate Unreal Engine 5 environments for embodied AI and robot training. EnvDreamer enables sampling of large, diverse, interactive, customizable, and validator passed virtual environments for training and evaluation across navigation, interaction, and manipulation. We illustrate the platform with a large set of generated scenes and simple baselines. Policies trained on EnvDreamer generated environments, without explicit mapping or human task supervision, achieve competitive results on multiple embodied benchmarks spanning navigation, rearrangement, and manipulation. EnvDreamer also supports image-conditioned reconstruction for real-to-sim studies. Finally, we release EnvDreamer-20k, a dataset of 20,000 validator passed environments with task programs, scene graphs, trajectories, and metadata to support reproducible benchmarking.

</details>


### [47] [MOIRA: Mass-Oriented Indexing with Ragged Attention for Long-Context Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04313)
*Dich Nhat Minh Nguyen,Tran Dang Duong Nguyen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种无需训练的按头/层自适应稀疏解码（MOIRA）与新内核，在 128k 长上下文上极大减少页读取并将解码速度提升数倍，同时保持密集注意力精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文解码受限于内存带宽——每个输出 token 都需读取每层的 KV 缓存。KV 页需求在头、层和步之间高度不均衡。固定预算浪费，现有的自适应方案要么付出额外选择开销，要么需要训练；现有高性能内核假设行长接近，无法高效处理头/层间变长页表。

Method: 对每个请求、层、KV 头和时间步，使用覆盖率规则选出能覆盖至少 γ 注意力质量的最小页集合；引入一个新内核（self-planning attention），使每个线程块根据不同页表长度自我分配工作量，从而将整个稀疏解码步骤保持在 CUDA 图内并避免主机端计划或大量填充；在 vLLM 中实现并在 H200/128k 上与 FA3/FlashInfer 比较。

Result: 在 H200 的 RULER 128k 场景下，MOIRA (γ=0.99) 读取约 30% 的页，TPOT 比密集 FA3 提升 2.2–2.5× 且匹配密集精度；γ=0.98 时 TPOT 提升到 2.7× 并仍在噪声范围内；在高负载下吞吐量最高提升约 51%。结果表明按头/层自适应预算并配合在 CUDA 图内运行的内核能在灵活性与速度间取得良好折中。

Conclusion: MOIRA 实现了无需训练的、按 KV 头与层自适应的稀疏解码路径，通过基于注意力覆盖率 γ 的页选择规则与“self-planning attention” 内核，在 CUDA 图内高效执行变长页表的稀疏读取，从而在保持密集注意力精度的情况下显著降低内存带宽占用并加速解码。

Abstract: Long-context decoding is limited by memory bandwidth, because every output token reads the KV cache of every layer. Sparse decoding reduces this cost by reading only part of the KV cache. We observe that the number of pages a query needs varies widely across KV heads, layers and steps. Fixed budgets are simple, but they are sized for demanding cases and tuned per workload; adaptive budgets follow this variation more flexibly, but existing designs pay for it with extra selection cost or training. At the kernel level, FlashAttention-3 (FA3) and FlashInfer are designed for rows of similar length: with page lists whose length differs per KV head, they either pad the lists (forfeiting much of the sparse saving), leave thread blocks unbalanced, or rely on a host-side plan that runs outside the CUDA graph. We propose MOIRA, a training-free sparse decode path in vLLM whose budget adapts per KV head and per layer. For every request, layer, KV head and step, a coverage rule keeps the smallest set of pages whose estimated attention mass reaches a fraction $γ$. A new kernel, self-planning attention, lets each thread block derive its own share of the work from the list lengths, so the whole decode step stays inside the CUDA graph. On an H200, at RULER's 128k context, MOIRA with $γ=0.99$ matches dense accuracy while reading about 30% of the pages and reduces the time per output token (TPOT) by 2.2-2.5$\times$ relative to dense FA3; with $γ=0.98$ it reduces TPOT by 2.7$\times$ and stays within the noise of dense. Under high serving load it raises throughput by up to 51%. These results suggest that a budget adapted per head and layer, paired with a kernel that keeps such budgets inside the CUDA graph, makes sparse decoding both flexible and fast.

</details>


### [48] [From Latent Space to Jacobian Space: Measuring, Evading, and Training Against Safety-Content Accessibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04316)
*Mohammad Mosafer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Output-only safety monitoring sees only the end of a model's computation, yet the model computes its answer before emitting it: what it is internally poised to say is safety-critical. Jacobian-space (J-space) readouts, linear maps from hidden states to the output vocabulary via the model's input-output Jacobian, have been proposed as a window on behaviorally accessible internal content, and a first safety protocol (JADR) showed that danger recognition is readable there. What remains unknown is how this accessible content relates to the latent-space safety geometry studied by representation engineering, and how safety training shapes it. We introduce two quantitative bridges: (i) a transport-amplification profile $A_\ell$, measuring how strongly a latent safety direction is carried toward output space by each layer's Jacobian, and (ii) a paired base-vs-tuned protocol that attributes accessibility to training provenance. Across four small model pairs (135M to 1.5B, three lens-fit seeds), tuned checkpoints concentrate recognition in upper-middle layers, and at 0.5B DPO installs refusal while J-space recognition drops to chance: training can widen the accessibility gap exactly where behavioral safety looks best. A deployment audit closes the loop: monitor-aware GCG-style suffixes suppress the prompt-side monitor at zero behavioral cost at every scale; only a learned monitor rung resists its own adaptive re-attack; the training-time defense fails its re-attack at every penalty weight; steering shows the amplification profile is descriptive, not causal; and continuous-prefix optimization fails where discrete search succeeds. Safety monitoring, training, and evaluation must operate on accessibility itself, not on outputs alone.

</details>


### [49] [Bidirectional Preference Synthesis: Learning Prompt-Conditioned Preferences from Boundary Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04328)
*Junbo Wang,Lidong Lu,Zhuoqun Li,Guiping Jiang,Xiangyu Wu,Tinghai Zhang,Tong Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BPS augments DPO data with reverse pairs under synthesized achieved prompts for validated boundary failures, fixing missing prompt dependence and drastically improving achieved-side ranking while preserving original rankings, all without changing DPO or needing online sampling.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Correction-based offline preference pipelines only mark model failures as rejected under the original prompt, which fails to capture boundary failures where a response violates the instruction but coherently satisfies a nearby intent; this missing prompt dependence leads to incomplete supervision.

Method: For each validated boundary failure, retain the conventional forward preference pair (original prompt: response rejected) and synthesize an achieved prompt under which the same response is correct; add the reverse pair (achieved prompt: response preferred). Train with standard DPO on the expanded pair set.

Result: On Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, BPS preserves original-side pairwise ranking and improves achieved-side ranking accuracy from 6.8% to 62.3% on held-out crossed anchors. Similar gains observed under a Kimi-K2.6 cross-teacher probe. Blind human audit supports the reverse preference direction. Downstream evaluations show clearer separation from Forward-DPO in multilingual multi-turn instruction following and consistent capability retention (agentic, tool-use, code checks).

Conclusion: BPS makes prompt-dependence explicit by treating boundary failures bidirectionally: keep the original rejected pair and add a reverse accepted pair under a synthesized achieved prompt. This preserves forward ranking while substantially improving achieved-side ranking, enabling more complete offline DPO supervision without changing the DPO objective or requiring reward-model training or online sampling.

Abstract: Correction-based offline preference pipelines commonly treat model failures only as rejected responses under the original prompt. This supervision is incomplete for boundary failures: responses that violate the given instruction yet coherently satisfy a nearby intent or constraint setting. We introduce Bidirectional Preference Synthesis (BPS), a data-construction method for standard Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) that makes this missing prompt dependence explicit. For each validated boundary failure, BPS keeps the conventional forward pair under the original prompt and adds a reverse pair under a synthesized achieved prompt, so the same response is rejected where it is wrong and chosen where it is right, without changing the DPO objective, training a reward model, or requiring online sampling. On Qwen3-4B-Instruct-2507, BPS preserves original-side pairwise ranking while raising achieved-side ranking accuracy from 6.8% to 62.3% on held-out crossed anchors, with a similar shift under a Kimi-K2.6 cross-teacher probe. A blind human audit supports the intended reverse preference direction, and downstream evaluations show the clearest separation from Forward-DPO in multilingual multi-turn instruction following, with consistent capability-retention patterns on agentic, tool-use, and code checks.

</details>


### [50] [Suppressing Pressure, Amplifying Evidence: Self-Guided Attention Steering to Mitigate Sycophancy and Stubbornness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04329)
*Yinghao He,Mengyu Xu,Haixiang Sun,Donghan Li,Yibo Wang,Lixu Wang,Kezhen Chen,Chi Li,Chunwei Liu,Bharat Bhargava,Chongyang Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CoPE-Bench用于衡量sycophancy与contextual stubbornness间的权衡，SPAE通过基于模型自我判断的token级注意力引导，在无需训练的前提下同时抑制迎合用户的倾向并增强对冲突上下文的更新能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前语言模型可能在两个方向失败——过度迎合未经证实的用户主张或在有力上下文证据出现时拒绝更新答案。单独评估这两类失败会掩盖二者之间的权衡，因此需要一个联合基准和干预方法来衡量与调节该权衡。

Method: 引入CoPE-Bench（每题6种实验条件：中性基线、正确/错误的用户压力、一致/冲突的上下文信息以及把错误用户主张和冲突上下文结合的联合条件），以及无训练的SPAE方法：利用模型自我判断识别相关token，通过token级注意力引导（抑制用户压力相关token、放大上下文证据相关token）来调节模型对用户主张和上下文信息的依赖。

Result: 在五个backbone上，SPAE较最强基线平均将“服从用户压力”降低18.8个百分点，并将联合条件下的更新能力提高5.5个百分点；在两轮对话设置中，相比最强提示基线平均提升联合条件更新13.2个百分点；并公开了数据与代码。

Conclusion: 提出的SPAE框架在抑制模型对不支持性用户压力（sycophancy）和提高在冲突上下文中更新答案（避免contextual stubbornness）之间取得了有益的权衡：显著降低了被用户误导的概率，同时提高了在联合条件下根据上下文修正答案的能力。

Abstract: Reliable language models should resist unsupported user pressure while effectively using objective contextual information. However, models may exhibit sycophancy by yielding to unsupported user pressure or contextual stubbornness by failing to update their answers when relevant contextual information warrants revision. Evaluating interventions for these failures separately can obscure whether mitigating one failure exacerbates the other. To assess this trade-off, we introduce CoPE-Bench with six conditions per question: a neutral baseline, correct or incorrect user pressure, contextual information consistent with or conflicting with the neutral answer, and a joint condition combining incorrect claims with conflicting contextual information. To regulate the influence of user pressure and contextual information, we propose SPAE (Suppressing Pressure, Amplifying Evidence), a training-free framework that uses the model's own judgments to identify relevant tokens, suppressing user pressure and amplifying contextual information through token-level attention steering. On average across five backbones, SPAE reduces pressure following by 18.8 percentage points and increases joint-condition updating by 5.5 percentage points relative to the strongest baseline in the main comparison. In two-turn dialogue, it improves joint-condition updating by an average of 13.2 percentage points over the strongest prompting baseline. The source data and codes can be found at https://github.com/03Grant/sycophancy-and-stubbornness.

</details>


### [51] [ShadowMiner v1 - An Experience Report on Implementing and Measuring a Problem-and-Hypothesis Discovery Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04339)
*Jinhyuk Choi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ShadowMiner v1 是一个九阶段流水线系统，通过将论文构建为知识图谱、寻找图谱空白并以此引导 LLM 生成假设，随后对假设进行查重、评分与事实核验，报告侧重实现经验与模块贡献评估，而非提出新算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动化地从大量 AI 文献中识别未被覆盖的研究问题并生成可检验的假设，以提高研究发现的规模与系统性，减少人工筛选与洞察成本。

Method: 构建九阶段流水线：将文献结构化为知识图谱，检测知识图谱中的结构性空白（graph gaps）作为研究盲点，将这些空白嵌入 LLM 提示生成假设；对每条生成假设进行验证（查重确认是否已有研究覆盖、质量评分、基于来源的事实一致性检验）。

Result: 实现了一个端到端系统（ShadowMiner v1），展示了各模块的工程实现与组合效果，并通过消融/度量分析评估各个已采用技术对最终发现能力的贡献，但未提出新的生成或评估算法。

Conclusion: ShadowMiner v1 整体上是一个整合既有方法以自动发现研究问题并从 AI 论文生成假设的工程化系统；报告并无新颖生成或评估方法，而是呈现实现细节与实践经验，以及对各组成组件贡献的度量。

Abstract: ShadowMiner v1 is a system that automatically discovers research problems and generates hypotheses from AI papers. It is a nine-stage pipeline. It structures documents into a knowledge graph and finds graph gaps in it - structural blind spots in research. These graph gaps are included in the LLM generation prompt. Each generated hypothesis is then verified by checking whether it is already covered by existing research, scoring its quality, and checking that the facts it relies on are accurately drawn from its sources. This report does not propose a new generation or evaluation technique. It describes our experience of implementing and applying ideas from prior work, and measuring whether each one actually contributed.

</details>


### [52] [AgentPersonaBench: Benchmarking Persona-Driven User Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04379)
*Jintao Huang,Yifan Wang,Hongyu Shen,Yi Daniel Lu,Shirley Huang,Minsik Oh,Yewen Wang,Muhammad Ahmed Mohsin,Zhen Xu,Yilan Fan,Zichen Yuan,Ahsan Bilal,Zibu Wei,Sankalp Jajee,Henry Gagnier,Saksham Kapoor,Jicheng Wang,Qianfeng Wen,Yixuan He,Steven Dillmann,Jiashu He,Yucheng Lu,Linqiang Guo,Danyang Zhang,Shi Bo,Raunak Mondal,Haixiang Tang,Weihang Xiao,Allen Nie,Jing Tang,Yueying Li,Yifan Simon Liu,Jianheng Hou,Dianzhuo Wang,Qianyu Zhu,Zhixu Silvia Tao,Zhejian Peng,Zihan Wang,Ishan Gupta,Jinxuan Fan,Wanting Jiang,Shushu Liang,Chenxi Qiu,Yijun Wang,Xiaomin Li,Yuexing Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: APB fills a gap by operationalizing behavioral persona adherence across realistic modalities; results show strong in-surface adherence but clear generalization and multi-attribute limits, suggesting further work on ecological validity, robustness, and per-trait analyses.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing benchmarks focus on conversational style or self-report; APB aims to measure latent, action-level persona adherence in realistic interaction environments to assess fidelity of persona-driven user simulation.

Method: Embed single target traits covertly into full synthetic profiles and test for observable adherence across four interaction surfaces (survey, chat, web, app). Create 2,460 tasks covering 867 traits; verify ground truth via automated audits plus expert review; evaluate 20 model arms and report pass rates per surface and overall.

Result: Leading models reach up to 84.7% full-pass adherence unprompted, but cross-surface retention is lower (37.9–64.3% passing all four surfaces). Multi-attribute prompts reduce retention; different model families show pronounced divergence in behaviors.

Conclusion: APB is a well-designed benchmark that meaningfully measures whether persona conditioning produces observable behavioral changes; it shows persona steering is achievable but limited by modality, multi-attribute demands, and model-family differences.

Abstract: We introduce AgentPersonaBench (APB), a benchmark evaluating whether persona conditioning faithfully steers downstream agent behavior. While language models are increasingly deployed for persona-driven user simulation, existing benchmarks primarily evaluate conversational styling or self-reports rather than authentic behavioral fidelity. APB evaluates latent persona adherence one trait at a time, embedding each target trait within a complete synthetic profile without explicitly naming the trait or disclosing the test. Ground-truth adherence is verified strictly from observable actions across four interaction surfaces of increasing realism: survey, chat, web (interactive web environments), and app (desktop software environments). APB comprises 2,460 tasks spanning 867 traits, verified through automated audits and expert review. Our evaluation of 20 frontier model arms demonstrates that high-fidelity user simulation is already attainable: leading models achieve up to 84.7% full-pass adherence under unprompted conditions. At the same time, APB identifies clear behavioral boundaries: adherence drops across interaction modalities (only 37.9-64.3% pass all four surfaces), multi-attribute demands degrade retention, and competing model families exhibit pronounced behavioral divergence.

</details>


### [53] [TrustMed-RL: Long-Horizon Reinforcement Learning for Evidence-Grounded Clinical Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04387)
*Wenxin Zhan,Yizheng Jiao,Haifeng Song,Shuai Xu,Chencheng Pan,Jiayi Feng,Anjie Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出数据集与基于 RL 的证据获取策略（TrustMed‑RL），实现比传统微调和若干大模型更可靠的、证据可追溯的罕见病诊断性能，并获得临床医生的积极评价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前医学大模型可在证据不完整或推理不支持的情况下给出正确诊断，需构建能在长序列决策过程中主动获取证据并基于证据给出诊断的系统以提升可审查性与可靠性。

Method: 从 PubMed 罕见病病例构建数据集并手工标注 >24,000 张图像面板，使用状态依赖（state‑dependent）动作空间模拟问诊、查体、检测、专科会诊和文献检索；基于 8B 视觉—语言策略网络，训练中采用临床适配的 GiGPO 算法与覆盖率（coverage）调整的诊断奖励函数。

Result: 在 2,500 条评估病例上诊断准确率 37.1%，较监督微调提升 12.4 个百分点；当要求至少获取 50% 支持性检测证据时准确率为 32.5%，较 GPT‑4o 提升 6.8 个百分点；在 MTMedDialog 和 AgentClinic 上也超越被评估基线；对 200 条被接受的测试轨迹进行的医生复核显示 83.0% 的轨迹获得 4–5/5 的证据支撑评分。

Conclusion: TrustMed‑RL 在证据驱动的长程诊断任务上显著优于开放权重基线与监督微调，生成的诊断轨迹在临床评价中具有较高的证据支撑性与可审查性。

Abstract: Medical language models can produce correct diagnoses despite incomplete investigations and unsupported reasoning. To support long-horizon, evidence-grounded diagnosis, we introduce \textbf{TrustMed-RL}. Built from PubMed rare-disease cases and over 24,000 manually annotated image panels, it integrates interviews, examinations, testing, specialist consultation, and literature search through state-dependent actions. Our 8B vision--language policy, trained with clinically adapted GiGPO and coverage-adjusted diagnostic rewards, achieves 37.1\% diagnostic accuracy on 2,500 evaluation cases, outperforming all evaluated open-weight baselines and improving over supervised fine-tuning by 12.4 percentage points.. When success additionally requires acquiring at least 50\% of supporting test evidence, TrustMed-RL achieves 32.5\%, exceeding GPT-4o by 6.8 percentage points. Furthermore, it surpasses all evaluated baselines on MTMedDialog and multiple larger 27--32B models on AgentClinic. In physician review of 200 diagnostically accepted test-set trajectories, 83.0\% receive evidential-grounding scores of 4--5 out of 5. Physicians' assessments suggest that these diagnostic trajectories are trustworthy and aligned with human diagnostic reasoning.

</details>


### [54] [TimeNet: An Extensible Unified Data Infrastructure for Next-Generation Temporal Foundation Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04407)
*Martin Maritsch,Timo Stoffregen,Thomas Kaar,Behsad Riemer,Maxwell A. Xu,Max Rosenblattl,Juncheng Liu,Nicolas Zumarraga,Yu Yvonne Wu,Denys Herasymuk,Sparsh Rastogi,Hyungjun Yoon,Bosong Huang,Arvind Pillai,Dmytro Lopushanskyy,Tony Chen,Robin Deuber,Yichen Liu,Shvat Messica,Dan Li,Jian Lou,Yuwei Zhang,Jaeho Kim,Renée Rosillo Garcia,Fan Wu,Robert Müller,Elgar Fleisch,Flora D. Salim,Dimitris Spathis,Yuzhe Yang,Aaqib Saeed,Daniel McDuff,Ming Jin,Markus Kreft,Kevin O'Sullivan,Robert Jakob,Azul Garza,Paul Schmiedmayer,Patrick Langer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TimeNet: an open-source, extensible temporal data standard and infra that unifies heterogeneous time-series datasets into reusable views for diverse tasks, enabling scalable joint training and showing empirical improvements (+14% F1) while maintaining practical I/O performance.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Current development of Temporal Foundation Models is hindered by fragmented, task-specific data formats and pipelines. A unified representation is needed to scale TFMs across domains, datasets, and tasks.

Method: Design and implement an open-source data standard and scalable infrastructure that represents signals, metadata, annotations, and supervision in a common model; support multimodal signals and multiple time axes; express tasks as reusable views over recordings; transcode diverse datasets into the format; enable configurable joint training via existing TFN pipelines.

Result: Transcoded datasets with 1.5M task instances across domains/modalities; maintained practical I/O performance versus native formats; enabled joint training across heterogeneous datasets yielding a 14% F1 improvement over single-dataset training.

Conclusion: TimeNet provides a shared, extensible data model and infrastructure that decouples temporal data from task definitions, enabling heterogeneous time-series datasets to be combined and used for joint training of Temporal Foundation Models, improving downstream performance (e.g., +14% F1) while retaining practical I/O performance.

Abstract: Temporal Foundation Models (TFMs) aim to generalize across domains, datasets, and tasks. Yet, their development remains constrained by fragmented, task-specific data formats, annotations, and processing pipelines. We introduce TimeNet, an open-source data standard and scalable infrastructure that decouples temporal data from task definitions and represents signals, metadata, annotations, and supervision in a shared, extensible data model. TimeNet supports multimodal signals with regular, irregular, or ordinal time axes and expresses different task families (including classification, forecasting, temporal localization, question answering, generation, and editing) as reusable views over the same recordings. This shared representation enables heterogeneous time-series datasets to be combined for large-scale model training across domains, modalities, and tasks. We demonstrate TimeNet by transcoding datasets with 1.5M task instances spanning diverse domains, modalities, temporal scales, and forms of supervision, while retaining practical I/O performance relative to native formats. TimeNet enables an existing TFN training pipeline to support joint training on a configurable number of heterogeneous datasets through configuration changes alone. We show this capability by training TFM across multiple datasets and tasks, obtaining a 14% F1 score improvement compared with models trained on individual datasets. These results show that TimeNet provides the data and systems foundation needed to move beyond task- and dataset-specific TFMs toward models that can learn jointly across heterogeneous domains, modalities, temporal scales, and forms of supervision from a common data model.

</details>


### [55] [CORE-RL: Confidence-Oriented Reliability Evaluation of Black-Box Reinforcement Learning Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04418)
*Santhosh GS,Ananya Ravi,Devika Jay,Abhishek Sarkar,Perepu Satheesh Kumar,Saurav Prakash,Kaushik Dey,Balaraman Ravindran*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CORE-RL为黑盒RL策略提供了一套基于统一可靠性度量与有限样本统计界（Clopper–Pearson与Hoeffding）的评估管线，能在噪声与动力学漂移包络下输出高置信度的安全/性能证书与ODD，支持自动否决不合规策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在关键领域部署RL前需验证其多目标对齐性与对环境漂移的鲁棒性，但许多策略以黑盒形式交付以保护IP，无法使用基于内参的传统评估方法，因而需要面向黑盒策略的、具有统计保证的评估框架。

Method: 设计统一可靠性度量（将早期终止与安全违例形式化整合），构建感知噪声、执行噪声与环境动力学变化的噪声认证包络，对策略进行蒙特卡罗式评估并计算统一可靠性度量的Clopper–Pearson置信区间及奖励与安全代价的Hoeffding下界，基于这些统计界限定义并输出安全的ODD。

Result: 在连续控制任务上实证：CORE-RL能识别并拒绝不满足安全/性能阈值的策略，量化并绘制策略的安全ODD，并提供高置信度的性能与安全证书，展示了在黑盒设置下对策略可靠性与鲁棒性的可量化评估能力。

Conclusion: 提出了CORE-RL管线，能够对作为黑盒（本地可执行或远端API）交付的强化学习策略，在考虑早期任务终止与安全约束违例的统一可靠性度量下，给出基于有限样本的高置信度证书并报告安全运行设计域（ODD），从而自动否决不合规策略并为部署提供统计依据。

Abstract: The deployment of Reinforcement Learning (RL) agents in critical domains must be preceded with a pipeline to evaluate the alignment of the RL agent with complex multi-objective specifications and robustness under real-world environmental drift. However, to protect intellectual property, the RL agent may be delivered for evaluation as opaque executable or remote API, which makes traditional evaluation techniques based on the internals of the policies infeasible. To address this gap, CORE-RL: Confidence-Oriented Reliability Evaluation of black box RL policy is proposed in this paper. The CORE-RL pipeline introduces a Unified Reliability Metric that formally integrates early task termination and safety constraint violations, preventing unsafe policies from masking failures through premature episode halts. By subjecting the policy to a noise certification envelope of perceptual noise, actuation noise and change in environment dynamics, the pipeline computes the finite-sample Clopper-Pearson bounds on unified reliability metric and Hoeffdings' lower bound on reward and safety cost. The pipeline then defines safe operational design domain to report high-confidence certificates for safety and expected performance. Experiments on continuous control tasks demonstrate the CORE-RL pipeline's ability to automatically reject non-compliant policies and map the safe Operational Design Domain (ODD) of safety-aware policies. Thus CORE-RL provides an evaluation framework towards a quantitative, transparent and reproducible, statistical rationale necessary to safely evaluate, compare, and deploy black box RL solutions.

</details>


### [56] [Asking Earns Nothing: Scoring the Decision to Act in BFCL Multi-Turn](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04429)
*Yangze Liu,Zhongyi Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文发现BFCL多回合基准在‘应当询问’情形下鼓励行动而非询问，官方分数与回合正确决策不一致。通过成对样本和回合级判分器作者揭示并量化了该偏差，展示提示能移动官方分数却不改善实际决策，并公开数据与工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证并暴露BFCL多回合基准在涉及是否应当询问这一决策上的评分设计缺陷，以避免排行榜因鼓励行动而误导模型行为和排名，推动更合理的评估机制。

Method: 构造“应当询问”对照样本：从基本样本派生出两个版本（信息完整与信息缺失）使得在同一回合模型在完整版应当执行世界改变调用，在缺失版应当询问；不使用LLM裁判，直接读取存储轨迹并判断模型是否在该回合尝试了世界改变调用；统计模型在两侧的行为以计算‘决策准确率’，并与官方评分比较；进一步通过提示（告诉模型要询问/要行动）观察对回合决策与官方分数的影响；公开数据和工具。

Result: 关键结果包括：在223对子样本上，gpt-5.4在完整回合上83.4%尝试调用、在缺失回合上78.0%保持不调用，决策准确率80.7%（在七个模型中最好）；但官方评分把gpt-5.4排第六；对模型下达不同提示能显著改变官方分数（例如向gpt-5.4加一行‘不要询问’使其官方分数在相关类别上上升13.5至23.5分），而回合决策准确率无显著变化。作者还发布了回合级判分工具、样本对和31个经人工核验的坏题。

Conclusion: 本文指出当前BFCL多回合基准在‘应当询问（should-ask）’类型题目上存在评分偏差：评分系统本身忽视了模型应当在特定回合询问的正确性，反而以促成世界改变（action）为优先，从而鼓励模型盲动而非询问。作者通过构造成对的基线/缺失信息样本（一对在同一回合索要相同信息，一侧信息完整应当行动，另一侧信息缺失应当询问），设计了回合级判分器直接检查模型是否尝试执行世界改变调用，发现官方分数与该回合判定的相关性差异显著。按回合判分，gpt-5.4在223对样本上达80.7%决策准确率，但在官方排行榜中仅列第六；对模型的提示语（促使行动/促使询问）能大幅改变官方分数而不显著改变回合决策准确率。作者发布了这些样本、可在任意BFCL输出目录上运行的回合级判分器和31个经人工核验的坏题。

Abstract: An agent that lacks the information it needs should ask rather than act, and the task definitions of agent leaderboards say so. BFCL multi-turn builds two of its four categories around a turn on which the model is supposed to ask, and its scorer never looks at that turn: the gold trajectory there is empty, the checker skips it, and the scripted user cannot answer, so asking earns nothing, guessing costs nothing on that turn, and asking twice loses the item. The benchmark also contains the control experiment for that decision. A should-ask item is a base item with one piece of information removed from one turn, so the same request appears twice at the same turn index, once complete and once not: on the first the model should make the call that changes the world, on the second it should ask. We score one decision per pair, whether the model attempted a world-changing call on that turn, read off the stored trajectories with no LLM judge; acting always and asking always both score 50. On the 223 pairs that pose this decision, gpt-5.4 attempts the call on 83.4% of the complete turns and holds back on 78.0% of the incomplete ones, the best decision accuracy of seven models at 80.7%; on the same items the official score ranks it sixth and puts first a model that lands in the middle here. One added line telling gpt-5.4 not to ask pushes it toward acting on both sides of the pair, so its decision accuracy shows no detectable change, while its official score rises by 13.5 to 23.5 points on the two should-ask categories and on the base twins; the opposite line, telling gemma-4-31B-it to ask first, improves its decision by 4.5 points and gains no official score. The score moves with the push toward action, not with the decision. We release the pairs, a turn-level scorer that runs on any BFCL output directory without an API key, and 31 manually verified bad items.

</details>


### [57] [MERCI Cards: An LLM Evaluation and Deployment Framework for High-Stakes Domains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04430)
*Aparna Komarla,Annalisa Szymanski*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A mathematical framework and MERCI Cards enable iterative, multi-objective evaluation and deployment of LLMs in high-stakes domains, demonstrated in criminal justice to improve monitoring and targeted improvements.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As LLMs are adopted in high-risk, resource-constrained professional domains, practitioners need principled protocols to systematically track, monitor, and improve model performance through iterative deployment.

Method: Formalizes LLM integration as a weighted multi-objective optimization over model selection, rubric design, evaluation, and deployment; demonstrates framework application through case study in criminal justice, and uses MERCI Cards to structure tracking and monitoring across cycles.

Result: Shows that the framework and MERCI Cards can identify under-performing areas, direct developer and user attention to validation and error-correction, and guide system improvements across iterations in a criminal-justice case study.

Conclusion: The paper introduces a formal mathematical framework for iterative evaluation and deployment of LLMs in high-stakes workflows and proposes MERCI Cards as a practical artifact to guide improvements across deployment iterations.

Abstract: As LLMs are increasingly deployed in high-stakes professional workflows, engineers and researchers require principled protocols to systematically track, monitor, and improve model performance across deployment cycles. We present a mathematical framework for iterative LLM evaluation and deployment, and demonstrate its application to AI systems used in criminal justice. Our framework formalizes LLM integration in high-stakes, high-risk, and resource-constrained domains across model selection, rubric design, evaluations and deployment via a weighted multi-objective optimization. We demonstrate that MERCI Cards can guide improvements of the system across deployment iterations, direct developer attention toward under-performing areas, and focus user attention on validation and error-correction in the LLM's outputs.

</details>


### [58] [CRAFT: An Agentic Spreadsheet Form Filling System with Template Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04437)
*Leyao Gu,Yingjie Xiong,Zirui Tang,Jiangtao Zhou,Yeye He,Chunwei Liu,Xuanhe Zhou,Fan Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CRAFT是一个模板感知的表格填充代理，结合反思与受限局部修复（由RASG定位槽位、标签-槽提示与保护区域约束）来恢复并重写被破坏的表单模板，在新基准上显著提升了准确率并已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 表格表单填充需要整合外部证据、将值精确定位到单元格并保留不规则模板结构；早期编辑错误会覆盖标签或错位字段，从而破坏后续决策，需一种能反思并局部修复的机制。

Method: 提出模板感知代理框架CRAFT；使用Rectangle-Aware Slot Grounder (RASG)来候选可写单元格；将检测到的错误绑定到表格区域并在必要时恢复模板；用标签-槽提示和受保护区域约束后续编辑；在流水线中集成结构判定和槽重定位。

Result: 在新建的FormFillBench（327个表单）的两个赛道上，CRAFT比最强基线分别将pair accuracy提升了8.51和23.38个百分点；消融实验支持结构裁决与槽重定位的设计，且在第二个backbone上保留相对优势；代码和基准已开源。

Conclusion: CRAFT通过将反思式验证与受限的局部修复相结合，基于模板结构将检测到的错误精确地定位到电子表格区域，恢复被破坏的模板状态并重新定位可写槽，从而显著提高表单填充任务的准确性。

Abstract: Spreadsheet form filling requires agents to consolidate external evidence, ground values to precise cells, and preserve irregular template structure. Errors in early edits can overwrite labels or misalign fields, undermining later decisions. We propose CRAFT, a template-aware agent framework that connects reflective validation to constrained local repair. Instead of treating reflection as a free-form request to regenerate the workbook, CRAFT grounds detected errors to spreadsheet regions, restores corrupted template state when necessary, and re-grounds plausible writable slots before subsequent edits. A Rectangle-Aware Slot Grounder (RASG) proposes writable cells, while label-slot hints and protected regions constrain subsequent edits. We introduce FormFillBench, with 327 forms across Instruction-Only and Multi-File tracks. Compared with the strongest baselines, CRAFT improves pair accuracy by 8.51 and 23.38 percentage points on these tracks, respectively. Component-removal experiments support structural adjudication and slot re-grounding within the pipeline, and the framework retains its relative advantage among the methods evaluated with a second backbone. The code and benchmark FormFillBench are available at https://github.com/Glllllly/CRAFT.

</details>


### [59] [RAGrasp: Geometry-Semantic Template Retrieval and Grasp Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04438)
*Shenzhe Zhu,Chengxiao He,Jan Harder*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGrasp is a retrieval-based grasp pipeline that leverages locally collected RGB-D templates, DINOv2 features, SAM2 segmentation, and a retrieval+refinement cascade to transfer and refine grasps without retraining, achieving near-perfect real-world success in the reported trials.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Avoid heavy task-specific retraining and domain gap issues of large public/synthetic datasets by exploiting a compact set of locally collected, embodiment- and sensor-aligned examples for fast, deployment-ready grasping.

Method: Build a template memory from local RGB-D observations; extract self-supervised DINOv2 features and combine appearance/depth cues to prompt SAM2 for object isolation; apply a two-stage geometry–semantic retrieval cascade to select templates; route through a confidence gate to one of two grasp-transfer estimators; refine transferred grasps using mask-support and silhouette-contact constraints; finally convert refined 2D grasps to 3D via calibration.

Result: In real-world trials RAGrasp achieved 20/20 successful grasps on seen objects and 19/20 on unseen objects. The system showed effective adaptation from limited local annotations and tolerance to tested viewpoint and lighting variations.

Conclusion: RAGrasp demonstrates that retrieval-augmented grasping from a small, locally collected, grasp-annotated RGB-D template memory enables deployment-specific adaptation without end-to-end retraining, achieving high real-world grasp success and robustness to modest viewpoint and illumination changes.

Abstract: We present RAGrasp, a retrieval-augmented pipeline for planar parallel-jaw grasping from a compact set of locally collected, grasp-annotated RGB-D (color and depth) templates. Unlike task-specific predictors trained primarily on large public or synthetic grasp datasets, RAGrasp requires no end-to-end retraining for a new deployment.Its template memory is constructed from observations collected with the deployment camera, robot, and gripper in the target workspace, thereby aligning stored examples with the local sensing and embodiment conditions. The system uses self-supervised DINOv2 visual fea- tures together with appearance and depth cues to prompt the Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2), which isolates the query object. A two-stage geometry-semantic retrieval cascade then selects a template, and a confidence gate chooses one of two grasp- transfer estimators. The transferred grasp is refined using mask- support and silhouette-contact constraints before calibrated 2D- to-3D conversion. In real-world trials, RAGrasp achieves 20/20 successful grasps on seen objects and 19/20 on unseen objects. Within the evaluated setting, the results demonstrate deployment- specific grasp adaptation from limited local annotation and tolerance to the tested viewpoint and illumination changes.

</details>


### [60] [Causally Fair Generation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04444)
*Patrik Okanovic,Torsten Hoefler,Drago Plecko*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CFG是一个在因果图与参考人群上对LLM生成进行因果干预以消除不正当人口差异的框架，具有形式保证并在多模型多场景下进行了实证评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 因LLM生成会影响重要决策，且常涉及多个相互因果关联的变量，纯统计方法难以将观测到的差异归因于生成机制；因此需要基于因果推断的方法来恰当评估与缓解人口子群间的不平等。

Method: CFG通过三步：提取相关概念、将生成过程在参考人群与因果图上进行定植（adaptation），以及对用户选择的因果效应进行干预/移除；支持保留对任务有正当性（business necessity）的路径；在理论上证明在适当因果假设下可完全消除不正当的因果效应。

Result: 作者在三个真实场景与一个带已知因果真值的合成数据集上，用四种LLM验证CFG，展示其在保留有正当路径同时消除不正当因果效应方面的有效性与可行性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CFG方法为在LLM生成中识别并移除由因果路径导致的人群差异提供了可操作框架，并在满足因果可辨识假设时给出形式化的消除歧视效应的保证。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate, complete, and transform information in settings where their outputs can shape consequential decisions, raising concerns about their impact on demographic disparities. In this context, causal inference provides a principled basis for assessing fairness, because it attributes observed disparities to the mechanisms that generated them, which a purely statistical approach cannot do even with infinite data. In LLM generation, a query may request several causally related variables, each of which is both an outcome of interest and a possible cause of other outputs, and the information supplied in the prompt need not follow a topological or a temporal order. This calls for methods that can analyze and selectively remove disparities from such a flexible generation process. In this paper we introduce Causally Fair Generation with LLMs (CFG, for short). CFG extracts relevant concepts, grounds generation in a reference population and causal diagram, and removes user-selected causal effects. CFG also allows pathways deemed justifiable for the task's utility to be retained, which is known in legal literature as business necessity. Further, we provide formal guarantees for our method when eliminating all discriminatory causal effects in the adapted population model, under appropriate causal assumptions. We evaluate CFG with four LLMs in three real-world settings based on population data and on a synthetic dataset with a known causal ground truth.

</details>


### [61] [Understanding Generative AI Use in Programming MOOCs: The Role of Course Context and Learner Characteristics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04447)
*Marina Lepp*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在两门编程MOOC中，GenAI被广泛采用，课程情境（时长/作业量/复杂度）比学习者背景更能影响使用方式；但自报数据与横断面设计限制了关于对学习成效影响的明确结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着ChatGPT等GenAI工具普及，研究者关心学习者如何在开放、异质的MOOC环境中整合这些工具，以及这对教学设计和学习效果的潜在影响。

Method: 对两门用爱沙尼亚语授课的编程MOOC（About Programming: 4周，n=187；Introduction to Programming: 8周，n=182）在课后问卷中收集自报的GenAI采用、使用频率与目的数据，采用非参数统计方法比较不同课程与不同学习者背景间的差异，并做探索性与学习相关结果的关联分析。

Result: 发现GenAI在两门课程中被广泛采用，性别、年龄、教育水平或编程经验与采用率无显著差异；但较长且任务更多的课程里，使用频率显著更高，且更倾向于用于调试和想法生成；对代码解释和调试的使用频率也显著更高；与学习成果的关系在探索性分析中较弱或有限。

Conclusion: 课程情境比学习者个体特征更能解释学习者如何使用生成式人工智能（GenAI）；在两门编程MOOC中，GenAI采用普遍，但学习时长更长、作业量更大的课程中使用频率更高，且更常用于调试与创意生成；与学习成果的关联性有限。

Abstract: The increasing availability of generative artificial intelligence (GenAI) tools, such as ChatGPT and code-completion assistants, raises questions about how learners integrate these tools into learning activities, particularly in MOOCs that attract diverse participant populations. This study examines the use of GenAI in two programming MOOCs taught in Estonian that differ in duration, workload, topic complexity, and assignment volume: About Programming (4 weeks, 26 expected hours, n = 187) and Introduction to Programming (8 weeks, 78 expected hours, n = 182). Post-course questionnaire data were analyzed using non-parametric statistical methods to examine self-reported adoption, usage frequency, and purposes of GenAI use across courses and learner backgrounds. The results show that GenAI adoption was widespread in both MOOCs, with no statistically significant differences by gender, age, education level, or prior programming experience. However, participants in the longer, more extensive MOOC reported significantly higher usage frequency and were more likely to use GenAI for debugging and idea generation. Reported usage frequency for code explanation and debugging was also higher in the longer course. Exploratory analyses found limited relationships between GenAI use and learning-related outcomes. The findings suggest that course context may play a greater role than learner characteristics in shaping how GenAI tools are used. These results provide implications for instructional design and guidance in programming education.

</details>


### [62] [Trinity: Self-Evolving Vision-Language Models with a Self-Verifier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04469)
*Youngwan Lee,Yong-Ju Lee,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Trinity leverages an EMA self-verifier within a single VLM that queries, solves, and verifies to create higher-quality self-supervision from images alone, substantially improving visual scientific reasoning benchmarks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing self-evolving VLMs reward agreement among sampled answers, which is a weak proxy for truth and fails to detect ungrounded or systematically wrong consensus. A self-verifier can provide a stronger, label-free check for question grounding and answer correctness.

Method: Introduce Trinity: one VLM plays three roles. Questioner proposes image-grounded questions; Solver answers them; Verifier is an exponential moving average (EMA) of the policy (self-verifier) that screens for grounding and correctness, scores reasoning, adjudicates conflicts between reference answers and solver consensus, corrects references and penalizes Questioner when consensus is right. Training uses only images.

Result: Trinity trained on unlabeled images improves Qwen3-VL-8B notably on scientific visual reasoning: +8.6 points on SciVQR biology split and +12.8 on MathVerse. Reward dynamics reflect a healthy self-play curriculum.

Conclusion: Trinity shows that a single VLM acting as Questioner, Solver, and an EMA-based self-Verifier can filter and correct its own generated supervision from unlabeled images, yielding large gains on visual scientific reasoning benchmarks without external labels.

Abstract: Self-evolving vision-language models (VLMs), a form of self-improvement in which a model generates its own training data from unlabeled images, are a promising route toward agents that expand their reasoning capability in an unsupervised manner, without relying on ever-larger annotation budgets. Existing methods pair a Questioner that proposes problems with a Solver that answers them, but reward both roles mainly by agreement among sampled answers. Agreement is a weak proxy for truth: it cannot tell whether a question is grounded in the image, whether the proposed reference answer is right, or whether a confident majority is wrong in the same way. We present Trinity, in which one VLM plays three roles, Questioner, Solver, and Verifier, and the Verifier is a self-verifier: an exponential moving average (EMA) of the policy itself, requiring neither labels nor an external judge. The Verifier screens every generated question for image grounding and answer correctness before it becomes supervision, scores Solver reasoning against the image, and adjudicates disputes between the reference answer and a strong Solver consensus, correcting the reference and penalizing the Questioner when the consensus is right. Trained on images alone, Trinity improves Qwen3-VL-8B on mathematical visual reasoning and on science benchmarks with biology content, for example, +8.6 points on the biology split of SciVQR and +12.8 on MathVerse, and its reward dynamics behave as a healthy self-play curriculum should. These results suggest that a self-evolving multimodal agent can strengthen its scientific reasoning from unlabeled scientific images alone, with the model itself serving as the verifier.

</details>


### [63] [Reactivating Alignment: Defending LLMs from Jailbreaks via Intention-Aware Input-Output Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04470)
*Luoyu Chen,Weiqi Wang,Chenhan Zhang,Zhiyi Tian,Shui Yu*

Main category: cs.AI
