TL;DR: SENTINEL extracts latent attacker intent from semantically aligned input–output regions during generation and uses projection-based scoring to stop harmful continuations—effective, low-over-refusal, and robust across tests.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing defenses either perturb inputs or suppress outputs without explicitly modelling where malicious intent is hidden, causing brittle defenses and excessive over-refusal; reframing mitigation as intent extraction promises more precise, generation-aware intervention.

Method: A plug-and-play, generation-time pipeline that (1) leverages input–output semantic consistency of instruction-tuned LLMs to find matched subsequences between prompt and generated text, (2) extracts these intention-revealing subsequences, (3) scores their harmfulness via refusal-direction projection vectors, and (4) halts generation when scores exceed thresholds.

Result: On HarmBench across multiple LLMs, SENTINEL reduces jailbreak success to about 5% while maintaining low over-refusal, remains robust under adaptive attacks, and analysis suggests it redistributes jailbreak features from alignment blind spots into aligned regions enabling detection.

Conclusion: SENTINEL effectively detects and blocks jailbreak attacks at generation time by extracting attacker intent from semantically aligned input–output regions, achieving low jailbreak success rates (~5%) while keeping over-refusal low and showing robustness to adaptive attacks; mechanistically it shifts jailbreak cues into aligned regions where they can be scored.

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) remain vulnerable to jailbreak attacks that conceal harmful intent within complex adversarial prompts. Existing defenses primarily rely on input perturbation or harmful-output suppression, but they rarely model where malicious intent resides, resulting in brittle protection and excessive over-refusal.
  We propose SENTINEL, a plug-and-play, generation-time jailbreak defense that reframes mitigation as an intent extraction problem. Our key insight is that instruction-tuned LLMs exhibit strong input--output semantic consistency: regardless of jailbreak complexity, generated outputs tend to align with the attacker's true intent. SENTINEL exploits this property by matching semantically aligned input--output regions to extract intention-revealing subsequences, scores these subsequences using refusal-direction projections to estimate harmfulness, and halts generation when necessary. Experiments on HarmBench across multiple LLMs show that SENTINEL reduces jailbreak success rates to close to 5\% while maintaining low over-refusal. We further demonstrate robustness to adaptive attacks and provide a mechanistic interpretation: SENTINEL re-distributes jailbreak features from alignment blind spots to aligned regions.

</details>


### [64] [Semantic Causal-Factor Inference from Aviation Incident Narratives Using A Variational Autoencoder with Cosine-Similarity-Based Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04472)
*AZIIDA NANYONGA1,HASSAN WASSWA,UGUR TURHAN,KEITH FRANCIS JOINER,GRAHAM WILD*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用VAE把调查文本映射到概率潜在空间并重构可能原因表示，在大规模NTSB数据上取得了较高的语义相似度，展示了辅助因果提取的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动从长且复杂的事故调查叙述中提取散布式的因果证据，以辅助事故分析并减轻人工标注负担。

Method: 将调查叙述与专家报告的可能原因转换为数值表示，使用编码器映射叙述到概率潜在空间，解码器估计对应的可能原因表示。训练目标为KL散度与基于余弦相似度的语义重构损失的组合。使用2005–2020年20,919份NTSB报告评估。

Result: 在保留测试集上，预测与专家报告的可能原因表示平均余弦相似度为0.786（SD=0.120）；预测表示还产出了解释性词项，表明模型能捕获与因果信息相关的语义特征。

Conclusion: 提出的VAE框架能把调查叙述编码到概率潜在空间并重构出与专家报告相符的可能原因表示，测试集上表示间平均余弦相似度0.786，结果显示该方法在从航空事故调查叙述中提取因果信息方面具有潜力，同时保留专家判断作为正式因果确定的依据。

Abstract: Aviation accident and incident investigations generate extensive unstructured textual information containing evidence relevant to the causes and contributing factors of safety occurrences. Automatically extracting such information is challenging because causal evidence may be distributed across long and complex investigation narratives. This study proposes a semantic causal-factor inference framework combining natural language processing with a variational autoencoder (VAE) to learn the relationship between aviation investigation narratives and expert-reported probable causes. Investigation narratives and their corresponding probable causes are transformed into numerical representations, after which the encoder maps narrative representations to a probabilistic latent space. The decoder estimates representations of the corresponding probable causes and is trained using an objective that combines Kullback-Leibler divergence with cosine-similarity-based semantic reconstruction. The framework was evaluated using 20,919 finalized U.S. National Transportation Safety Board investigation reports from 2005 to 2020. On the held-out test set, the predicted and expert-reported probable-cause representations achieved a mean cosine similarity of 0.786 (SD = 0.120). The predicted representations also yielded interpretable terms associated with causal information in the reports. The results demonstrate the potential of probabilistic latent representation learning for AI-assisted extraction of causal information from aviation safety narratives while retaining expert investigation as the basis for formal causal determination.

</details>


### [65] [InferOpt: Constrained Multi-Objective Search for LLM Inference Configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04473)
*Qi Chen,Yingying Cheng,Zhaoyi Sun,Li Zhou,Fan Zhang,Jie Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将推理时的层级配置视为受约束多目标黑盒优化，提出可复用的代理先行+预算截除+重验证流程（InferOpt），在KV与MoE的高维逐层空间上显著压缩资源占用且保持模型质量，优于多种基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实际部署大型语言模型需设置大量层级推理参数（如各层KV保留、每层expert数），现有机制多为机制相关的单点启发式，不能扩展到逐层高维搜索空间，且难以在资源-质量间自动权衡。

Method: 设计并实现InferOpt：接受变量边界、确定性资源计费函数和评价hook；在代理数据集上搜索并在调用模型前剔除超预算候选；自适应收紧预算并在最终将Pareto代表在全量数据上重验证。实验覆盖连续的KV（28维）与离散的MoE（26维）层级搜索空间，并与Random、NSGA-II、MOTPE比较。

Result: KV空间（Qwen2.5-7B）：通过post-prefill剪枝将16K缓存削减64.4%，TPOT减少22.9–48.5%；InferOpt搜索的逐层预算相较于同预算的均匀策略在接近Full KV参考点时分别提升7.3%和14.0%。MoE空间（DeepSeek-V2-Lite）：InferOpt搜索得到的top-k调度去除了43.0%的路由token–expert对，损耗仅0.59个score点，优于匹配预算的基线。总体上，在代理超体积（proxy hypervolume）与资源保留率上，InferOpt均领先于Random、NSGA-II和MOTPE。

Conclusion: InferOpt将推理配置问题重塑为受约束的多目标黑盒优化，并提供了一套实用的搜索流水线：使用冻结的采样代理集、预算前置截除、预算自适应收紧及在全量集上重验证，能够在高维层级搜索空间中找到更优的资源/性能折衷，显著优于常用启发式及若干基线优化算法。

Abstract: Serving an LLM means setting dozens of inference-time knobs, from per-layer KV retention to per-layer expert counts. Practice sets them with mechanism-specific heuristics that return a single operating point and do not scale to layer-wise search spaces. We recast inference configuration as constrained multi-objective black-box optimization and build InferOpt, a reusable search framework that requires only variable bounds, a deterministic resource cost, and an evaluation hook. InferOpt searches on a frozen sampled proxy set, rejects over-budget candidates before any model call, tightens the budget adaptively, and re-validates Pareto representatives on full-scale dataset. One pipeline covers a 28-dimensional continuous KV space (Qwen2.5-7B) and a 26-dimensional discrete MoE space (DeepSeek-V2-Lite). On KV, post-prefill pruning cuts the 16K cache by 64.4% and TPOT by 22.9--48.5%, and the searched layer-wise budget by InferOpt beats a matched uniform budget by 7.3% and 14.0% of the Full KV reference points. On MoE, a searched top-k schedule by InferOpt removes 43.0% of routed token--expert pairs while staying within 0.59 points of the default, closer than the matched-budget baselines. Against Random Search, NSGA-II, and MOTPE, InferOpt leads on both spaces, taking the best proxy hypervolume and the lowest retention on KV and staying closest to the uncompressed reference at the lowest experts on MoE.

</details>


### [66] [VCLMU: Mechanism-Centric Virtual Cell World Modeling for Perturbation Response](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04475)
*Yuwei Miao,Azim Dehghani Amirabad,Scott Oloff,Junzhou Huang,Tianyu Cui,Rui Liao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: VCLMU提出将细胞状态拆解为具有可复用身份与观测性状态的LMU，并把扰动当作作用于这些LMU的动作来诱发机制级随机转移；两阶段预训练和实证评估显示该方法在预测扰动特异性响应和机制可解释性上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模型通常直接从未扰动的分子谱和扰动映射到结果观测，缺乏显式的、可解释的潜在机制层面转变表示；作者希望通过机制级潜在状态和基于动作的转移来提高扰动反应的可预测性与可解释性。

Method: 设计LMU：每个LMU包含基于多模态生物证据的可复用身份（gene set/机制指示）和与观测相关的状态向量。将扰动编码为作用于LMU的动作，允许机制特异的随机状态转移，然后解码为转录响应。采用两阶段预训练：先在≈200K伪总体（pseudo-bulk）扰动谱上预训练，再在基因对齐的单细胞扰动数据上精炼。

Result: 在六个扰动不重叠的基准上，VCLMU在扰动特异性响应恢复上持续优于强基线，同时在全局响应精度上保持竞争力。通过LMU基因集合与扰动响应的富集分析，LMU被证明捕获了结构化的生物响应程序。

Conclusion: 提出的VCLMU将细胞状态表示为一组潜在机制单元（LMUs），并将遗传扰动视为对这些潜在单元的作用，从而在机制层面建模扰动引起的状态转移，有助于更好地预测并解释细胞对干预的转录响应。

Abstract: Predicting cellular responses to genetic perturbations is a central capability for virtual cells and a key step toward computational modeling of biological interventions. Most existing models directly map an unperturbed molecular profile and perturba- tion to the resulting observation without explicitly representing the latent cellular transition induced by the intervention. We introduce a mechanism-centric virtual cell world model that represents cellular state as a set of Latent Mechanism Units (LMUs) and treats genetic perturbations as actions on these latent states. Each LMU combines a reusable identity grounded in multimodal biological evidence with an observation-specific state, allowing a perturbation to induce mechanism- specific stochastic transitions before decoding the resulting transcriptional response. We train VCLMU through two-stage pretraining, first on around 200K pseudo-bulk perturbation profiles and then on gene-aligned single-cell perturbation data. Across six perturbation-disjoint benchmarks, VCLMU consistently improves perturbation- specific response recovery over strong baselines while maintaining competitive global response accuracy. We further analyze learned LMUs through enrichment between perturbation responses and LMU gene sets and show that they capture structured biological response programs. These results support mechanism-level latent state transition as a useful formulation for virtual cell models that aim to predict and interpret cellular responses to biological interventions.

</details>


### [67] [ManifoldCache: Training-Free Diffusion Acceleration via Constraint Manifold Caching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04510)
*Prashant Pandey,Devineni Sri Venkatraya Chowdary,Brejesh Lall*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ManifoldCache: a proof-backed, training-free caching scheme for diffusion models on constraint manifolds; cache only before the noise midpoint to get safe, efficient inference and larger certified caching strides with deeper networks.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Inference for diffusion models constrained by hard geometric/physical constraints is computationally expensive (hundreds-to-thousands of neural evaluations). Existing acceleration techniques fail on CMDMs. The motivation is to design a principled accelerator that preserves constraint fidelity and mode coverage while drastically reducing inference cost.

Method: They formalize CMDMs where the target distribution lies on a constraint-defined manifold, derive an orthogonal decomposition of the conditional score into normal and tangential components, and use this to prove that the noise-schedule midpoint is a sharp safe-caching boundary. They further analyze how the decomposition propagates through network block Jacobians to certify cache strides, and propose a zero-training, zero-data cache schedule (ManifoldCache) that exploits these proofs.

Result: A theoretically grounded, training-free accelerator that (i) identifies a provably safe phase for caching based on the noise midpoint, (ii) provides certified cache stride bounds that grow with network block depth, and (iii) requires no calibration data or extra training. The paper unifies eight prior constrained-generation tasks under the CMDM abstraction and demonstrates the method’s applicability across them (per abstract).

Conclusion: Paper concludes that a principled, training-free caching strategy (ManifoldCache) based on an orthogonal decomposition of the conditional score lets one safely accelerate inference in Constraint-Manifold Diffusion Models (CMDMs). Caching before the noise-schedule midpoint yields provably bounded error while caching after it causes irrecoverable mode-confusion for a positive fraction of trajectories; deeper network blocks permit larger certified cache strides.

Abstract: Diffusion models for structured scientific generation must produce samples satisfying hard geometric constraints imposed by physics, chemistry, or biology, yet inference in these settings is prohibitively slow, demanding hundreds to thousands of neural-function evaluations per sample. We unify eight state-of-the-art models spanning medical volumetrics, molecular conformations, protein backbone design, crystal structure prediction, and multi-view 3D scenes under a single abstraction, Constraint-Manifold Diffusion Models (CMDMs), in which the target distribution is supported on a manifold defined by an externally specified constraint map. All existing acceleration families fail on this class: quantization exhausts memory on high-dimensional volumetric operators; pruning breaks constraint fidelity; fast ODE solvers allow trajectories to drift off the constraint manifold; and feature-caching heuristics are blind to constraint geometry, inducing mode confusion in the high-noise regime. We introduce ManifoldCache, the first training-free, data-free accelerator designed from first principles for CMDMs. The key insight is that the conditional score decomposes orthogonally into a normal component, which enforces constraint satisfaction, and a tangential component, which navigates within the manifold. Exploiting this structure, we prove that the noise-schedule midpoint is a sharp safe-caching boundary: caching before it incurs provably bounded error, while caching after it guarantees a strictly positive fraction of trajectories suffer mode confusion, a gap that persists up to the boundary. We further prove that deeper network blocks admit provably larger certified cache strides within the safe phase, as a consequence of the score decomposition propagating through block Jacobians. The resulting schedule requires no calibration data, along with zero training overhead.

</details>


### [68] [Towards Credible Agent-Based Policy Simulations: Disentangling Opportunities and Preferences in a Financial Inclusion Case Study of Egypt](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04515)
*Alba Aguilera,Georgina Curto,Nardine Osman,Ahmed Al-Awah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: A data-driven, Capability-Approach linked ABM is developed and calibrated to disentangle opportunity vs preference drivers of financial inclusion gaps in Egypt, producing group-specific, policy-relevant hypotheses.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Policy simulations need empirical grounding and credible assumptions so they can inform decisions; the Capability Approach offers a normative framing to distinguish agents' opportunities (feasibility) from their motivations (preferences), which is crucial to understand financial-inclusion inequalities and to make simulation results policy-relevant.

Method: Develop a general modelling framework consistent with the Capability Approach; implement an agent-based model of heterogeneous individuals and firms whose behaviour depends on financial state, barriers, opportunities, preferences; fit the model in two stages—initialisation to build representative synthetic populations and calibration to estimate behavioural (preference) parameters—while fixing feasibility parameters; analyse gaps between opportunities and realised outcomes; provide code release.

Result: A contextualised ABM for financial inclusion in Egypt that, after two-stage fitting, yields transparent, group-specific hypotheses about institutional/social barriers versus motivational drivers of inclusion gaps; the approach improves model–stakeholder linkage and reproducibility (code available).

Conclusion: The paper proposes a Capability-Approach aligned, data- and expert-informed agent-based modelling framework to enhance the credibility of policy simulations; it is demonstrated on financial inclusion in Egypt by building a fitted ABM that separates feasibility (opportunity) parameters from preference (motivation) parameters, producing transparent, group-specific hypotheses about drivers of observed inclusion gaps.

Abstract: Credibility is a central topic for agent-based models intended to support policy-making. Simulations must not only represent the target scenarios and their core dynamics but also demonstrate that their assumptions, parameters, and outputs are empirically grounded and sufficiently accurate for their intended use. This paper addresses this challenge by presenting a general modelling framework, aligned with the Capability Approach, for building credible policy simulations that rely on data and domain-expert knowledge. It then demonstrates how it can be contextualised and implemented to study the social challenge of financial inclusion in Egypt, building on an agent-based model that represents heterogeneous individuals and firms behaving according to their financial states, barriers, opportunities, and preferences. The model is fitted to real-world data in two stages, initialisation and calibration, which respectively build representative synthetic populations and estimate behavioural parameters. By fixing the feasibility parameters, which determine agents' opportunities, and calibrating preference parameters across different population groups, we are able to distinguish and analyse the role of institutional and social barriers in the system, as well as the role of agents' motivations and priorities. This calibration stage provides transparent and group-specific hypotheses about the drivers of observed financial-inclusion gaps, which can further be analysed as gaps between agents' opportunities and realised outcomes, a very relevant insight for policy-making. This paper is thus a step towards improving the credibility and usefulness of policy simulations, strengthening the relationship between the model, the real target system, and the stakeholders who will use it. The code is available at: \url{https://www.comses.net/codebase-release/df8383cb-f49b-4f09-8cce-73603b59adcc/}.

</details>


### [69] [EvoCast: Reliable Autonomous Research Agents for Iterative Forecasting Architecture Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04517)
*Kaipeng Xu,Xianli Yan,Yan Wang,Xiang Liu,Shan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种将开放式 LLM 搜索与确定性程序控制结合的自治架构演化系统，通过迭代消融与证据驱动的研究方向生成，减少人工干预并在实证案例中超越若干基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手动将丰富的深度时序预测模型适配到具体任务仍需大量专家工作；现有 AutoML 受限于预定义搜索空间，而通用 LLM 研究代理在实验协议、实现可靠性和模型推广上缺乏信任保障。

Method: 构建‘认知—权威’分离架构：LLM 代理负责开放式假设生成、研究方向与代码实现尝试；确定性程序权威负责源代码编辑边界、规范化评估流程、和模型晋升决策。系统先通过实际执行的机制消融建立并诊断任务基线，再基于数据集特征、诊断结果、历史回合和失败记录生成有证据支撑的研究方向，实验结果作为循证反馈驱动后续回合。

Result: EvoCast 在三个真实世界预测案例中完成了复杂架构改动：相比纯代理基线和若干强基线，展示了更高的实现成功率、更低的代理端 token/时间成本，并开发出效果更优的任务专用架构；代码已开源。

Conclusion: EvoCast 有效地将自治研究代理与确定性程序权威相结合，能够在迭代演化过程中自动生成并实现面向特定时序预测任务的架构改动，从而提升实现成功率并在若干真实数据集上超越对照基线。

Abstract: Deep time-series forecasting models have rapidly diversified, yet adapting them to a specific task still requires extensive expert effort in model selection, mechanism diagnosis, architecture design, implementation, and evaluation. Existing AutoML methods are constrained by predefined search spaces, while general-purpose LLM research agents lack reliable control over experimental protocols and model promotion. We introduce EvoCast, a fully autonomous research-agent system for iterative forecasting architecture evolution. EvoCast first establishes and diagnoses a task-specific baseline through executed mechanism ablations, then generates evidence-grounded research directions from dataset characteristics, diagnostic results, prior rounds, and failure records. Its central design, cognition-authority separation, assigns open-ended hypothesis generation and code implementation to LLM agents, while deterministic program authorities control source-edit boundaries, canonical evaluation, and promotion decisions. Experimental outcomes are accumulated as evidence to guide subsequent rounds. Results show that EvoCast completes complex architecture modifications with higher implementation success and lower agent-side token/time cost, and develops task-specific architectures that outperform selected baselines, strong forecasting models, and agent baselines in three real-world forecasting cases. The code is available at https://github.com/18e0-x/EvoCast.

</details>


### [70] [Action-Consequence Alignment for Reliable Planning and Self-Improving in Latent World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04539)
*Jinping Wang1,Zhiqiang Gao,Xiantong Zhen,Ling Shao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ACA通过惩罚局部替代动作在预测误差上的优势，使世界模型的预测更与动作后果对齐，从而提升基于预测的规划效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 注意到低预测误差并不能保证可用的规划——模型可能为物理上更差的动作给出更低的预测误差（受自我挠痒实验启发的动作-感知不对应现象），从而导致规划选错动作，需要使预测误差与动作后果对齐。

Method: 分析预测-规划不一致性并量化局部替代动作的“prediction advantage”；设计ACA训练项，在训练过程中惩罚局部搜索到的替代动作相对于事实动作在预测误差上的优势；不引入额外模型组件或训练时与环境的交互，并将同一原理用于指导额外数据采集策略。

Result: 在多样环境与评估设定下，ACA提高了规划性能并减少真实目标误差；基于ACA的定向数据采集优于随机局部采样。

Conclusion: 提出Action Consequence Alignment（ACA）作为训练目标，弥合预测模型与规划之间的差距，使模型在局部备选动作上不再显示预测误差优势，从而提高规划可靠性与真实目标精度。

Abstract: Latent world models learn to predict observed transitions, yet low prediction error alone does not guarantee reliable planning. Inspired by self tickling experiments in neuroscience showing that disrupting motor sensory correspondence increases prediction mismatch, we examine whether learned world models preserve an analogous action consequence correspondence.The results show nearby alternatives can receive lower prediction errors despite producing physical outcomes farther from the recorded target. With that future treated as a goal, this reveals a concrete prediction planning mismatch: the model assigns a lower cost to an action that achieves the target less accurately. To mitigate this gap, we introduce Action Consequence Alignment (ACA), a training objective that complements forward prediction by penalizing the prediction error advantage of locally searched alternatives over factual actions without additional model components or environment interactions during training. The same principle can also guide additional data collection for self improvement. We demonstrate that across diverse environments and evaluation settings, ACA improves planning performance and reduces real goal error, while ACA guided data collection outperforms random local sampling. These results support action consequence alignment as a practical principle for bridging predictive learning and reliable planning.

</details>


### [71] [Autonomous Structuring of Radiology Reports Across Modalities at Archive Scale Using an Open-Weight Large Language Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04541)
*Friedrich Puttkammer,Fabian Drexel,Marlene Fritzsche,Era Stambollxhiu,Miriam Kumpf,Lena Schmitzer,Lea Schumann,Lina Xu,Johannes Moll,Jannik Lübberstedt,Zeineb Ben Chaaben,Anirudh Narayanan,Hartmut Häntze,Renato Cuocolo,Antonios Billis,Alexander Löser,Jawed Nawabi,Marcus R. Makowski,Cosmin I. Bercea,Shahrooz Faghihroohi,Lisa C. Adams,Keno K. Bressem*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: An open-weight LLM with hierarchical templates successfully structured a multi-million radiology-report archive automatically with high fidelity and throughput; key failure mode is multi-region reports.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Enable scalable, local, human‑free conversion of free-text radiology reports into structured data for downstream use (analytics, research, interoperability) using an open-weight LLM to avoid reliance on closed models or manual annotation.

Method: Developed a pipeline of 150 hierarchically organized templates and used the open-weight model gpt-oss-120B in three constrained-decoding steps to (1) select a template and (2) fill template fields. Evaluations included template-selection scoring against expert labels on 914 sampled reports, field-by-field correction of 920 radiography/CT reports by five residents, and full processing of a second-center archive (2010–2025) on one GPU.

Result: Template optimal selection 74.4% (95% CI 71.5–77.1%), appropriate selection 82.3% (79.7–84.6%); single-region 87.7% vs multi-region 54.1%. Macro semantic textual similarity 0.95 (radiography) and 0.97 (CT). Residents left 88.7% of 24,638 fields unchanged. Unsupported content flagged in ~1–1.5% of reports. Processed 2,186,982 reports producing 2,401,544 structured outputs at 1,258 reports/hour on one GPU; 96.5% of archive received structured output.

Conclusion: An open-weight LLM pipeline can automatically convert a large multimodality radiology-report archive into structured reports with high fidelity and throughput, though multi-region reports are the main remaining source of template-selection errors.

Abstract: Purpose: To develop and evaluate an open-weight large language model (LLM) pipeline that converts an entire archive of free-text radiology reports into structured reports without human oversight. Materials and Methods: In this retrospective study, a pipeline with 150 hierarchically organized templates was developed at one center and tested at a second center on reports from 2010 to 2025. The open-weight model gpt-oss-120B selects the template in three constrained-decoding steps and fills it on one local graphics processing unit. Template selection was scored against expert labels on 914 randomly sampled reports of five modalities, structuring quality on 920 radiography and CT reports corrected field by field by five residents. The pipeline then processed the complete archive of the second center. Proportions are reported with Wilson 95% confidence intervals (CIs). Results: An optimal template set was selected for 74.4% of reports (680 of 914; 95% CI: 71.5%, 77.1%) and an appropriate set for 82.3% (752 of 914; 95% CI: 79.7%, 84.6%), 87.7% for single-region and 54.1% for multi-region reports. Macro semantic textual similarity between output and corrected reference was 0.95 for radiography and 0.97 for CT, residents left 88.7% of 24,638 fields unchanged, and unsupported content was flagged in 1.0% and 1.5% of reports. Of 2,186,982 archive reports, 96.5% received structured output, 2,401,544 structured reports, at 1,258 reports per hour on one graphics processing unit. Conclusion: An open-weight LLM pipeline structured a complete multimodality report archive without human oversight with high content fidelity. Multi-region reports remained the main source of template errors.

</details>


### [72] [Decide, Ask, or Defer: Clinical LLMs under Incomplete Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04542)
*Mingzhan Yang,Weili Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 引入DECIDE/ASK/DEFER并采用盲化协议，揭示了临床LLM在信息获取与放弃决策上的多样性与权衡，但并未带来一致的策略改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的二元DECIDE/ABSTAIN把不同的非决策状态合并，不能显式评估信息获取行为，也无法区分需要更多证据与需要向临床医师转诊的情形。通过引入‘询问更多信息’这一动作，旨在更精细地刻画模型在证据不充分时的策略。

Method: 提出DECIDE/ASK/DEFER三元决策框架及一个盲化（禁止使用证据完整性元数据）协议；使用DDXPlus构造200个匹配的临床证据状态作为测试集；在这些状态上对Qwen、Gemini与GPT进行评估；包括有针对性信息获取与随机信息获取的匹配分析，并与二元DECIDE/ABSTAIN基线进行比较。

Result: 模型在相同证据下的动作选择存在显著差异（在200个状态中有137例不一致）。对于Qwen，有针对性地选取信息能显著改变其后续做出自主决定的概率，这一影响比诊断正确率更明显。与DECIDE/ABSTAIN基线对比表明，三元策略在安全性与自治性之间存在权衡：既能拯救部分错误的自动决策，也会使部分正确的自动决策被取消。

Conclusion: 将信息获取从“是否放弃/决定”中分离出来可以揭示二元放弃范式隐藏的行为差异；三元策略（DECIDE/ASK/DEFER）能在某些情况下挽救错误的自主决定但也会取消一些正确的自主决定，因此并未带来单方面的改进。

Abstract: Clinical LLMs must decide not only what diagnosis to produce, but also whether the available evidence is sufficient for autonomous decision making. Binary DECIDE/ABSTAIN formulations merge distinct non decision states and do not explicitly evaluate information acquisition. We introduce a DECIDE/ASK/DEFER formulation together with a blinded protocol that prevents models from using evidence completeness metadata. We evaluate Qwen, Gemini, and GPT on 200 matched clinical evidence states constructed from DDXPlus. The models show substantial differences in action selection under identical evidence, with disagreement in 137 of 200 states. For Qwen, a matched targeted versus random analysis shows that selected information changes the likelihood of a subsequent autonomous decision more clearly than diagnostic correctness. Its matched DECIDE/ABSTAIN baseline further reveals a safety autonomy tradeoff: the three action policy rescues some erroneous autonomous decisions but also removes some correct autonomous deci sions. These results show that separating information acquisition from clinician deferral exposes behavior that binary abstention hides, without yielding a uniformly improved decision policy.

</details>


### [73] [Label Agreement Does Not Measure Authorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04544)
*Amir Sabbaghziarani,Bradley Thomas Baker,Theodore J. LaGrow,Sergey Plis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 标签一致性是必要但不充分的指标；须独立测量授权、完整性与稳定性并在自动承诺前设置门控以暴露失败，且仍需人工介入与更严格的验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多团队将标签本体与元数据协调任务交给具代理性的LLM流水线；表面聚合分数常显良好，但流水线仍有隐蔽的失败模式，需系统化发现与量化这些隐藏问题。

Method: 构建一个基于代理的大语言模型（LLM）标注与元数据统一流水线，并在两个不同联盟的精神分裂症与对照神经影像队列（COBRE与FBIRN）上审计。分别测量并对照：标签一致性、授权性（agent是否有权提出该标签）、输出完整性（是否包含必需的可执行字段，特别是JSON字段）和稳定性（对上游证据变更的敏感性）；并用上游建议、重放初次回答、改变证据与控制实验配对。对出现的失败将承诺（commitment）加上条件门控以便人工干预。

Result: 主要量化结果：向代理展示上游建议仅使标签一致性从0.857微幅提升到0.870，但对选定动作的协议一致性从0.409跃升到0.830；一个模型解析为JSON的输出仍在10%案例缺失必需字段，另一个模型缺失率为33%；代理重放其初次答案在原始证据上得分完美，但在改变证据的情况下得分降为零；此外未被这些指标覆盖的一类行序错误会抹去大部分诊断信息。代码公开： https://github.com/amir-sbg/Label-Agreement-Does-Not-Measure-Authorization。

Conclusion: 标签一致性（label agreement）并不能衡量代理提出标签的授权性、输出的可操作性或对证据变化的稳定性；分别测量这些性质并在自动承诺前设置核查能揭示并路由这些失败，但不能从根本上消除错误。

Abstract: Many groups now delegate label ontology and metadata harmonization to agentic LLM pipelines. We built one and audited it. Our aggregate scores looked healthy, but the pipeline kept failing in ways they did not show, so we set out to find what they hid. Label agreement asks whether a proposed label matches a reference. It does not ask whether the agent was entitled to propose it, whether the output was complete enough to act on, or whether the label moved when the evidence moved. We measured those three separately on COBRE and FBIRN, two schizophrenia and control neuroimaging cohorts from different consortia, and they come apart, from label agreement and from each other. Showing the agent an upstream proposal barely moves label agreement, 0.857 to 0.870, while agreement on the chosen action doubles, 0.409 to 0.830. Output that parses as JSON still drops a required field on 10% of one model's cases and 33% of the other's. And an agent that replays its first answer scores perfectly on original cases and zero once we change the evidence that decides them. Downstream, a row-order error that none of these metrics reports erases most of the diagnostic signal. So we measure these properties apart, pair each with a control, and gate commitment on the result, which makes failures visible and easy to route to a person. None of this prevents failure. Our reference labels are rule-derived, so agreement with them means consistency, not correctness. Code is available at https://github.com/amir-sbg/Label-Agreement-Does-Not-Measure-Authorization.

</details>


### [74] [$\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$: Guiding Agent Evolution from Pattern Recognition to Solution Invention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04555)
*Wenyin Liu,Yiheng Huang,Kai Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TRIZ^a 用 TRIZ 原则引导 LLM 智能体的进化式生成并以功能信息与理想度衡量改进，实验在网络安全任务中带来可追溯的小幅 F1 改进，但推广受硬门控限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弥补传统自动化 R&D 中随机或未分类型变异的不可解释性与低效性，提供可解释、结构化且可追溯的自动化创新流水线，在对抗性强且演化快速的领域（如网络安全）验证其效力。

Method: 将 TRIZ 的 40 条发明原则和矛盾矩阵作为变异/生成指导，使用 LLM 驱动的智能体进行进化式搜索；在固定的参考契约下用功能信息（FI）与 TRIZ 理想度构建可比尺度，并以硬门控规则决定哪些改进可被推广；通过配对重运行(protocol fingerprinting)与硬门验证评估改进。

Result: 在 PowerDuck GOOSE、CICIoT2023、CIC-DDoS2019 三个基线实验中分别实现绝对 F1 提升 +2.88、+4.23、+0.15 百分点；在对 CICIoT2023 进行 45 次活动的完整 campaign 中宏平均 F1 从 0.8325 提升到 0.8483，但该提升未通过冻结的推广门控。所有改进路径均可追溯到具体的矛盾与 TRIZ 原则选择。

Conclusion: TRIZ^a 提出将 TRIZ 发明理论与基于大模型的智能体进化搜索结合，用结构化、可解释的原则替换随机变异并通过功能信息与理想度的可比尺度衡量有益/有害功能，辅以硬门控区分推广与指标改进。实验在多个网络安全数据集上给出小幅但可追溯的 F1 提升，同时保留从矛盾识别到代码变换与推广决策的端到端可追溯性。

Abstract: We propose $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ (TRIZ exponentiated by an agent), a general R\&D automation paradigm that combines TRIZ inventive theory with LLM-driven agent evolutionary search. TRIZ's 40 inventive principles and contradiction matrix provide structured, explainable directions for solution generation, replacing random or untyped mutation with theory-guided ideation. Functional information (FI), operationalized under a frozen reference contract, is combined with TRIZ Ideality to measure useful and harmful function on a commensurable information scale, while hard gates keep promotion distinct from metric improvement. We validate $\mathrm{TRIZ}^{a}$ in cybersecurity--an adversarial and rapidly evolving domain--on PowerDuck GOOSE, CICIoT2023, and CIC-DDoS2019. Under paired-rerun protocols with protocol fingerprinting and hard-gate validation, the legacy experiments yield absolute F1 improvements of $+2.88$, $+4.23$, and $+0.15$ percentage points, respectively. A completed 45-activity CICIoT2023 campaign further increases macro-F1 from $0.8325$ to $0.8483$, but does not pass its frozen promotion gate. Every result remains traceable from contradiction identification and TRIZ principle selection to code transformation, evaluation metrics, and promotion decision.

</details>


### [75] [Recursive Improvement of a Differentiable Scientific Software Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04561)
*Pengcheng Hou,Xiaojun Tan,Sihan Hu,Ruisi Wang,Shuo Chen,Lei Wang,Youjin Deng,Kun Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个以AI智能体和统一微分接口为核心、面向异构科学软件生态的可微编程演化流程，并在20个包上验证，证明能提升可微性、加速梯度计算并推动研究工作流复用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可微分编程能将科学计算与基于梯度的推断、学习与设计连接起来，但将其扩展到异构且已建立的软件生态需要大量专门工作（实现导数、接口集成与质量评估）；以AI编码智能体辅助可以加速该过程，但需要系统地识别研究需求并评估实现质量。

Method: 构建统一的微分接口以暴露可复用导数规则并与现有数值例程集成；以研究需求驱动开发，使用智能体辅助在不同包中实现自动微分（AD）扩展；通过独立导数检查、工作流测试与性能基准对实现进行验证；将验证后的软件、测试与研究程序作为共享资源供后续研究重用。

Result: 在物理、化学与生物建模领域跨20个软件包构建并验证了AD扩展；研究工作流展示了导数与实现的重用；基准测试显示在梯度评估与参数估计上相比有限差分有计算节约；通过研究驱动修订使先前不支持的工作流可微、修正了科学可观测量的导数并消除冗余计算；在量子控制与热设计示例中基于物理评估修订目标函数，提升设计效果同时重用既有导数。

Conclusion: 本文提出并验证了一个以智能体驱动的可微科学软件演化环境，通过统一的可微分接口、可复用的导数规则以及独立的质量评估，把需求识别、开发实现和验证评估串联起来，从而在现有科学软件生态中扩展可微编程能力。

Abstract: Differentiable programming connects scientific computation with gradient-based inference, learning and design. Extending these capabilities across a heterogeneous software ecosystem requires specialized effort to implement derivatives, integrate interfaces and evaluate quality. AI coding agents can accelerate this transformation, but translating their capabilities into useful scientific software requires identifying research needs and evaluating how well implementations meet them. We present an environment for agent-driven evolution of differentiable scientific software that connects demand identification, development and quality evaluation. A unified differentiation interface exposes reusable derivative rules alongside existing numerical routines, allowing research tasks to share these capabilities. Research requirements guide development, with implementations assessed through independent derivative checks, workflow tests and performance evaluation. Validated software, research programs and tests become shared resources for subsequent studies. We construct and validate automatic differentiation extensions across 20 packages spanning physical, chemical and biological modeling, with research workflows demonstrating reuse across tasks. Benchmarks demonstrate computational savings over finite differences in gradient evaluation and complete parameter estimation. Research-driven revisions make previously unsupported workflows differentiable, correct derivatives of scientific observables and eliminate redundant computation. Quantum-control and thermal-design studies revise objectives in response to physical evaluation, improving designs while reusing existing derivatives. This work provides a practical approach to expanding differentiable programming across established scientific software and organizing AI agents around the recursive improvement of a shared computational ecosystem.

</details>


### [76] [Strong Helps Weak: Directional Cross-Modal Alignment Transfer in Multi-modal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04580)
*Hoigi Seo,Byung Hyun Lee,Minjun Kim,Dohyun Mah,Jongho Lee,Se Young Chun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 发现并利用一种不对称模型合并现象：将对齐良好的源模态定向合并到数据稀缺目标模态，可以增强模态-文本对齐并显著提升目标性能；基于此提出的DCAT提供闭式、高效的权空间对齐转移方法，无需额外大规模训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在数据稀缺或每样本体积大（如音频、视频）或领域特定模态下，难以获得同模态的多样变体用于传统模型合并或大规模再训练。发现一种不对称现象：将强源模态合并入弱目标模态能提升后者性能，故希望刻画其成因并设计高效的对齐转移方法。

Method: 提出Directional Cross-modal Alignment Transfer (DCAT)：基于理论导出的对齐相关量（与互信息下界相关），构造一个增强对齐的目标函数，并导出一个闭式的权空间（weight-space）解。该解可用小规模校准集计算，从而直接在模型权重层面实现源模态向目标模态的定向合并，免去大规模再训练。

Result: 理论上推导出一个与下游表现高度相关的互信息下界，并证明其单调依赖于对齐相关量；实证上DCAT在多个目标模态基准上优于现有模型合并方法，能用小规模校准集和闭式权重更新实现显著性能提升。

Conclusion: 将来自数据丰富、对齐良好的源模态的对齐信息定向迁移到数据稀缺的目标模态，能在不额外微调的情况下显著提升目标模态的大模型性能，且这种增益主要来源于模态-文本对齐性的增强。

Abstract: Multi-modal large language models (MLLMs) achieve strong modality understanding by pairing a large language model (LLM) with an encoder for a target modality such as vision, video, or audio. However, improving an MLLM's capability for a given modality typically requires additional training on large modality-specific datasets, incurring substantial data collection and compute costs. Model merging offers an alternative, but it is often infeasible for data-scarce, large per-sample size, or domain-specific modalities (\textit{e.g.}, audio and video), where same-modality model variants are rarely available. In this work, we characterize an intriguing asymmetric phenomenon: merging a well-aligned, data-rich source-modality MLLM into a data-scarce target-modality MLLM substantially improves the target on its own benchmarks. Our theoretical and empirical analyses show that this gain stems from enhanced alignment between modality-specific and textual tokens, induced by the stronger donor modality. Specifically, we derive a mutual-information lower bound that is monotonic in alignment-related quantities and strongly correlated with downstream MLLM performance. Building on this principle, we propose Directional Cross-modal Alignment Transfer (DCAT), a novel framework that transfers textual alignment from a strong, well-aligned source (donor) modality to a weak target (recipient) modality, boosting target-modality performance without further fine-tuning. We further show that the alignment-enhancing objective admits a closed-form weight-space solution computed from only a small calibration set. DCAT outperforms existing model-merging methods, offering an efficient path toward cross-modal alignment transfer. Project page with code is available at \url{https://seohoiki3215.github.io/DCAT_project_page}

</details>


### [77] [Bounds, Decompositions and Null Behaviour of KRATOS: A Mathematical Specification of a Recognition-Comparability Diagnostic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04592)
*Maria Dolores Gonzalez,Alberto Barbado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 完整刻画了KRATOS的数学性质与解释条件，揭示其对群体大小分布和引用长尾敏感，并提供置换检验下的解析矩与稳定性洞见。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提供一个在固定、有限分析群组宇宙内，量化参与（文献分布）与识别（引用分配）不一致性的可解释、可计算的复合诊断规范。

Method: 对组成指标进行解析推导：证明识别对齐分数仅依赖于各组均值与总体均值之比；导出构成上限为到均匀参考的全变差的仿射函数；使用Fréchet型不等式建立参与—识别因子的界限；给出对数分解与不变性，并推导在全局和分层置换无效模型下识别比率的闭式一二矩。

Result: 得到了一组封闭形式的代数与统计属性：比例依赖性、构成天花板与Fréchet界、对数分解、主/对称识别分数排序关系、不变性以及置换无效下矩的闭式表达；并指出该指标对小群体、长尾引用与元数据重分配的敏感性。

Conclusion: KRATOS为将文献参与度与引用权重对比的群组结构诊断工具，其数学结构可被完整刻画并解释其界限、相等条件与不等式约束。

Abstract: KRATOS is a group-structured bibliometric diagnostic that compares the distribution of documents (participation) with the distribution of citation weight (recognition) over a fixed, finite universe of analytical groups. This note gives its complete mathematical specification and derives the properties that govern its interpretation. Beyond bounds and equality conditions for each component, we show that the recognition-alignment score depends on citation data only through ratios of group mean citation rates to the corpus mean; that the composition ceiling of the participation--recognition factor is an affine function of the total variation distance to the uniform reference; and that the factor itself satisfies Fréchet-type bounds in terms of this ceiling and a participation-weighted recognition score. We establish an exact logarithmic decomposition of the composite index, an ordering between the primary and a reciprocal-symmetric recognition score, invariance properties, and closed-form first and second moments of the recognition ratios under global and stratified permutation nulls. These results explain why composite orderings can be sensitive to small groups, heavy-tailed citation distributions and metadata reassignment. All results are verified numerically with an accompanying script. The specification concerns measurement structure only; it does not define a measure of epistemic change or justice.

</details>


### [78] [LatentIndex: Cross-Layer Sharing with Layer-Specific Selection for Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04635)
*Zhaohui Wang,Zhixin Pan,Fanxu Meng,Muhan Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LatentIndex shares continuous latent caches across groups of layers and uses layer-specific scoring to keep per-layer selection flexibility; it reduces cache storage and indexer computation, improves attention-mass recall versus IndexCache, and—via a hierarchical refinement—delivers multi× decode speedups while maintaining quality near native dynamic sparse attention.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sparse attention indexers reduce attention computation but still repeat index selection and keep per-layer key caches; reusing indices across layers lowers overhead but forces identical token sets across layers. The goal is to get the efficiency of reuse while allowing layers to select different tokens.

Method: Group consecutive attention layers; construct a shared latent cache from the anchor (first) layer’s hidden states; absorb key decoders into queries so the shared cache can be scored directly; provide per-layer scoring heads to allow independent selection; introduce a training-free calibration and a training-aware variant; propose Hierarchical Selection (HS) where followers refine a shared candidate set from the anchor.

Result: With four-layer sharing, LatentIndex cuts logical indexer-cache storage by 61.1% on DeepSeek-V3.2. Training-free LatentIndex improves head-wise attention-mass recall over IndexCache by up to 3.28 percentage points on DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5, while keeping RULER and LongBench performance near native DSA. HS yields 2.30–2.72× decode indexer speedups over DSA across 8K–128K contexts, with most of LatentIndex’s recall retained.

Conclusion: LatentIndex enables efficient cross-layer reuse of continuous latent representations for sparse attention indexers, significantly reducing per-layer key-cache storage and repeated selection work while preserving layer-specific token selection and competitive downstream quality.

Abstract: Sparse attention reduces core-attention computation, but its indexers still incur repeated selection work and per-layer key-cache storage. Reusing selected indices across layers reduces this overhead but constrains multiple layers to the same token set. We introduce LatentIndex, which extends the latent-sharing principle of Multi-head Latent Attention across indexer layers. Each layer group constructs a shared latent cache from its first layer's hidden states, while layer-specific scoring enables independent token selection. Absorbing key decoders into queries enables direct scoring of the shared cache without reconstructing historical per-layer keys. We develop training-free calibration and investigate a training-aware instantiation of this principle. To balance quality and computation, a hierarchical selection (HS) variant lets followers independently refine a shared candidate set proposed by the anchor. With four-layer sharing, LatentIndex reduces logical indexer-cache storage by 61.1% on DeepSeek-V3.2. Across DeepSeek-V3.2 and GLM-5, training-free LatentIndex improves head-wise attention-mass recall over IndexCache by up to 3.28 percentage points while maintaining RULER and LongBench performance close to native DSA. HS further achieves 2.30-2.72 times decode indexer speedups over DSA across 8K-128K contexts, retaining most of LatentIndex's recall. LatentIndex offers a new perspective on cross-layer indexing: sharing continuous representations rather than discrete selections enables efficient reuse while preserving layer-specific token selection.

</details>


### [79] [SEIS: Self-Evolving Inference Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04646)
*Zhen Xu,Jingyu Liu,Zongze Li,Tahseen Rabbani,Ce Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 使用代理驱动的自我进化策略可自动重构推理引擎，实现大幅加速且保持准确性，表明端到端自动优化的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作多聚焦局部优化（如内核或内存），而整系统的端到端优化潜力未被充分挖掘。

Method: 通过多轮迭代会话，代理继承先前会话经验与代码变更，自动对mini-sglang引擎的整体设计与实现进行重构和优化。

Result: 在H100上运行Qwen3-0.6B时，优化后引擎比原始实现快3.27倍，并在单请求负载下优于vLLM、TensorRT-LLM和SGLang；并通过数值差异与数学和长上下文检索任务的下游精度验证正确性。

Conclusion: 代理化自我进化（SEIS）能在无人工干预下对整个推理引擎进行端到端优化，显著提升吞吐量并保持数值与下游任务精度。

Abstract: Inference systems determine how fast and how cheaply language models can be served, so making them faster has direct practical value. However, prior work focuses mostly on optimizing certain parts such as kernels or memory within the large system. In this work, we take a holistic approach and apply agentic self-evolution to optimize the whole system end-to-end. Our SEIS (Self-Evolving Inference Systems) autonomously optimizes the entire mini-sglang engine without human intervention through iterative sessions with inherited experiences and code changes. Serving Qwen3-0.6B on H100, the resulting engine reaches 3.27X the throughput of the original mini-sglang implementation and beats SOTA engines like vLLM, TensorRT-LLM, and SGLang in the single-request workload. The correctness of the optimized inference engine by SEIS is tested in terms of numerical difference and downstream accuracy on math and long-context retrieval tasks. The code and session histories show that the speedup comes from redesigning the whole engine and that building on earlier sessions beats independent attempts. These results suggest that agentic self-evolution can optimize a complex system end-to-end. The evaluation also has to evolve with the engine, and letting agents evolve it is a natural next step.

</details>


### [80] [PermVLA: Factorization Order as a Regularizer for VLA Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04659)
*Yanqiao Chen,Yuhan Rui,Dongsheng Hou,Zijie Nie,Yutong Wan,Qi Hao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) policies commonly learn action chunks through a fixed left-to-right (LTR) factorization, although the same expert trajectory distribution admits many valid chain-rule factorizations. We identify factorization order as an overlooked regularization choice and introduce causally anchored permutation (CAP), which samples action reveal orders with a tunable chronological prefix. Its auxiliary objective trains one shared policy to predict actions from different known subsets of the same expert chunk, while deployment retains deterministic LTR control. We call this conditional-set augmentation: it creates multiple conditional prediction problems from one expert chunk without adding demonstrations. This discourages reliance on the single chronological prefix used by ordinary teacher forcing. Controlled experiments show that CAP consistently outperforms standard LTR training on LIBERO and LIBERO-Plus, with the same advantage appearing in cross-dataset CALVIN evaluation. A diagnostic that measures the expected squared difference between a chunk's joint log likelihood under two reveal orders verifies that CAP training internalizes agreement across reveal orders. These findings position sampled subset-conditioned auxiliary objectives as a general recipe for constructing VLA regularizers, illustrated by an extension to diffusion action generators.

</details>


### [81] [Efficient Neural Surrogates for Linear Radiation Transport on the Lattice and Hohlraum benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04665)
*Carmelo Gonzales,Steffen Schotthöfer,Cory D. Hauck*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 对比了两类参数匹配的神经替代器在二维线性RTE基准上的表现，发现架构对归纳偏置（如傅里叶特征、区域加权损失）有强烈偏好，建议针对每种架构和QoI单独设计训练配方；并提供了完整复现资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性RTE在核工程、惯性约束聚变、医学成像和天体物理等领域是计算瓶颈，传统求解器在高维相空间上昂贵，导致外层工作流（优化、不确定度量化、参数扫描）受预算限制。神经替代有望通过在大量查询间摊销成本缓解这一瓶颈，但不同模型家族的架构与归纳偏置在不同传输问题间的可迁移性不明。

Method: 作者将两种参数匹配的神经替代架构作为端到端的最终时刻粒子浓度近似器进行基准比较：一个是基于物理注意力的Transolver，另一个是多尺度图网格的Bi-Stride Multi-Scale MeshGraphNet（BSMS-MGN）。在二维线性RTE的Lattice与Hohlraum基准上进行训练和评估，并通过对傅里叶特征和基于区域加权训练损失的消融试验来揭示架构相关的归纳偏置差异。

Result: 实验表明：1) 傅里叶特征和区域加权损失的效用强烈依赖于具体架构；2) 两个架构在相同参数预算下表现差异显著且对不同QoI敏感性不同；3) 单一的场级评分不能完全反映下游实用性，必须按感兴趣量评估。作者公开了训练/数据/评估管线以便复现与下游集成。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：针对线性辐射输运方程的神经替代模型其性能高度依赖网络架构所对应的归纳偏置，常见的设计选择（如傅里叶特征、加权损失）不能通用地从一个模型家族移植到另一个；因此需要针对每个架构和感兴趣量（QoI）单独调优。作者同时发布了训练配方和数据/评估管线以支持复现和在外循环工作流中的应用。

Abstract: Linear radiation transport equations (RTEs) form the simulation foundations underpinning design and analysis tasks in nuclear engineering, inertial confinement fusion, medical imaging, and astrophysics, but resolving the high-dimensional phase space at engineering fidelity remains expensive enough that outer-loop workflows, such as design optimization, uncertainty quantification, and parameter sweeps, are routinely budget-bound on traditional solvers. Neural surrogates promise to relax this bottleneck by amortizing simulation cost across thousands of downstream queries, but the architectural choices and engineered inductive biases that make a surrogate accurate on one transport problem do not transfer straightforwardly across model families. We benchmark two parameter-matched neural surrogate architectures, the physics-attention Transolver and the multi-scale graph network Bi-Stride Multi-Scale MeshGraphNet (BSMS-MGN), as end-to-end approximations of the final-time particle concentration for the two-dimensional linear RTE on the canonical Lattice and Hohlraum benchmarks. An ablation across Fourier features and region-weighted training loss exposes strongly architecture-dependent inductive-bias preferences, indicating that design choices common to physics-informed surrogate workflows must be revisited per architecture rather than imported across model families, and that downstream utility depends on per-QoI sensitivity rather than a single field-level score. The model training recipe, training data, and evaluation pipeline are released alongside this paper to support reproduction, transfer to related transport problems, and evaluation as amortized forward-model components in larger outer-loop simulation workflows.

</details>


### [82] [A Tropical Geometry View of Forgetting: A Per-Unit Projector for Knowledge-Preserving Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04670)
*Yuyang Zhang,Xiaoyin Chen,Chunlin Ren,Qihuang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用ReLU热带几何提出门控感知逐单元投影器，理论量化保护代价并在OPT模型上实证可在更低预算下减少微调遗忘。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在语言模型微调中，更新权重会破坏原有能力（遗忘）。已有无回放的保护方法（Adam-NSCL、GPM）通过在每行禁止一个共有子空间来限制更新，但这种“行级共享子空间”过于粗糙，未利用每个单元的激活结构。作者利用ReLU的分段线性（热带）结构设计更精细的单元级保护机制。

Method: 基于ReLU层的热带几何结构，证明层输出变化的平方可以分解为三类项（in-cell, open-to-closed, closed-to-open），据此定义每个单元的门控感知投影器——对每个单元仅在其激活的（open）token子空间上保护权重；证明预算分离定理，给出精确保护所需秩的下界；在OPT系列模型上与Adam-NSCL、GPM和若干对照集合进行比较，并分析修剪修复（pruning repair）中的问题与凸约束的界。

Result: 给出精确恒等式与理论下界，提出并实现门控感知逐单元投影器；在OPT-1.3b上，在各预算（每行9到60方向）下均比Adam-NSCL忘得少，差距从1.1x扩大到4.3x；在OPT-6.7b上在相同预算下匹配Adam-NSCL但学习更多；表明open/closed分区是关键变量，open token集合优于随机/符号盲/反门控集合；在修剪修复中，传统的导数局部模型对open对盲目，凸一侧惩罚可限制权重逸出多边形。

Conclusion: 提出并验证了一个基于ReLU热带几何的“门控感知”（gate-aware）逐单元投影器，能在微调时更精细地保护原有模型的行为，从而减少遗忘；理论上给出一个平方输出变化的精确恒等式和预算分离定理，量化了精确保护的代价；在OPT-1.3b和OPT-6.7b上实验证明其在相同或更低预算下优于Adam-NSCL和GPM。

Abstract: Fine-tuning a language model on new text degrades what it already does. Replay-free projectors such as Adam-NSCL and GPM forbid one shared subspace of a layer's inputs in every row of the update. The tropical geometry of a ReLU layer shows why this is too coarse. In data space, the units' walls are tropical hypersurfaces whose cells are dual to the upper vertices of a zonotope; in weight space, each old token is a hyperplane, and the tokens cut out a polyhedron, the closure of the weights that keep every token on its side. An exact identity joins the two pictures: the squared change of the layer's output under any weight change splits into in-cell, open-to-closed and closed-to-open terms, and the first two live on the tokens each unit fires on. The identity names a gate-aware per-unit projector, and a budget-separation theorem prices exact protection: it costs a unit the rank of its own open tokens, while a shared subspace pays at least the rank of their union in every row. On OPT-1.3b, where 96% of (token, unit) pairs are closed, the projector forgets less than Adam-NSCL at all six matched budgets from 9 to 60 constrained directions per row, the gap widening from $1.1\times$ to $4.3\times$; with 1/5.5 of the directions it halves the forgetting of Adam-NSCL at GPM's energy threshold. On OPT-6.7b, it matches Adam-NSCL's forgetting at matched budget while learning more. As the theory predicts, the open/closed partition is the operative variable: open tokens beat random, sign-blind and anti-gate token sets on 18 of 18 seed-pairs. In pruning repair, every derivative-based local model of the output error at the dense weights is blind to pairs that open: the minimisers of the gate-weighted objective can leave the polyhedron, the objective's closed-form solution is 1.94 nats worse than no repair on OPT-1.3b, and a convex one-sided penalty bounds the escape.

</details>


### [83] [MASBench: Benchmarking LLM-based Multi-Agent Collaboration under Partial Observability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04672)
*Qizhi Chu,Zekai Yu,Sijie Wen,Yang Liu,Chen Qian,Cheng Yang,Chuan Shi,Zhiyuan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MASBench：一个面向部分可观测条件的LLM为中心的多智能体协作基准，按任务类别评估协议/记忆/路由三类机制，并用确定性指标衡量性能与通信开销，提供实证设计建议并开源代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体基准多假设全局可观测，但现实场景常是部分可观测且受物理或隐私约束，缺乏系统化工具来评估不同协作机制与通信消耗之间的权衡，尤其是在以LLM为核心的自治代理团队中。

Method: 构建一个在部分可观测条件下的多智能体协作基准（MASBench），将任务划分为推理、调度和博弈三类，分别评估代表性协作机制（协议、记忆、路由）；定义确定性评测指标（性能得分、通信成本、成本效益）；在多种LLM骨干与机制配置上进行大规模实验以比较与归纳设计经验。

Result: 给出一个可复现的部分可观测MAS评测平台、明确的评估指标及实验结果，揭示了不同协作机制在任务类别、通信代价与整体效用上的差异，并为MAS设计提供实证建议；同时公开了代码库以便社区复现和扩展。

Conclusion: MASBench填补了现有多智能体评测在部分可观测场景下的空白，通过任务分层与三类协作机制（Protocol、Memory、Routing）的系统化评估，提供了关于性能与通信成本权衡的可重复、确定性基准，为基于LLM的多智能体系统设计提供了实证指导并公开了代码。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have progressively evolved into the core of autonomous agents. Building on this progress, LLM-based multi-agent systems (MAS) coordinate multiple agents into a synergistic team to accomplish complex tasks that exceed the capabilities of individual agents. The effectiveness of such systems depends not only on the agents themselves, but also on how collaboration mechanisms are designed and organized. Note that real-world collaboration is typically partially observable, where each agent can only access partial information about the environment due to physical or privacy-related constraints. However, many existing multi-agent benchmarks assume global observability, and leave limited support for systematically evaluating collaboration mechanisms. To bridge this gap, we introduce MASBench, a multi-agent collaboration benchmark designed under partially observable constraints. It is organized into three progressive task categories: Reasoning, Scheduling, and Game. Through this structure, we progressively evaluate three representative collaboration mechanisms: Protocol, Memory, and Routing. MASBench further provides deterministic evaluation metrics, including performance score, communication cost, and cost effectiveness, to characterize both collaboration outcomes and communication overhead. Experiments across diverse LLM backbones and mechanism configurations offer empirical guidance for effective MAS design. Code is available at: https://github.com/BUPT-GAMMA/MASBench

</details>


### [84] [When Debate Helps: Proposal Supply and Verification-Aware Readout in Multi-Agent Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04686)
*Zihao Zhao,Tunyu Zhang,Haizhou Shi,Yusong Zhao,Xinxi Zhang,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者将辩论成功分为提案供给与读出两部分，提出LVD和覆盖式代理构造，展示两者互补能使辩论超过投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释为何多主体辩论经常未能超越简单多数投票，指出成功辩论需要既有正确提案可供选择又能在读出阶段识别正确答案。

Method: 提出可恢复空余(recoverable headroom)度量，设计Latent Verification Debate (LVD)模型，在固定提案干预下通过答案特定验证证据影响最终生成；用神经灌木(neural-thicket)代理与覆盖目标构建社会以提升提案供给。

Result: 证明在固定提案供给下，真证据能改变答案概率和生成决策；覆盖选择的代理群能提高互补性提案供给并在匹配预算下提升聚合准确率；交互轮次带来额外收益。

Conclusion: 提案覆盖与基于证据的读出是辩论优于投票的互补条件。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate can improve reasoning, yet often fails to beat simple majority voting. We argue that successful debate requires two distinct mechanisms: proposal supply must surface a correct answer, and readout must identify that answer when voting misses it. We formalize the first requirement through recoverable headroom, which measures cases where a correct proposal is available but the majority answer is wrong. For the second, we develop Latent Verification Debate (LVD), an accounting model in which candidate proposals receive answer-specific verification evidence before final generation. Controlled fixed-proposal interventions estimate this latent effect in equivalent peer-support units and show that correct evidence changes answer probabilities and generated decisions while proposal supply remains fixed. To improve proposal supply, we construct societies from neural-thicket agents using labeled and label-free coverage objectives. Across two backbones and matched-budget reasoning benchmarks, coverage-selected societies increase complementary proposal supply and improve aggregate accuracy in repeated stochastic evaluations. Round-level controls further show that interaction provides gains beyond applying the same finalizer directly to the initial proposals. These results identify proposal coverage and truth-sensitive evidence use as complementary conditions for debate to outperform voting. Code is available at https://github.com/Wang-ML-Lab/when-debate-helps.

</details>


### [85] [RAGStress: A controlled benchmark for evaluating retrieval-augmented generation under knowledge-base degradation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04691)
*Shiqi Yang,Jiekai Ma,Gaoyuan Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: RAGStress 提供受控的知识库损坏基准，揭示语义保真性损坏对 RAG 的严重影响并警示常用评估指标的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前 RAG 评估多假设知识库无污染，缺乏对现实世界知识库退化（噪声、错误、对抗性注入等）下系统行为的系统化、可控测试。

Method: 构建受控基准 RAGStress：在单一知识库、按元数据过滤的实验设计上，从 57 个 MMLU 主题和 182,546 个文档中生成测试语料；对四类自然化损坏（事实错误、数字打字错误、相关性投毒、矛盾注入）施加三档损坏严重度（微妙、中等、明显）；进行了 52,500 次模型-问题-条件评估并提供生成脚本、损坏提示、元数据模式与评估代码的工件包。

Result: 发现包括：干净检索掩盖鲁棒性差异；语义保真类（如事实篡改、矛盾注入）对性能破坏比信号效用类（如数字错字、相关性扰动）更严重；无检索的准确率不能预测受损检索时的鲁棒性；混合知识库上的准确率不应被当作最坏情况鲁棒性指标。并明确基准的用途与局限。

Conclusion: RAGStress 表明，在受损知识库情形下，RAG 系统的鲁棒性存在显著差异且受语义保真类损坏影响最大；干净检索掩盖了模型间差异，基线无检索性能无法预测受损检索下的表现，混合 KB 性能不能代表最坏情况。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) is typically evaluated under the implicit assumption that the underlying knowledge base (KB) is clean, leaving the behaviour of RAG systems under realistic KB degradation poorly characterised. We introduce RAGStress, a controlled evaluation benchmark for stress-testing RAG systems under systematic KB corruption. The benchmark pairs four naturalistic corruption types (factual corruption, numeric typo, relevance poisoning, and contradiction injection) with three severity levels (subtle, moderate, and obvious) over a single-KB, metadata-filtered experimental design built from 57 MMLU subjects and 182,546 documents. Across 52,500 model-question-condition evaluations, RAGStress reveals that clean retrieval can mask robustness differences, semantic-fidelity corruptions are substantially more harmful than signal-utility perturbations, no-retrieval accuracy does not predict corrupted-retrieval robustness, and mixed-KB accuracy should not be treated as worst-case robustness. We document the benchmark's intended use, supported claims, and limitations, and provide an artifact bundle including generation scripts, corruption prompts, metadata schema, and evaluation code. RAGStress is intended as a controlled stress test for RAG robustness under KB corruption, not as a general model leaderboard.

</details>


### [86] [Penumbra: Sample-Efficient Adversarial Search for Regulatory Obligations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04693)
*Anthony Rhodes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Penumbra finds minimal-edit pairs that straddle compliance boundaries efficiently via an adaptive adversarial search and a two-evaluator committee, yielding higher defeat-mode coverage per budget than naive strategies.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: In domains like finance and healthcare, violations often lack lexical signatures and are sample-costly to discover because each probe requires a generation plus two adjudications; thus regulatory red-teaming is constrained by sample efficiency rather than brute-force volume.

Method: Start from a verified anchor response; iteratively expand an edit budget to generate candidate responses; use a two-evaluator committee to adjudicate compliance; stop when the committee verdict flips and emit the adjacent pair; allocate generation/adjudication budget adaptively to maximize coverage of distinct (obligation × defeat-mode) cells per candidate.

Result: Adaptive allocation achieves uniform-allocation full-budget coverage on 59% of candidates at matched budget, and 1.43× the defeat-mode coverage of naive enumeration for equal records. Empirically, on 60 financial-advisory obligations Penumbra returned 144 close pairs; on 18 clinical-triage obligations it produced 49 similar pairs. The method concentrates cost on exploring the boundary rather than text length.

Conclusion: Penumbra is an adaptive adversarial search method that efficiently finds minimal edits to model-generated responses that flip compliance under high-stakes obligations, emitting paired adjacent responses (one compliant, one violating) to reveal the decision boundary.

Abstract: Agents are entering finance, healthcare and law, sectors where a violation leaves no lexical signature and carries real penalties. Whether an omission is material, or a disclosure sufficient, depends on what the response left out. Probing such an obligation means finding responses one minimal edit from flipping compliance, and every probe costs a generation and two adjudications, so the binding constraint on regulatory red-teaming is sample efficiency, not volume. We introduce Penumbra, an adversarial search that walks from a verified anchor under an expanding edit budget until a two-evaluator committee changes its verdict, and emits the two adjacent responses that straddle the change. Allocation is adaptive, and the objective is coverage of the defeat surface: distinct (obligation x defeat mode) cells resolved per candidate. At matched budget, adaptive allocation reaches uniform allocation's full-budget coverage on 59% of the candidates; at equal records it covers 1.43x the defeat modes of naive enumeration, and the gain is confined to the axis it targets. On 60 screened obligations of a financial advisory constitution, Penumbra returns 144 pairs, each a compliant and a violating response that the committee places on opposite sides of the boundary, differing by a handful of words where a model asked for both directly produces texts sharing almost nothing. A second constitution, for clinical triage, reproduces this on 18 obligations: 49 pairs at the same tightness, with the same modes hardest. Pairs like these show where an obligation's own terms stop deciding, which is what an agent deployed under it must be tested against, and the search finds them at a cost that scales with the boundary, not the text.

</details>


### [87] [Not Self-Decidable: LLMs Cannot Draw the Boundary of What an Agent Verifier Can Check](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04699)
*Anthony Rhodes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 模型一致性只是提名而非证明。需要通过合成检查并在外部干预下验证其稳健性（CoVer），以构造真正的自判定边界，但这会以降低覆盖率为代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机是解决实际场景中验证器面对外部制定规则（金融、医疗、法律）时的难题：规则的可检验性不能由代理事先固定分割，而必须在运行时对每一个谓词做出决定，而这一工作量远超人工审计能力。

Method: 作者在六个语料（包括 EU AI Act、FINRA 指南和一个已部署的信贷智能体规则集）上收集约 22,000 条标签，来自三家实验室构建的四个模型。发现模型在明显例子上高度一致，但在监管文本上崩溃；基于一致性的“corroboration”常误判可由固定检查解决的谓词。为此提出 CoVer（corroborate-then-verify）：将模型一致性视为提名，随后合成检查并通过干预（读取智能体不可写字段）、确定性重写等方式验证该检查的鲁棒性。

Result: 主要结果：模型在显而易见的判断上几乎完美一致，但在监管文本上表现差且错误方向相反，无法作为保守选项；在已部署规则集上，模型共同倾向于过度断言“固定检查可行”（即不升级），CoVer 能显著拒绝多数由简单一致性误判的条目，但会降低覆盖率；将参考裁判作为“地面真相”的替代方法无法证明自判定性，因为同类裁判会巩固盲点。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：模型本身不能被信任为判断哪些规则可由固定检查决定（self-decidability）；验证器必须构造“可自判定性”的边界，而不是从模型中直接抽取这一能力。

Abstract: A verifier for an agent faces rules of two kinds: the ones a fixed check can settle and the ones that require a judge. A team that derives its own checks fixes that split up front. Where the requirements come from outside, as in finance, healthcare and law, the agent enforces rules it did not write, so the split falls to runtime, recurring for every predicate of every rule on every action at a rate no reviewer can audit. Every escalation scheme assumes a model can make that decision itself, that it is self-decidable. Across six corpora, including the EU AI Act, FINRA guidance and a deployed credit agent, we collect roughly 22,000 labels from four models built by three labs. They agree almost perfectly where the answer is obvious and collapse on regulatory text; their errors run in opposite directions, so no model can be trusted as the conservative choice; and on the deployed agent's own rule-set they err together, over-claiming that a fixed check will do, the direction that never gets escalated. We introduce CoVer (corroborate-then-verify), which treats unanimity as a nomination, admitting a predicate only when the check synthesized for it survives intervention, reading fields the agent cannot write and holding under deterministic rewording. That gate rejects most of what corroboration wrongly admits, at a cost in coverage we report rather than tune away. The obvious alternative, agreement with a reference judge, certifies nothing: it climbs from 30% to 77% across calibration bands while the genuinely decidable share does not move, because a judge drawn from the population under indictment ratifies the blind spot it shares. Self-decidability is not a capability to elicit from a model but a boundary the verifier must construct.

</details>


### [88] [Learning to Clarify Underspecified Intents Under Limited Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04719)
*Pranav M R,Manuel Cherep,Pattie Maes,Nikhil Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用价值-信息视角与基于强化学习的多轮模拟用户训练，让AI助理通过询问更具信息量的问题，在图像生成任务中以更少交互达到更高用户效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户常提供不完整的请求；助理需在猜测和询问之间权衡，且可用的用户交互有限，因此应优先获取因缺失信息导致的最大可避免效用损失的信息。

Method: 在图像生成任务中，将需求的歧义性重构为价值-信息（value-of-information）问题，建立强化学习框架并用多轮模拟用户进行训练，目标是最大化在不完全信息下的效用恢复；随后进行了预注册的人类实验验证。

Result: 在一项含456次交互、76名参与者的预注册实验中，该方法帮助用户以更少的问题、更短的交互时间和更低的成本，更好地匹配参考图像，显示了统计显著的改进。

Conclusion: 将提出询问策略视为价值信息问题，使助理在不确定性下获取能最大减少可避免用户效用损失的信息，从而显著提升交互效率与最终任务质量。

Abstract: AI assistants receive requests that leave out information needed for a good outcome, for example about users' preferences or goals. They must then either speculate or ask for more information before proceeding. We reconceptualize this as a value-of-information problem: the assistant should acquire information whose absence causes the greatest avoidable loss in user utility. This is rarely known ex ante; rather, assistants must predict it in order to optimally allocate limited user interactions. We instantiate this problem in image generation and derive a reinforcement learning framework using multi-turn simulated users to maximize utility recovery under uncertainty. In a preregistered study with 456 interactive sessions across 76 human participants, this helped users significantly better match reference images with significantly fewer questions, less total interaction time, and lower cost. This points toward a simple and scalable framework for training language model assistants to better disambiguate user intent by asking more informative questions.

</details>


### [89] [PyINE: A Framework for Scalable Elicitation and Oversight via Code Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04737)
*Pierre-Luc St-Charles,Alessandro Palmas,Damiano Fornasiere,Storm Lei,Mirko Bronzi,Jean-Pierre Falet,Iulian Serban,Yoshua Bengio*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PyINE 用可核验的程序执行轨迹构造可扩展监督基准，展示了常用廉价监督手段对捷径驱动错误覆盖不足，而更强的检查虽更可靠却更昂贵且难以阈值化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前推理模型会在表面上给出合理但不完整或误导性的推理——需要一种可定量、可核验、可扩展的实验设置来研究监督者是否能识别并阻止这类捷径驱动的错误。

Method: 提出 PyINE 框架：用可插装的 Python 程序作为可验证执行基底，通过程序产生环境和确定性执行轨迹作为权威标签，并机械化生成任务变体以便做反事实评估；构建 PyINE-v1 数据库（近 1M 确定性执行轨迹，>500k LLM 生成的代码变体）；用标准强化学习（带可验证回报）训练偏向捷径的模型；评估若干监督手段（激活探针、文本分类器、提示式评判器和轻量辩论协议）。

Result: 在 PyINE-v1 上：训练出的模型能更好地预测执行产出但仍在含误导提示的场景失败；廉价监督器在总体指标上表现尚可但对罕见捷径错误覆盖差；复杂/模型化的检查覆盖更全面但成本高且难以做成阈值化决策。PyINE-v1 将失败覆盖问题结构化为一个重用的实验平台。

Conclusion: PyINE-v1 提供了一个可验证、可扩展的实验平台，用以暴露和量化模型在“靠捷径但表面合理”推理时的监督盲点。研究表明：即便模型在预测执行结果上提升明显，仍会在与程序真实行为冲突的提示下出现系统性错误；廉价的监督方法常常漏掉这些罕见但重要的失败，而强监督方法覆盖更好但代价高且难以设定可靠阈值。

Abstract: Reasoning models can remain capable of solving a task while still defaulting to cheaper but misleading shortcuts. This creates a central oversight problem: when a model gives an answer with plausible but incomplete reasoning, can an overseer determine whether that output should be trusted? To study this problem, we introduce PyINE, a framework for scalable elicitation and oversight using instrumented Python programs as a verifiable execution substrate. In PyINE, programs define task environments, execution traces provide authoritative labels for outcomes and intermediate facts, and task variants can be generated mechanically rather than through static human annotation. We instantiate the framework in PyINE-v1, a first release built from nearly one million deterministic execution traces and over 500,000 matched LLM-generated code variants used for counterfactual evaluation. Using standard RL with verifiable rewards on cue-varied tasks, we train a shortcut-following model that improves substantially at predicting execution outcomes while still making systematic errors when misleading human-facing cues conflict with the program's realized behavior. We then evaluate activation probes, trained text classifiers, prompted judges, and a lightweight debate protocol as overseers of this model. We find that performance pooled at the dataset level can hide weak coverage of the failures that matter most: cheap learned overseers often miss rare shortcut-driven errors, while stronger model-based checks are more balanced but substantially costlier and harder to turn into reliable thresholded decisions. PyINE-v1 turns this failure-mode coverage problem into a reusable experimental setting for developing oversight methods that are verifiable, failure-mode-aware, and cost-sensitive.

</details>


### [90] [Toward a Locally Deployable Agentic Co-Scientist: Small-Model Planning for Early-Stage Drug Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04740)
*Tian Liang,Jiayu Chang,Alejandro F. Frangi,Mobarak I. Hoque,Richard A. Bryce*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LoRA-tuned compact LLMs can reliably plan modular tool invocations for drug-discovery pipelines within seen composition patterns, but struggle to compose entirely new multi-step workflows; compositional generalization is the main limitation to address.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Enable early-stage computational drug discovery by coordinating heterogeneous scientific tools in multi-step workflows with a lightweight, locally deployable planning model that is private and efficient.

Method: Collected 1,263 manually refined query–plan pairs via workflow-graph path coverage; defined a Unified Molecular Schema and a plug-in tool interface; LoRA fine-tuned three compact LLM families to generate parseable, schema-compliant plans invoking 18 tools; evaluated under query-level and workflow-grouped splits.

Result: On a query-level split, compact models (e.g., Llama 3.2-3B) reached tool-selection F1=0.998, sequence exact match=0.979, argument F1=0.960 on 47 held-out cross-group queries. On a stricter workflow-grouped split excluding identical ordered sequences, sequence exact match fell to 0.452–0.548, indicating difficulty in generalizing to novel workflow compositions.

Conclusion: Compact, locally deployable LLMs can plan calls to modular computational chemistry tools using a unified molecular schema, achieving high accuracy on held-out queries but showing limited compositional generalization to unseen full workflow sequences.

Abstract: Early-stage computational drug discovery requires coordinating heterogeneous scientific tools across multi-step workflows. We present a lightweight, tool-augmented framework in which a locally deployable compact language model plans calls to 18 modular tools. A Unified Molecular Schema maintains shared molecular records, while a plug-in interface supports tool replacement and extension. We construct 1,263 manually refined query-plan pairs through workflow-graph path coverage and apply LoRA fine-tuning to three compact model families. Under the query-level split, all fine-tuned models generate fully parseable and schema-compliant plans on 47 held-out cross-group queries. Llama 3.2-3B achieves a tool-selection F1 of 0.998, sequence exact match of 0.979, and argument F1 of 0.960. Under the stricter workflow-grouped split, which excludes identical ordered tool sequences across partitions, sequence exact match reaches 0.452 to 0.548, highlighting the remaining difficulty for compact models in generating complete workflow paths unseen during training. These results demonstrate the feasibility of compact, locally deployable planning while identifying compositional generalization as an important direction for further improvement.

</details>


### [91] [Formalizing the Moral Evaluation of Speech Acts: Truthfulness, Lies and Ethical Dilemmas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04747)
*Benjamin Icard,Gauvain Bourgne,Jeanne Bonnaventure,Jean-Gabriel Ganascia*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并实现了一个基于信念的可执行逻辑框架，用以在义务论、后果论和原则论视角下评估言语行为，案例展示了参数化的通用性，但在可扩展性、概率/不确定性建模与实证验证方面仍有改进空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在高道德风险下应选择何种言语行为的问题，回应康德与孔斯坦特关于善意谎言的经典争论，并推广到一般的断言性言语行为评估。

Method: 构建形式化语言与推理规则以描述代理人信念与言语行为的伦理后果；在Answer Set Programming中编码规则与评价准则；用萨特《墙》中的说谎场景及两种变体作为案例分析，展示通过调整参数可适配新情境。

Result: 得到一个可执行的ASP框架，能够对具体情境下不同伦理理论下的发话选项给出形式化判断；示例显示在不同参数设置下说谎可能既有救命也有致死的不同后果；框架声称具有领域可移植性。

Conclusion: 论文提出了基于主体信念的言语行为伦理评估逻辑框架，能够在义务论、后果论和原则论三种伦理立场下对发话进行判定，并用ASP实现。

Abstract: In life-or-death situations, a benevolent lie may appear more moral than telling the truth. Yet such lies can backfire, producing unintended and sometimes fatal consequences. This tension, famously disputed by Kant and Constant in 1797, applies not only to lying but to assertive speech acts in general, raising the question of which utterance should be chosen when moral stakes are high. We present a logical framework for the ethical evaluation of speech-act utterances based on agents' beliefs. Implemented in Answer Set Programming (ASP), the framework assesses utterances under deontologism, consequentialism, and principialism, and is illustrated on Sartre's The Wall (1939), a reworking of that controversy in which lying leads alternately to rescue and to death. Our setting is general by design: as two variants show, accommodating a new moral situation amounts to adjusting parameters, not rules.

</details>


### [92] [Agentic discovery of blood biomarker from distilled private health records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04749)
*Seffi Cohen,Liat Antwarg Friedman,Amir Anisman,Ruth Johnson,Michelle M. Li,Ayush Noori,Ben Reis,Ran Balicer,Noa Dagan,Marinka Zitnik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过仅发布在封闭数据上训练的GAT权重，构建了一个可供智能代理在不接触患者原始数据的情况下评价和优化CBC候选生物标志物的评分器，外部验证显示了稳健的AUC提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 常规CBC含有潜在新型生物标志物，但受隐私保护限制，原始医疗记录无法直接与先进语言模型代理共享；目标是把由大量真实患者数据学到的证据以不泄露个人隐私的形式提供给发现代理。

Method: 在数据边界内对每个13种免疫介导疾病训练图注意力网络（GAT）以预测候选CBC表达式的病例对照AUC，并仅公开训练好的权重；代理使用“提出-评分-改进”循环、基于该评分器对候选表达式进行重排与优化。

Result: 代理在外部验证中，将起始文献种子表达式中位AUC提高了4.18个百分点；在三个独立队列中，重排三种前沿研究工具的候选项在大多数比较中优于其首选结果，但不同队列增益存在差异。

Conclusion: 发布的评分器在不暴露患者原始数据的前提下，支持基于CBC的隐私保护型生物标志物假设生成，且在外部队列中经代理发现的表达式在AUC上有可测量的提升。

Abstract: Routine complete blood counts (CBCs) could yield new biomarkers, but the private records needed to evaluate candidates cannot be shared with frontier language model agents that excel at discovery. We distilled the evidence held in the Clalit Health Services panel of over 5.4 million patients into a released scoring tool: for each of 13 immune-mediated diseases, a graph attention network was trained inside the data boundary to predict the case-control AUC of candidate CBC expressions, and only the trained weights were released. The tool grounds an agent's propose-score-refine loop in real-world data without exposing any patient data. In external validation, agent-discovered expressions improved on their literature-seeded starting points by a median of 4.18 AUC percentage points, and across three independent cohorts, reranking the candidates of three frontier research tools improved on their first choices in most comparisons, with gains that varied by cohort. The released scorer supports privacy-preserving biomarker hypothesis generation.

</details>


### [93] [Dynamic Routing as a New Dimension for Test-time Versatility of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04751)
*Michal Štefánik,Marek Kadlčík,Josef Kuchař,Michal Spiegel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 从少量示例用置信度挑选动态路由，在无梯度下能稳定改进与CoT互补的推理性能，尤其在外分布任务，但仍受后训练选择困难与路由引发的分布偏移限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不增加参数或数据并且不做梯度更新的前提下，为大模型开辟除CoT之外的另一个可在测试时快速适配的新自由度，以提升模型在新任务和外分布场景下的泛化与效率。

Method: 在多种后训练模型上，从3或10个示例出发，使用基于模型对示例标签赋概率并用模型置信度仲裁的零训练（无梯度）程序选择策略，快速识别动态路由程序；在49个MMLU任务及若干外分布任务（如ARC-AGI）上评估性能，并分析早期预训练信号与路由引起的残差流分布偏移对鲁棒性的影响。

Result: 基于置信度的路由选择在平均上提升了MMLU准确率，在4/7模型上有显著收益，并在ARC-AGI等外分布任务上对小模型（7B）带来翻倍准确率提升；路由与CoT在不同查询上成功互补，两者组合优于单独CoT；但置信度选择无法充分利用早期预训练中存在的路由潜力，且不成功的路由会把残差流驱出后续层的期望分布，降低鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 动态路由可作为大型语言模型在测试时适配的第二条轴线，补充链式思维（CoT）；通过置信度仲裁从少量示例快速挑选路由程序能带来稳定的准确性与效率提升，但现有置信度选择策略未能挖掘全部潜力，尤其在后训练阶段选择困难和路由带来的残差流失配分布导致鲁棒性问题。

Abstract: Beyond scaling their parameters and data, large language models currently gain versatility on new problems along a single axis: the tokens they spend on chain-of-thought (CoT). We investigate whether dynamic routing programs, which execute a subset of the model's layers or iterate some of them, can open a second axis of test-time adaptation, complementary to CoT and free of any gradient update.
