We formalize the pipeline, define a companion Cross-Model Semantic Drift / Emotional Consistency Score benchmark (CMSC-E), and report results from a three-arm pilot using 176 synthetic memory objects and three downstream model families. We find an uncorrected improvement in cross-model emotional consistency for fully canonicalized memory relative to raw memory (+0.050, 95% bootstrap CI [0.013, 0.086], paired t-test p = 0.010), but this result does not survive Bonferroni, Holm, or Benjamini-Hochberg correction across the six comparisons tested. None of the factual-drift (CMSD) comparisons reach significance at any correction level. We report these results as exploratory rather than confirmatory and outline needed follow-up work, including larger samples, independent judge models, human-validated rendering, and preregistration.

</details>


### [124] [AutoSciBench: Autonomous Benchmark Generation for Evaluating Scientific Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05140)
*Dongki Kim,Namkyeong Lee,Surag Nair,Carl Edwards,Xiner Li,Edward De Brouwer,Jenna Lynn Collier,Sung Ju Hwang,Gabriele Scalia,Ehsan Hajiramezanali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoSciBench 通过 concept+recipe 表示和基于 solver 轨迹的自适应修订，能自动生成更具挑战性与更高质量的科学评测任务，使评测随 agent 能力演进保持有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统科学基准容易饱和且难以频繁更新：手动构建与维护耗时且需专业知识，导致评测滞后于 agent 能力提升。研究目标是探索能否自动生成并随能力演进自适应的科学评测基准。

Method: 提出任务表示为两层结构：高层 concept（科学领域、数据模态、所需推理类 型）和低层 recipe（问题/环境/答案构造与验证方法）。让 agents 解题并产生 solver 轨迹与 judge 反馈，利用这些信号修订 recipe 或 concept，以闭合捷径并把任务推进到原始数据重审、中间结果解释与证据整合方向。对已完成的 refinement 轨迹进行经验蒸馏以指导新 concept 生成。

Result: 在计算生物学、材料科学、临床影像三个领域的实证中，从现有基准出发的自动生成任务将 solver 平均准确率在计算生物学和材料科学中分别降低约 22.4 与 25.5 个百分点；新生成任务在所有三个领域的平均质量评分更高，表明生成流程既提高了难度也提升了任务质量。

Conclusion: AutoSciBench 能够自动生成并迭代适配科学领域的 agent 基准，使基准随 agent 能力演进而保持区分度，降低了 solver 在新生成任务上的准确率并提高任务质量评分，表明其在多学科场景下能有效揭示残余失败模式。

Abstract: As agents rapidly evolve, existing benchmarks can become saturated, limiting their ability to distinguish capabilities and reveal remaining failure modes. Particularly in scientific domains, constructing and updating benchmarks requires substantial time, labor, and domain expertise, making it difficult to keep evaluation aligned with advances in agent capabilities. We address this challenge by investigating whether scientific-agent benchmarks can be automatically generated and iteratively adapted as agent capabilities evolve. We introduce AutoSciBench, a framework that represents each task as a high-level concept specifying the scientific domain, data modality, and required reasoning approach, together with a low-level recipe specifying how the question, environment, and ground-truth answer are constructed and verified. Agents attempt to solve each task, producing solver trajectories and corresponding judge feedback which AutoSciBench uses to revise the recipe or concept, closing observed shortcuts and shifting tasks toward raw-data re-examination, interpretation of intermediate results, and evidence integration. Experience distilled from completed refinement trajectories further guides new concept generation, allowing lessons from earlier task refinement to inform subsequent benchmark construction. Starting from existing benchmarks, we evaluate AutoSciBench across computational biology, materials science, and clinical imaging. Generated benchmarks reduce average solver accuracy by 22.4 and 25.5 percentage points relative to the human-curated benchmarks in computational biology and materials science, respectively, while generated tasks receive higher average quality ratings across all three domains, suggesting that scientific-agent evaluation can adapt as agent capabilities advance.

</details>


### [125] [R1A-PC: Physics-Guided Electromagnetic Inversion of Three-Dimensional Human Point Clouds in Complex Static Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05144)
*Xudong Yuan,Ruyun Xu,Jingtai Yang,Xianzheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出R1A-PC：通过配对复场背景减法与频率平衡的Born伴随生成空间特征，结合两阶段有界坐标变形的解码器，能在复杂多径环境下高精度恢复2048点人体点云，消融验证物理引导与背景减法的重要性，并在模拟测试中显著优于TopNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂静态环境中，墙面/地面等引发的强多径响应掩盖了目标弱扰动，传统方法难以准确恢复三维人物几何，因而需要结合物理先验与配对测量以增强目标信号并利用环境信息改善重建。

Method: R1A-PC以有目标与无目标的配对复场为输入，先做复场背景减法以突出目标引起的振幅与相位变化，同时保留背景场作为环境条件；利用频率平衡的离散Born伴随（adjoint）生成三维空间知识图；解码器在两阶段有界坐标变形流程中，按当前点坐标多尺度查询并融合测量特征、背景特征与物理特征，第二阶段在第一阶段坐标更新后再次查询以精细调整点云。论文还分析了配对残差、原始伴随的加权法向算子结构及预测点云的可行域。

Result: 在固定采集几何且由全波模拟生成的未见背景上，R1A-PC对2048点点云的重建在0.05m阈值下取得平方Chamfer距离0.001434 m^2、F-score 0.96308。相比TopNet，Chamfer下降70.28%；去掉物理引导或背景减法会分别使Chamfer上升242.06%或241.18%。跨背景布局与姿态实验表明配对减法与位置相关伴随特征具有互补作用。

Conclusion: 本文提出的R1A-PC能在复杂静态环境下从配对的复场测量中有效恢复三维人体点云，通过物理引导的特征融合和双阶段有界变形实现高精度重建。配对背景减法和基于频率平衡的离散Born伴随算子是提高抗多径干扰能力和位置相关性特征表达的关键。消融实验证明物理引导与背景减法对性能至关重要。

Abstract: Recovering three-dimensional human geometry from electromagnetic measure?ments in a complex static environment is difficult because strong multipath responses from walls, floors, and other objects obscure the weak target per?turbation. We propose R1A-PC, a physics-guided method that reconstructs a 2048-point human cloud from paired complex fields measured with and without the target. Complex background subtraction emphasizes target-induced ampli?tude and phase changes, while the background field remains available as an environmental condition. A frequency-balanced discrete Born adjoint produces a three-dimensional spatial knowledge map. At each of two bounded deformation stages, the decoder combines complex measurement features, background fea?tures, and multiscale physical features queried at the current point coordinates; the second stage queries again after the first coordinate update. We analyze the residual of paired subtraction, the weighted normal-operator structure of the raw adjoint, and the feasible set of the predicted cloud. In a held-out background generated by full-wave simulation under a fixed acquisition geometry, R1A-PC obtains a squared Chamfer distance of 0.001434 m2 and an F-score of 0.963080 at 0.05 m. Compared with TopNet, the Chamfer distance decreases by 70.28%. Removing physical guidance or background subtraction increases the Chamfer distance by 242.06% or 241.18%, respectively. Experiments across background layouts and poses support the complementary roles of paired subtraction and position-dependent adjoint features.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond Instruction Following: Learning Grounded Skill-Following with Skill Contracts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05161)
*Jianghan Shen,Zhenjie Liu,Yue Li,Jie Huang,Siqi Luo,Yiming Cheng,Yizhi Yao,Kaijie Zhang,Cheng Tang,Minghui Zhang,Ming Hu,Yirong Chen,Ziyan Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 将专家技能形式化为带运行时的‘技能合约’，通过里程碑奖励与合约状态反馈提供稠密、可验证的程序执行训练信号，从而实现接近100%的阶段完成率并保持或略微提升任务结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指令遵循主要针对离散、响应级要求，而专家级技能通常是跨多阶段和环境交互的程序性要求；需要一种能在多个阶段上、以环境观测为依据保证可验证执行的训练与评估方法。

Method: 提出 Grounded Skill-Following：以“技能合约”形式表达专家技能（可见说明 + 明确的合约运行时），运行时定义必须阶段、许可动作、允许转移和接受终止；引入 Verified Progress Credit（里程碑首次完成即奖励）将阶段性完成奖励聚合至轨迹回报；在 rollout 中由合约运行时持续跟踪状态并提供 Contract-State Feedback（表明动作是否被接受并指引下一步）以引导策略。使用 PCR（Protocol Completion Rate）作为程序合规性度量并与任务结果解耦。

Result: 在联合训练下，Qwen3.5-4B 在 Math 数据集上 PCR=99.27%、Task Outcome=82.95%；在 Search 数据集上 PCR=99.96%、Task Outcome=46.61%。并通过受控试验分析了技能说明、训练信号与合约状态反馈对 PCR 与任务结果的影响，以及在隐藏部分信息下的行为依赖性。

Conclusion: 基于合约运行时的技能执行框架能将多阶段、可验证的程序性要求转化为稠密训练信号，从而显著提升代理的阶段级完成率（PCR），并在不牺牲甚至小幅提升任务最终结果的情况下保证过程合规性。

Abstract: Instruction following typically enforces discrete, response-level requirements, whereas an expert-authored skill prescribes procedural requirements spanning multiple phases and environment interactions. Given such a skill, we train the executor to execute all required phases instead of focusing solely on the final answer. We therefore introduce Grounded Skill-Following, which requires an agent to execute a fixed, expert-authored skill across its required phases by grounding decisions in environment observations. To achieve verifiable procedural execution, we formulate each skill as a skill contract combining visible skill instructions with an explicit contract runtime. The runtime specifies required phases, admissible actions, permitted transitions, and accepted termination. This structure provides a dense, verifiable training signal throughout execution. We leverage this by introducing Verified Progress Credit, which assigns rewards upon the initial completion of contract milestones and aggregates them into the trajectory return to guide policy optimization. During rollout, the contract runtime continuously tracks state transitions to provide Contract-State Feedback, which indicates whether the latest action is accepted and guides the agent toward valid next actions. To measure procedural compliance, we introduce the Protocol Completion Rate (PCR), defined as reaching accepted termination through all required phases, and decouple it from the final Task Outcome. Jointly trained with our framework, Qwen3.5-4B achieves Protocol Completion Rates of 99.27% on Math and 99.96% on Search, while slightly outperforming original baselines in Task Outcome (82.95% and 46.61%, respectively). Controlled studies examine how skill instructions, training signals, and contract-state feedback affect both metrics, while withholding interventions evaluate behavioral dependence on observation content.

</details>


### [127] [Memadapter: Counterfactual Adaptation Against Memory-induced Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05162)
*Ruqing Ning,Haibo Meng,Zhishang Xiang,Zerui Chen,Jinsong Su,Xin Wang,Qinggang Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MemAdapter——通过反事实诱导、情境反思与证据化推理自适应整合记忆，缓解记忆引发的拍马并提高长期记忆的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期记忆可支持个性化与长时交互，但会导致代理程序对用户历史信念过度对齐（拍马），既往通过过滤错误记忆的做法不足以应对：即便是客观正确的记忆在不同情境下也可能引发不当的从众或偏向，因此需要一种能根据上下文自适应调节记忆影响力的机制。

Method: 框架包含三部分：1) 反事实诱导（Counterfactual Induction）：通过构造或推理“如果记忆不成立/不同”情形来揭示记忆可能带来的风险；2) 情境感知反思（Context-Aware Reflection）：在当前任务上下文中自我反思并调节每条检索到记忆的推理影响力；3) 基于证据推理（Evidence-Based Reasoning）：以恰当证据为基础生成最终回答，同时保留记忆的合法影响。

Result: 在三个基准数据集上进行广泛实验证明，MemAdapter在不同场景中持续提升记忆可靠性；论文并开源了实现代码。

Conclusion: MemAdapter通过自适应整合检索到的记忆、引入反事实诱导、情境感知反思与基于证据的推理，有效缓解了记忆引发的拍马行为，提升了长期记忆在多场景下的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-term memory enables LLM-based agents to retain and reuse information across tasks and sessions, supporting personalization and long-horizon interactions. However, persistent memories can also induce sycophancy, causing agents to over-align with users' historical beliefs even when they are inaccurate, outdated, or inconsistent with objective evidence. Existing mitigation methods assume that memory-induced sycophancy originates from biased or incorrect memories and attempt to reduce this risk by filtering such memories at different stages of the memory pipeline. However, in the real world, objective and correct memories can still induce sycophancy, and the same memory can warrant different influence across different contexts. To this end, we propose MemAdapter, a novel framework that adaptively integrates retrieved memories to support objective and reliable reasoning. Specifically, MemAdapter consists of three components: (i) Counterfactual Induction, which leverages counterfactual reasoning to uncover the potential risk of retrieved memories; (ii) Context-Aware Reflection, which calibrates the inferential influence of each retrieved memory in light of the current task via self-reflection; and (iii) Evidence-Based Reasoning, which grounds the final response in appropriate evidence while preserving the legitimate influence of memory. Extensive experiments on three benchmarks demonstrate that MemAdapter consistently improves memory reliability across diverse scenarios. Our code is available at https://github.com/DEEP-JLU/MemAdapter.

</details>


### [128] [A Safe Action Is Not Enough: Feasible-Future Decoding for Vision-Language-Action Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05166)
*Tu Nguyen,Matthieu Zimmer,Vu Anh Vu,Ziyi Wang,Jannik Hammel Nielsen,Xuebing Zhou,Haitham Bou Ammar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出并分析了可衡量动作后续安全可行性的理论量（feasible-future mass），在此基础上给出无需重训练的有限候选重排序器VICS-G，在保证成功率与效率的前提下显著降低安全成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单步或局部的高概率/可接受动作并不能保证后续仍有路径安全完成任务——需要一个度量来量化在执行某动作后仍被基策略支持的安全完成质量，从而弥补单纯按似然排序带来的风险。

Method: 解析性地推导了历史条件下策略-环境轨迹律在‘以安全完成为约束’条件下的下一步边缘分布，揭示了‘候选相关的可行未来质量（feasible-future mass）’的概念；由于精确计算在线不可行，提出了选择性有限候选近似，并给出了恢复最佳可行候选的充分条件；最终实现为一个告警触发的、无需训练的重排序器（reranker）。

Result: 定义并计算了可行未来质量的解析表达，设计了计算可行性-似然差的近似算法并证明了相关恢复条件；在Safety-CHORES基准上，VICS-G将平均累积安全成本降低1.9%–57.5%，同时任务成功率在基策略采样之内2.5个百分点内，平均集数长度增加不超过0.82步。

Conclusion: 通过定义并闭合‘可行性-似然差’（feasibility-likelihood gap），该工作提出了一个与安全完成任务的未来质量直接相关的策略重排序方法。实验表明，在不重训练基策略且不做在线滚动的条件下，所提出的VICS-G解码器在大多数设置中显著降低了累积安全成本，同时保持任务成功率和步数接近基策略。

Abstract: A safe action is not necessarily a viable one. Under a frozen vision-language-action (VLA) policy, an action can be likely and locally admissible yet leave no policy-supported route to safe task completion. We call this the feasibility-likelihood gap: likelihood ranks the current action, whereas feasibility depends on the futures that remain after it.
  We derive the exact next-block marginal of the history-conditioned policy-environment trajectory law restricted to safe task completion. The derivation exposes a candidate-dependent feasible-future mass with two roles: its support records whether safe completion remains possible under the frozen continuation process, and its magnitude measures how much weighted safe-completion mass is preserved. Exact evaluation is impractical online, so we develop a selective finite-candidate approximation, derive conditions for recovering the best retained viable candidate, and instantiate it as an alarm-triggered, training-free reranker.
  On Safety-CHORES, VICS-G lowers mean cumulative safety cost by 1.9%-57.5% across six settings while remaining within 2.5 percentage points of policy sampling in success and 0.82 steps in mean episode length. The resulting decoder is tied to an exact policy-relative safe-completion target, yet requires neither retraining of the base policy nor online trajectory rollouts.

</details>


### [129] [CreativeFlow: A One-to-Many Analogical Relation Transfer Method for 3D Asset Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05167)
*Xuechen Li,Shuai Zhang,Nanxuan Zhao,Qing Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 一个基于类比发散思维的文本到3D生成框架，产出关系一致但几何多样的资产对，能显著提升创意多样性并附带数据集与基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受认知科学中类比与发散思维启发，目标是打破当前文本到3D生成中易产生的样式与构型同质化，提升创意多样性和视觉趣味性。

Method: 提出一个类比驱动的生成框架：从语义/关系空间推导出多组有意义且关系相似的源-目标配对，每对保持语义关系一致但在几何形状上发生系统性变换，集成进现有文本到3D管线以生成多样化候选。

Result: 生成了一组具有关系连贯性但几何多样性的3D资产，专家评分展示了显著提升的新颖性与视觉吸引力，并发布了相应数据集与基准以促进后续研究。

Conclusion: CREATIVEFLOW通过显式建模类比性发散思维，在文本到三维生成管线中有效缓解了创意同质化问题，生成了一系列在关系上相似但几何配置各异的源-目标资产对，并通过专家评估证明提高了新颖性与视觉吸引力，同时构建了面向关联感知3D模型训练的数据集与基准。

Abstract: Inspired by cognitive science, we present CREATIVEFLOW, an analogical generation framework that explicitly models analogical divergent thinking to mitigate creative homogenization in text-to-3D pipelines. Our method derives a series of meaningful yet relationally similar source-target asset pairs, each featuring distinct geometric configurations. Expert evaluations demonstrate that our framework substantially enhances creative novelty and visual fascination. This workflow and its resulting assets establish a foundational dataset and benchmark for future relation-aware 3D model training.

</details>


### [130] [AECG: Asymmetric Experience Consolidation and Governance In Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05176)
*Ao Tian,Jialong Liu,Daqi Zheng,Xin Sun,Mengting Li,Zhizhao Xiao,Zijian Huang,Honglei Wang,Zijian Hei,Yukun Yan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AECG：一个保持作用域并基于置信度与下游影响优先审查高风险记忆的动态治理框架，通过有针对性的干预和配对回放显著缓解记忆污染和作用域崩溃，在多套基准上获得显著改进（代码开源）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的多智能体系统依赖可重用的程序化记忆，但重复检索使错误记忆稳定化（记忆污染），且多智能体协作带来作用域崩溃——在不兼容决策层面重复使用某项记忆，从而导致错误级联；任务级失败难以提供精确监督，需一种主动、可审计的记忆治理机制。

Method: 提出一套经验整合与治理框架（AECG）：1) 保存并维护知识的协调作用域(scope preservation)；2) 基于多尺度置信度估计检测记忆可靠性衰退；3) 将可靠性衰退与下游影响联合用于在有界复审预算下优先挑选高风险记忆；4) 对选中条目施加针对性干预并通过配对回放（paired replay）在复核通过后重新激活技能。

Result: 在三种多智能体框架与四个基准上，AECG在12个框架-基准设置中取得11项最好成绩，相比最强竞争记忆方法最高提升10.23个百分点；去除作用域保持会使准确率下降最多16.89个百分点。代码开源于作者仓库。

Conclusion: AECG将多智能体系统中的记忆管理从被动积累转为主动的可信度治理循环，通过保持协调作用域、基于置信度的多尺度可靠性检测、结合衰变与下游影响进行优先复审、有针对性的干预与配对重放，显著降低记忆污染与作用域崩溃导致的连锁错误。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems increasingly rely on memory to transform execution trajectories into reusable procedural knowledge. Yet repeated retrieval also makes memory errors persistent: memory pollution arises when outdated, weakly supported, or spuriously successful procedures become recurring components of future reasoning. Multi-agent execution introduces an additional structural risk. Scope collapse occurs when procedural knowledge escapes the coordination scope in which it was shown effective and is repeatedly reused at incompatible decision levels, allowing local errors to influence cascades of downstream decisions. Meanwhile, task-level failures provide ambiguous supervision because they rarely reveal which recalled knowledge was responsible. We introduce AECG, a framework for asymmetric experience consolidation and governance for multi-agent systems. AECG turns memory from static experience storage into a dynamic reliability-governance loop, preserving coordination scope and using multi-scale, confidence-aware reliability to detect degradation. It then combines degradation with downstream impact to prioritize high-risk knowledge under a bounded review budget, applies targeted interventions, and reactivates revised skills only after paired replay. Across three multi-agent frameworks and four benchmarks, AECG achieves the best score in 11 of 12 framework--benchmark settings and improves over the strongest competing memory method by as much as 10.23 percentage points; removing scope preservation reduces accuracy by up to 16.89 points. AECG thereby reframes multi-agent memory from passive accumulation into auditable reliability governance. Code is available at https://github.com/fenhg297/AECG

</details>


### [131] [From Scientific Observations to Mechanisms: Benchmarking Hypothesis Generation by AI Scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05197)
*Xiaxun Xie,Qingqing Long,Meng Xiao,Wei Ju,Yuanchun Zhou,Xuezhi Wang,Hengshu Zhu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MechHypoBench 提出首个用于评估 AI 将经验数据转化为机械机制假说的基准：结合真实数据与文献机制并通过保留条件下的后果评估。实验证明现有代理与 AI 科学家在生成与真实机制一致的假说方面存在显著缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究多聚焦于统计预测或数据分析工具，但缺乏系统化评估 AI 能否把经验发现上升为可解释的机制性假说——这是科学发现的核心。需要一个保留真实观测复杂性同时有明确定义“地面真相”的基准。

Method: 作者构建了 MechHypoBench：从 14 个科学领域收集文献中描述的机制，配套真实世界的数据集（共 1,798 万条记录），在保持观测复杂性的同时明确底层机制。任务要求代理基于观测提出开放式机制假说，评估框架通过考察这些假说在被保留的条件下的后果来判定其正确性。进行了以通用代理和专用 AI 科学家为被试的实验与比较。

Result: 基准与评估框架成功揭示：多数自动生成的开放式假说与文献给定的底层机制在推断后果上不一致，表明当前方法在机制建模、因果归纳与跨条件泛化方面不足。该基准可用于定量比较方法并推动机制推断能力的改进。

Conclusion: MechHypoBench 实验证明现有通用 AI 代理与“AI 科学家”在从经验数据推导出真实机械机制的能力上存在显著差距，当前方法无法可靠生成对应真实机制的开放式因果/机制假说。

Abstract: Data-driven mechanistic hypotheses are essential to scientific discovery because they explain how underlying processes produce observed phenomena. AI agents and AI scientists increasingly support scientific data analysis. However, their ability to turn empirical findings into mechanistic hypotheses remains insufficiently examined. To address this gap, we introduce MechHypoBench, the first benchmark for evaluating whether AI agents and AI scientists can generate such hypotheses from empirical data. It combines paper-derived mechanisms from 14 scientific fields with real-world datasets containing 17.98 million records. The construction retains the observational complexity of empirical data while providing a specified underlying mechanism. Agents analyze the observations and propose open-form hypotheses. We develop an evaluation framework that assesses open-form mechanistic hypotheses through their consequences under withheld conditions. Experiments with general agents and AI scientists reveal a substantial gap between generated hypotheses and the underlying mechanisms.

</details>


### [132] [Image Synthesis as an Intermediate for Controllable Time Series Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05211)
*Haochen Yuan,Jing Xie,Yunbo Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用时序图作为视觉中介，结合多模态语义解析与强化学习式语义编辑，再通过时序VAE生成样本，可在few-shot预测中带来更有利的增强并提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接从语言生成数值序列难以精确且稳定地保持预期的时间结构，使用视觉中间体（时序图）可以更可靠地将高层时间语义落到具体数值上，从而改善few-shot预测的增广效果。

Method: 先将绘制的时序图输入多模态LLM以提取结构化时间语义（如趋势、季节性、波动性）；学习一个以下游预测奖励为目标的语义编辑策略以偏好有利的时序模式；最后用时序VAE将语义映射回一致的多变量数值序列，形成增强样本。

Result: 在标准公开预测基准上的实验证明，VisualBridge优于传统数据增强方法；消融实验显示可视语义定位、学习的语义控制策略和基于VAE的生成各自对最终效果均有显著贡献。

Conclusion: VisualBridge通过将时间序列可视化图像作为语义与数值序列之间的中间表征，并结合多模态大模型的语义解析、基于下游任务的语义编辑策略学习以及时序VAE的生成，能有效提高few-shot预测的增强数据质量，从而提升下游预测性能。

Abstract: Semantic-driven time-series generation offers a promising way to improve downstream learning in few-shot forecasting, but directly generating numerical sequences from language often fails to preserve the intended temporal structure. We propose VisualBridge, which uses time-series plots as a visual intermediate to bridge high-level temporal semantics and numerical sequences. An MLLM first converts plotted series into structured semantic representations, enabling explicit control over temporal properties such as trend, seasonality, and volatility. We then learn a semantic editing policy with downstream forecasting rewards, allowing the generation process to favor temporal patterns that are beneficial for the target task. The resulting sequences are further modeled by a temporal VAE to produce consistent multivariate augmentations. Experiments on standard public forecasting benchmarks demonstrate that VisualBridge improves few-shot forecasting over conventional augmentation methods, with ablations validating the roles of visual semantic grounding, learned semantic control, and VAE-based generation.

</details>


### [133] [Safe Context Switching for Agents in the Wild: Mitigating Subspace Interference via Orthogonal Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05219)
*Akash Das,Ishan Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对CoT推理与安全对齐之间的子空间干涉，作者提出AURA——一种估计对齐零空间并将推理更新限制在其正交补的谱正则化方法，能在不牺牲安全性的前提下提升推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到连贯的链式思维（CoT）所需的高方差内部态会与编码安全约束的潜在表示发生几何干涉（Sequential Subspace Interference），传统微调会导致对齐性能显著下降。

Method: 提出AURA（一种自适应唯一残差分配）——估计对齐流形的零空间并将推理更新约束到其正交补，通过谱独立性正则化使推理梯度与对齐目标在几何上互不干扰。

Result: 在多步数学与代码生成等逻辑任务上，标准微调造成约23.3%的对齐性能惩罚；AURA可恢复约23.0%的性能并保持高余弦相似度，说明谱正则化可缓解推理漂移问题。

Conclusion: 本文结论为：通过谱正则化（AURA），可在保持模型安全性的同时恢复逻辑推理能力，从而将推理与对齐有效解耦。AURA在实验中恢复了约23.0%的性能损失，并保持>0.98的余弦保真度。

Abstract: Most Large Language Models exhibit a fundamental tension between two sequential tasks, such as logical reasoning and safety alignment. The high-variance internal states required for sophisticated Chain-of-Thought (CoT) deduction can geometrically interfere with latent representations encoding safety constraints. We identify this phenomenon as Sequential Subspace Interference, showing that standard fine-tuning on logical tasks such as multi-step mathematics and code generation can result in a 23.3% interference penalty on alignment benchmarks, substantially weakening the model's safety priors. This Reasoning Drift is not adequately captured by current adaptation methods because gradients for logical tasks are rarely orthogonal to safety objectives. To address this issue, we propose AURA (Adaptive Unique Residual Allocation), a spectral regularization framework that enforces Spectral Independence between reasoning and safety. By explicitly estimating the null space of the alignment manifold and constraining reasoning updates to its orthogonal complement, AURA enables models to improve logical reasoning without compromising safety. Empirically, AURA recovers 23.0% of the lost performance while preserving greater than 0.98 cosine fidelity to the safe state, demonstrating that reasoning and alignment can be effectively decoupled through geometric regularization.

</details>


### [134] [Look Before You Leap: Thermodynamic Arbitration of Parametric and Non-Parametric Knowledge in LLM Agents via Self-Regulating Memory Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05223)
*Akash Das,Ishan Roy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARTA用元认知的熵估计来按需触发检索，旨在减少不必要的外部查询，提升效率与决策稳健性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前主流代理采用“始终检索”范式，导致模型对内隐知识不信任、频繁无谓检索（“诱发失忆”），造成资源浪费和对上下文噪声的敏感性。

Method: 提出MARTA：一种神经-符号框架，将检索视为有代价的动作。模型在内部生成思路并评估其熵/不确定性，只有在自评不足时才触发外部检索（有计划的、延迟的检索）。

Result: 论文提出一个概念与架构性解决方案：通过不确定性评估实现“先看再跳”的检索策略，从而在理论上实现更节能、鲁棒的决策流程。摘要未给出具体实证结果。

Conclusion: 提出通过赋予模型元认知能力，按需检索以在参数化内部知识与外部检索之间建立更高效的平衡，从而减少不必要检索带来的低效与脆弱性。

Abstract: The architecture of modern LLMs consists of a profound cognitive polarization. LLMs possess implicit intuition encoded in their parameters, yet rely on a disconnected, explicit mechanism to access the outside world. Agentic frameworks have not bridged this gap; instead, models are often compelled into pathological "induced amnesia." Under the prevailing "Retrieve-Always" paradigm, agents must distrust their internal knowledge, making every user interaction a "tabula rasa" event that must be checked externally. This creates reflexive dependence that can be thermodynamically wasteful, cognitively fragile, and susceptible to irrelevant context. We propose a return to first principles, operationalizing the biological maxim "Look Before You Leap." We introduce MARTA (Metacognitive Adaptive Retrieval and Thought Architecture), a neuro-symbolic framework that bridges parametric and non-parametric knowledge. Rather than treating retrieval as mandatory, MARTA models it as a cost, taking the leap only when perceived internal inadequacy warrants external information. By allowing the agent to gauge the entropy of its own thoughts before acting, MARTA enables deliberative retrieval and uncertainty-aware decision making. Our approach suggests that giving agents the capacity for introspection can restore a more efficient balance between internal knowledge and external information.

</details>


### [135] [GFGE: Unifying Explainable AI Methods through an Interpretation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05225)
*Jinfeng Zhong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: GFGE把XAI解释视为证据的解读与传达过程，使用五类角色、操作图与证据记录为不同方法建立统一、可追溯的描述与比较基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XAI方法来源与需求多样，缺乏统一的框架来说明何为可作为解释的“证据”、如何将其组织并传达给特定受众，以及如何追踪与比较不同方法的解释性主张。

Method: 提出Interpret/Explain Schema (IES)作为概念基础，并将其具体化为五类角色（数据解释、模型解释、输出解释、可选后验分析、聚合）；使用角色类型化的操作图记录方法间依赖，使用证据记录保存来源、假设与局限；以解释问题、受众与情境为约束指导过程；并对归因、替代模型、反事实、概念/样本原型、内在规则、论证与语言模型方法给出实例化。

Result: 提出并实现了GFGE框架，展示了如何用相同角色体系表示内在、后验与混合工作流；通过实例化揭示各类方法的不同证据要求；提供跟踪解释主张至其证据来源并识别未解决依赖的机制（操作图与证据记录）。

Conclusion: GFGE为XAI提供了一个统一的概念与操作框架，通过把“解释”视为从证据到受众的解读-传播-再解读过程，能够将不同方法的工作流与证据链条清晰对应，从而支持可追溯性、对比分析与未决依赖识别。

Abstract: Explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) encompasses methods that draw on different sources of information and address different explanatory needs. A common framework is needed to describe how this information becomes evidence and is communicated as an explanation for a particular recipient. We propose the General Framework for Generating Explanations (GFGE), grounded in interpretative frameworks and the complementary activities of \emph{sense-reading} and \emph{sense-giving}. Its conceptual foundation is the Interpret/Explain Schema (IES), which connects an analyst's interpretation of system evidence, the communication of a selected account, and the recipient's interpretation of that account. GFGE operationalises this schema through five roles: data interpretation, model interpretation, output interpretation, optional post-hoc analysis, and aggregation. A role-typed operation graph records method-specific dependencies, while evidence records retain the sources, assumptions, and limitations of explanatory claims. The explanatory question, audience, and context guide the procedure. We instantiate GFGE for attribution, surrogate, counterfactual, concept and prototype, intrinsic rule, argumentation, and language-model methods. These instantiations show how intrinsic, post-hoc, and hybrid workflows can be represented through the same roles while preserving their distinct evidential requirements. GFGE provides a common basis for analysing explanation workflows, tracing communicated claims to their evidence, and identifying unresolved explanatory dependencies.

</details>


### [136] [Learning from imperfect teachers for low-resource acoustic generalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05256)
*Shuanglin Li,Ruxiao Qian,Jian Liu,Haijun Lin,Wenwu Wang,Siyang Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 BA-MPD（包含 BAC 与 MPD），通过边界锚定的标签修正与质量分割的蒸馏损失，有效抑制教师噪声并提升低资源声学任务上的学生性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当教师在标注稀缺或类别不平衡下训练时，其软标签分布可能含有不均可靠的分量，直接对齐整个分布会把教师的偏差传递给学生，损害学生的决策边界与泛化性能。需要一种既能保留教师有用知识又能抑制有害偏差的蒸馏方法。

Method: 提出一项基于 logit 的蒸馏目标 BA-MPD，包括 Boundary-Anchored Correction (BAC) 与 Mass-Partitioned Distillation (MPD)。BAC 在教师预测的 top 集合中若缺少真实标签则将其替换最低排名项以保持集合总质量与不确定性；MPD 通过多个分离损失项：在集合内强制关系一致性、平衡高低置信组间质量分配，并对低置信依赖加权，从而有选择地蒸馏教师信息。

Result: 在两个声学基准及多种标注预算下，BA-MPD 相较于监督学习基线和传统 KD 均有稳定提升，并与强的 logit 蒸馏基线性能相当；跨预算实验显示在教师和学生标注预算不匹配时也能保持有效，证明其利用不完美教师的能力。实现代码已开源。

Conclusion: BA-MPD 能在低资源声学学习中有效缓解教师模型带来的偏差，通过对 logits 级别的修正与分段蒸馏同时保留有用信息并抑制有害排名错误，从而提升学生模型的泛化能力。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation (KD) improves low-resource acoustic learning by enriching one-hot supervision with the softened predictive distribution of a fixed teacher network. However, a teacher trained with limited or imbalanced annotations may produce a biased distribution whose components are not uniformly reliable. Although this distribution can still encode useful knowledge, direct full-distribution matching may also transfer teacher-induced biases, thereby distorting the student's decision boundary and degrading its generalization performance. To address this limitation, we propose Boundary-Anchored Mass-Partitioned Distillation (BA-MPD), a logit-based distillation objective composed of Boundary-Anchored Correction (BAC) and Mass-Partitioned Distillation (MPD). BAC addresses missing ground-truth labels in the set of the teacher's top predictions by swapping the true label for the lowest-ranked entry of the set, thus keeping the mass and uncertainty of the set unchanged. MPD then distills this corrected distribution through separate losses that enforce relational consistency within the set, balance the mass between high- and low-confidence groups, and weight lower-confidence dependencies. Ultimately, BAC and MPD together suppress harmful ranking errors and noisy low-confidence details, while retaining all useful teacher information. Experiments on two acoustic benchmarks under multiple label budgets show that BA-MPD consistently improves over supervised-learning baselines and vanilla KD while remaining competitive with strong logit-based KD baselines. Cross-budget results further show that BA-MPD remains effective when the teacher and student models use mismatched label budgets, demonstrating its ability to exploit imperfect teachers across supervision gaps. Implementation available at https://github.com/ShuanglinLi/BA-MPD.

</details>


### [137] [When Agent Context Goes Stale: Incoherence in Volatile Agent Context](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05281)
*Yingying Liu,Junzhou Fang,Chenxiong Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Concord 是一个将观测与其可变源关联并检测变更的上下文一致性框架，通过可配置策略避免使用陈旧观测，在构建基准上显著提高正确率并降低 token 成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代智能代理在推理时依赖来自工具的观测（如工作区文件内容），但这些底层数据源会被用户或外部工具修改，而模型上下文中保留的是陈旧内容；现有运行时缺乏机制通知模型观测已过期，从而导致错误的状态推断与行动。

Method: 设计并实现通用的 Concord 框架：记录观测与来源的映射；周期性或事件驱动地检测来源变化；提供可配置的处理策略（更新、标注、抑制等）在重用前调整上下文；并在具体运行时-资源设置下做出实例化。构建 ConcordBench 基准，在文件被编辑后评估三款前沿模型的表现，比较 Concord、若干基线与 Oracle 的恢复能力与代价（token 使用）。

Result: 在 ConcordBench 设定下，Concord 在三款评估模型上对恢复到最新工作区状态的回答在所有评测案例中均保持一致；在“恢复计数”上与 Oracle 持平，同时比最强非 Oracle 基线节省 46.4% 的 token 使用。

Conclusion: Concord 提出并验证了一个保持代理上下文观测与可变数据源一致性的框架，通过将每条观测与源关联、检测源变更并采用可配置策略处理陈旧上下文，从而避免模型因使用过时观测而产生错误陈述。

Abstract: Modern agents increasingly ground their reasoning in observations returned by tools, such as file contents read from a workspace. However, the data sources underlying these observations may later be modified by users, other agents, or external tools, while the model retains only the stale content in its context window. Existing agent runtimes provide little support for notifying the model that a previously observed fact has become stale, causing agents to reuse outdated observations and make incorrect claims about the current workspace state. We propose Concord, a context coherence framework that maintains the consistency between tool observation in agent context and the mutable sources from which they were derived. Concord links each observation to its source, detects source changes, and uses configurable handling policies to update, annotate, or suppress stale context before reuse. Concord is applicable across different agent runtimes and external resources, and can be easily extended to new runtime-resource settings. We implement Concord as a general framework, and instantiate a concrete use case to assess its effectiveness. We construct ConcordBench, where previously observed file contents become stale after subsequent edits. Across three evaluated frontier models, Concord produces answers consistent with the restored workspace state in all evaluated cases under these constructed conditions, matching the oracle on recover count for this benchmark, while using 46.4% fewer tokens than the strongest non-oracle baseline.

</details>


### [138] [Fusion is the New Mutation: Bandit-Guided Evolution on Workflow Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05284)
*Zhiwei Shang,Jiahang Sun,Mingrong Gong,Mingze Kong,Zikun Qu,Pingchen Lu,Junhao Dong,Zhipiao Liu,Hongwei Yang,Guoqing Xie,Yao Shu,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: DAGO uses contextual bandits (diagonal LinUCB) over embeddings of parent sets plus LLM fusion and a shared DAG to efficiently discover better workflows under limited evaluation budgets, yielding modest but consistent gains and lower search cost.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Evaluation of agentic workflows is expensive, and naive search over multi-parent fusions wastes budget; learning from fusion feedback to prioritize promising parent combinations can improve sample efficiency.

Method: Formulate each multi-parent fusion candidate as a bandit arm encoded via pretrained embeddings of parent workflows' code and prompts; use a diagonal LinUCB contextual-bandit policy to learn a shared linear reward model and select arms balancing predicted offspring quality and uncertainty; generate child workflows with an LLM using summary-guided fusion; update bandit using child's validation score and record lineage in a growing DAG.

Result: Across six benchmarks (math reasoning, code generation, QA), DAGO attains highest macro-average score among baselines, improves over AFlow from 80.3 to 81.7 under matched budgets, and cuts aggregate search cost by 11.2%. Ablations show LinUCB selection outperforms random and greedy variants, highlighting benefit of feedback-driven exploration.

Conclusion: DAGO effectively balances reuse and exploration for workflow synthesis by modeling parent-combination selection as a contextual bandit problem and maintaining a shared DAG of discovered workflows; empirical results show improved macro-average performance and reduced search cost compared to baselines.

Abstract: Automated agentic workflow optimization relies on costly evaluations, making it essential to allocate a limited evaluation budget effectively. Multi-parent fusion can reuse designs from previously discovered workflows, but identifying promising parent combinations requires learning from limited fusion feedback. We introduce DAGO (Directed Acyclic Graph Optimization), a contextual-bandit-guided framework that learns which parent workflows to fuse under a limited evaluation budget. DAGO formulates each candidate parent combination as an arm, represented by pretrained embeddings of its constituent workflows' code and prompts. A diagonal LinUCB policy learns a shared reward model across arms and balances exploitation of arms with high predicted offspring quality against uncertainty-driven exploration. After an arm is selected, an LLM generates a child workflow through summary-guided fusion, and the child's validation score serves as the reward for updating the bandit. A shared directed acyclic graph maintains discovered workflows and their multi-parent lineage, providing an expanding pool of parents for subsequent arm proposals. Across six benchmarks covering mathematical reasoning, code generation, and question answering, DAGO achieves the highest macro-average score among the evaluated baselines. Under matched validation-evaluation budgets, it improves over AFlow from 80.3 to 81.7 while reducing aggregate search expenditure by 11.2%. Ablation studies show that LinUCB-guided arm selection outperforms both random selection and its exploration-free variant, supporting the value of feedback-driven selection and exploration-exploitation balance.

</details>


### [139] [Readable Before Actionable: Causal Tracing of Indirect Prompt Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05295)
*Zhe Yu,Wenpeng Xing,Xingxing Yang,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 可读的“角色”信号与能改变行为的干预方向是不同的：解码存在但不等于可控干预，深度（层）、位置与上下文决定干预效果；对抗/防御需要在层与位置上做更有针对性的操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 间接提示注入（indirect prompt injection）使 LLM 代理遵从外部数据中的指令，但简单的可读探针可能只识别指令而未定位能改变下一步行为的状态编辑。论文旨在研究可解码信号与因果行为干预之间的差距。

Method: 使用反事实角色探针（role probes）、分量级激活打补丁（component-wise activation patching），以及在 AgentDojo 轨迹上分别施加干预；在 Qwen 上做可控工具选择实验，估计独立的“角色方向”（role direction），并在不同层、不同位置、单点与跨度、多次编辑、与通道子空间移除等设置下评估效果。

Result: 发现可读的角色解码在内容与格式变换下仍能保持；在 Qwen 中，解码能力先于沿独立估计的角色方向打补丁导致的明显工具选择改变；在 AgentDojo 中，从被劫持与抵抗轨迹估计的方向能在注入跨度和动作前位置减少攻击成功，但对随机位置影响甚微；随轨迹变长，单位置编辑在后层效果减弱，跨度或重复编辑能进一步降低攻击成功；移除学到的通道子空间不影响角色解码，但有效干预方向跨通道迁移性差。

Conclusion: 论文区分了可解码的“角色”信号与能有效改变行为的干预方向，指出两者在层深、位置和通道上的依赖不同。

Abstract: Indirect prompt injection causes LLM agents to follow commands embedded in external data. A probe may distinguish instructions from data without identifying a state edit that changes the next action. We study this gap through counterfactual role probes, component-wise activation patching, and separate interventions on AgentDojo trajectories. Role decoding survives changes in content and format. In controlled Qwen tests, it precedes strong tool-choice effects from patches along an independently estimated role direction. On AgentDojo, directions estimated from hijacked and resisted training trajectories reduce attack success at pre-action and injected-span positions, but have little effect at random positions. In longer Qwen trajectories, single-position edits become less effective at later layers; span-wide and repeated edits reduce attack success on the same evaluation set. Removing the learned channel subspace preserves role decoding, yet effective intervention directions transfer poorly across the tested channels. These findings distinguish a readable role signal from an effective behavioral intervention: depth matters in controlled tool choice, while position and context also matter in attack trajectories.

</details>


### [140] [MESH-Harness: Self-Improving Agent Harnesses via Bandit-Guided Compositional Evolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05300)
*Zhiwei Shang,Yu Huo,Mingrong Gong,An Yan,Zikun Qu,Junhao Dong,Bryan Kian Hsiang Low,Chenglin Wu,Zhongxiang Dai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 通过模块化复用与反馈驱动的组合搜索，MESH-Harness 在有限预算下有效改进 agent harness，带来显著性能提升与成本节约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不改变语言模型权重且评估预算受限的情况下，通过改进围绕模型的组织与工具协同（即 harness），提升下游任务性能并控制优化成本。

Method: 将 harness 划分为具有明确角色接口的功能模块，维护角色专用的实现池；用共享模块向量表示与全协方差 LinUCB 预测并打分候选组合的表现与探索价值；用混合起点坐标上升（mixed-start coordinate ascent）在组合空间中直接选择完整配置；用验证轨迹驱动本地代码编辑并将新实现加入固定容量的角色池以供后续重组。

Result: 在四类文本任务上（检索增强的数学推理、代码生成与交互式科学任务等），与 Meta-Harness 在等同候选评估预算下对比，分别提升约 5.70、7.01、2.00、5.00 个点；迭代优化相比一轮配置再提升 5.63–7.79 点；报告配置下测试时成本降低 44.2%，含搜索的总成本降低 14.6%。

Conclusion: MESH-Harness 通过模块化设计、共享模块表示与基于全协方差 LinUCB 的组合评分，以及混合起点坐标上升和基于验证轨迹的本地代码编辑，实现了在固定模型权重下、有限评估预算内系统性地改进 agent harness，兼顾性能提升与成本控制。

Abstract: An agent harness is the code that organizes context, maintains state, and coordinates tool calls for a language model. We study how to improve the harness under a limited evaluation budget while keeping model weights fixed. Our method, MESH-Harness, organizes each harness into functional modules with explicit role-specific interfaces, allowing alternative implementations of each module to be substituted and recombined. It uses shared module representations and full-covariance LinUCB to score candidate combinations based on predicted performance and exploration value. Mixed-start coordinate ascent selects complete configurations for evaluation without enumerating the combinatorial space. Validation traces then guide local code edits, and the resulting candidates are incorporated into fixed-capacity role-specific pools for subsequent recombination. On text tasks, retrieval-augmented mathematical reasoning, code generation, and interactive scientific tasks, MESH-Harness outperforms Meta-Harness by 5.70, 7.01, 2.00, and 5.00 points, respectively, under matched candidate-evaluation budgets. Iterative harness optimization improves MESH-Harness by 5.63-7.79 points over its first-round configurations. For the reported configurations, aggregate test-time cost is 44.2% lower than that of Meta-Harness, while total cost including search is 14.6% lower. These results show that combining module-level design reuse with feedback-driven compositional search can systematically improve agent harnesses while keeping overall optimization cost under control.

</details>


### [141] [AgentDiscover: Autonomous Discovery with Minimal Search Scaffolding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05334)
*Mahdi Farahbakhsh,Ilan Sela,Fatemeh Doudi,Vishnu Teja Kunde,Krishna Narayanan,Jean-Francois Chamberland,Dileep Kalathil*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AgentDiscover通过让大模型承担规划角色并配以结构化长期记忆与服务器监督，在多域发现任务中以更高的成本效率超越传统“模型仅提议”的固定搜索框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统利用大模型做科学发现的框架通常把流程硬编码为人类预先设计的搜索算法，模型仅负责提出候选。随着模型能力提升，作者质疑预先固定策略是否仍优于让模型自我规划搜索——即测试把搜索控制权交给模型是否能带来可伸缩优势。

Method: 设计并实现AgentDiscover：一个由编码型智能体驱动的发现框架，核心组件为（1）将模型上下文作为短期工作记忆以进行规划与执行；（2）中央数据库作为长期记忆，记录想法、候选与关系，并以使经典选择规则（如MAP-Elites、MCTS）可通过单次查询实现的结构化方式存储；（3）服务器在每次提交后监督与引导智能体，保证长期运行中的方向性；在若干领域（内核工程、生物、算法设计、数学）与基线比较，评估成本效率与最终质量。

Result: 在作者的实验中，AgentDiscover在成本-效果曲线（更低成本下达到更好分数）上优于已有框架；在内核工程、计算生物、算法设计和数学任务中取得更好或相当的结果；其生成的程序若参加过往AtCoder启发式竞赛可获七次第一；并在十一个数学和系统优化任务上匹配或超过所有使用相同模型的基线。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 本文提出让大型语言模型从“提议者”提升为“规划者”，通过AgentDiscover系统将模型与结构化长期记忆（实验数据库）结合，允许模型用上下文作为工作记忆、计划搜索、执行实验并记录尝试，从而在多个发现任务上更高效地超越以固定人类设计搜索策略的框架。

Abstract: Frameworks that use large language models for scientific discovery typically rely on a fixed, human-designed algorithm that decides what the model sees at each step, leaving the model only the role of proposer. The model knows nothing of the search beyond what it is shown. As models grow more capable, a question arises: does a search strategy chosen by a human before the run scale better than promoting the model from proposer to planner and letting it own the search? The Bitter Lesson suggests that choosing the strategy in advance is the kind of hand-designed structure that general methods eventually outscale. We introduce AgentDiscover, in which a coding agent plans the search using its context as working memory, runs experiments, and records every attempt in a database of ideas, candidates, and their relations. This database serves as the agent's long-term memory and is structured so that the selection rules of classical algorithms such as MAP-Elites and Monte Carlo tree search each reduce to a single query, which the agent is free to use, combine, or replace. A server maintains the database and steers the agent after every submission, keeping it on course over long runs. In our experiments, AgentDiscover is more cost-efficient than existing frameworks, reaching better scores at lower cost. On tasks in kernel engineering, biology, algorithm design, and mathematics, AgentDiscover outperforms prior discovery frameworks. Its programs would have placed first among human competitors in seven past AtCoder heuristic contests, and on eleven mathematical and systems optimization tasks it matches or exceeds every baseline that uses the same model. Our code is available at https://github.com/mhdfb/AgentDiscover.

</details>


### [142] [EnGRICH: Enhancing Generative Reward Modeling with Critiques from Humans](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05370)
*Xuancheng Li,Beining Wang,Haitao Li,Heng Wang,Yujia Zhou,Qingyi Pan,Blaze Chen,Yiqun Liu,Min Zhang,Qingyao Ai*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 用小量人类批评训练的MetaCritic为GRM提供细粒度过程监督并能推广到结果数据，从而提高批评可靠性与下游奖励建模性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GRM通常仅以最终偏好正误作为监督，偏好结果空间受限导致不可靠的自然语言批评可能仍被强化；同时人类批评稀缺且常被压缩为标量奖励，细粒度信息未被充分利用。EnGRICH旨在利用少量人类批评的评判准则，提高GRM对批评过程的真实性与可用性。

Method: 在训练时并行训练GRM与MetaCritic：MetaCritic基于人类批评构建响应特定的评分准则（rubrics），评估生成批评在证据覆盖与正确性上的表现，生成过程奖励用于细粒度归因与引导更好批评的探索；同时对MetaCritic进行目标性优化，使其将人类评判准则推广到仅有偏好结果的数据；推理阶段仅使用训练好的GRM。

Result: 在七个奖励模型基准上，EnGRICH稳定优于竞争基线；消融与分析表明MetaCritic构建的响应性rubric、过程奖励与对结果数据的推广是性能提升的主要机制。

Conclusion: EnGRICH通过在训练阶段引入一个用少量人工批评训练的MetaCritic，能将人工评判的细粒度评估标准推广到仅有偏好结果的大规模数据上，从而显著提升生成式奖励模型（GRM）输出批评的可靠性与质量，且在多基准上优于现有方法。

Abstract: Generative reward models (GRMs) are important for LLM optimization. Unlike scalar reward models, GRMs generate natural-language critiques alongside preference judgments, providing finer-grained evaluation signals. Their effectiveness depends heavily on critique reliability. However, existing GRM training typically uses final preference correctness as outcome supervision. Because the preference outcome space is highly constrained, unreliable critiques can still yield correct outcomes and thus be reinforced. Recent work leverages human critiques for process supervision, but such critiques are scarce and are often reduced to scalar rewards, leaving their fine-grained evaluative information underutilized. We argue that evaluative criteria learned from human critiques can be generalized to broader outcome-only preference data. To this end, we propose \textbf{EnGRICH}, a GRM training framework that pairs the GRM with a training-time MetaCritic learned from a small set of human critiques. MetaCritic constructs response-specific rubrics and uses them to evaluate the evidence coverage and correctness of generated critiques. The resulting signals provide both process rewards for fine-grained credit assignment and structured guidance for exploring better critiques. During GRM training, MetaCritic is further optimized to generalize human-grounded evaluative criteria to outcome-only data. At inference, the trained GRM operates independently. Experiments across seven reward-model benchmarks show that EnGRICH consistently improves over competitive baselines, while further analyses validate the effectiveness of its core mechanisms.

</details>


### [143] [Learning Field Reconstruction from Incomplete Data by Globally Correcting Local Estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05375)
*Renhao Zhong,Zihan Zhou,Chiyuan Ma,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种先学局部、再全域修正的两阶段估计器，通过补丁级共享坐标条件预测与观测条件的一致性合成局部场，再以全域残差对其修正，在海洋数据集上显著降低MSE并提升重构细节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当每个训练场都不完整时，完整场分布不可辨识且有限数据常导致对尖锐过渡和局部变化的平均化预测。需要一种基于观测可验证的结构先验——局部空间关系——来偏好合理的补全。

Method: 设计了两阶段估计器：一是共享坐标条件预测器在不完整补丁上学习局部场估计，并将重叠预测通过观测条件的一致性机制合成为局部一致场；二是全域估计器在保持原始观测的同时，对冻结的局部场估计学习残差修正（以局部估计及其与观测的差异作为输入），从而实现局部构建与全局证据的结合。

Result: 在三个具有真实观测空洞的海洋数据集上，所提方法在被保留的由观测支持的值上取得最低MSE与最高PSNR，相对于最强外部基线将MSE降低了28.9%–34.5%。

Conclusion: 本文提出的局部构建、全局可修正估计器在不完整训练样本上能更好地恢复物理场，从而得到比现有外部基线明显更低的MSE和更高的PSNR，证明了用局部空间结构作为先验并通过全域残差修正能提高完成质量与细节保留。

Abstract: Reconstructing physical fields from training samples that are always incomplete requires learning spatial structure from fragmented observations.Existing context--query work establishes how held-out observations provide valid training targets, but this does not make the complete-field distribution identifiable when every training field is incomplete.With finite data, weak evidence of sharp transitions and localized variations can further favor averaged predictions that attenuate local detail.A structural prior is therefore needed to favor plausible completions; local spatial relationships offer one grounded in the observations.We propose a locally constructed, globally revisable estimator that explicitly learns local field estimates and subsequently corrects them using full-domain observations.A shared coordinate-conditioned predictor learns from incomplete patches, allowing relatively well-observed neighborhoods to provide direct supervision of local structure.Its overlapping predictions are reconciled into an observation-conditioned consensus field.A full-domain estimator retains the original observations and learns a residual correction around this frozen field estimate, allowing locally constructed structure to be revised by broader evidence.The local estimate serves as both an explicit input, accompanied by its discrepancies with the observations, and a prediction starting point that the global model can revise.On three real-world ocean datasets with authentic observation gaps, our estimator achieves the lowest MSE and highest PSNR on withheld source-supported values, reducing MSE by 28.9\%--34.5\% against the strongest external baseline.

</details>


### [144] [Sibyl: An Efficient Small-large Model Collaboration Framework for Long-horizon Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05383)
*Zhewei Fang,Yuxin Zhang,Zhenwei Shao,Mengze Li,Zheng Lin,Long Chen,Zhou Yu,Zhe Chen,Zhiwen Chen,Zhaode Wang,chengfei lv*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Sibyl trains SLM agents to make step-wise decisions on when to call cloud models and internalize their guidance via a three-stage pipeline, achieving strong performance on long-horizon tasks with very few cloud calls.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SLMs are efficient for on-device agents but lack reasoning for long-horizon tasks; selective step-level consultation with cloud models can help, but determining when to consult and balancing task success vs cloud-call cost is challenging due to entanglement of cloud contributions with later actions.

Method: Three-stage training: (1) consultation-free self-evolving RL to build base policy; (2) decisive-disagreement state mining to cold-start consultation behavior; (3) consultation-aware RL to jointly optimize consultation decisions and guidance internalization.

Result: On ALFWorld and WebShop, a 0.6B SLM trained with Sibyl outperforms baselines by large margins (95.2% and 80.4% higher success rates) while using only 0.8 and 3.9 cloud calls per trajectory on average.

Conclusion: Sibyl enables small language models (SLMs) to selectively consult cloud models at the step level, internalize guidance, and jointly optimize consultation, resulting in high task success with minimal cloud calls.

Abstract: Small language models (SLMs) offer a promising foundation for on-device agents through low-latency, resource-efficient inference, yet limited reasoning and planning capabilities constrain their performance on long-horizon tasks requiring multi-step interaction with the environment. Step-level collaboration between SLMs and larger cloud-hosted models can bridge this gap, but identifying states that warrant cloud assistance remains challenging: the contribution of each cloud call is entangled with subsequent actions and can be assessed only from the final task outcome. Compounding this challenge, the SLM must balance two competing objectives: maximizing task success and minimizing cloud calls. To address this, we propose Sibyl, an algorithm that trains SLM agents to selectively consult cloud models at the step level and internalize their guidance for subsequent decisions, achieving strong task performance with minimal cloud reliance. Sibyl follows a three-stage training pipeline that (1) builds a robust base policy through consultation-free self-evolving reinforcement learning (RL); (2) cold-starts consultation behavior via decisive-disagreement state mining; and (3) jointly optimizes consultation decisions and guidance internalization through consultation-aware RL. Experiments on ALFWorld and WebShop demonstrate that Sibyl, using only a 0.6B-parameter model, outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, including agent training and routing methods, by 95.2% and 80.4% in success rate while averaging only 0.8 and 3.9 cloud calls per trajectory, respectively.

</details>


### [145] [MMPostTrainBench: Benchmarking Autonomous Research for Multimodal Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05398)
*Yuxin Liu,Yuxuan Wang,Zhenxin Lei,Lingchen Meng,Yuchong Sun,Junming Lin,Hongcheng Liu,Yunfei Chu,Qize Yang,Jin Xu,Lei Zhang,Zhendong Mao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Extending autonomous LLM-driven research to multimodal tasks uncovers frequent regressions and selection failures; MMResearch—a memory- and evidence-grounded framework—partially restores gains and improves final submissions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To evaluate whether autonomous research agents can produce sustained, multimodal model improvements and to surface failure modes unique to multimodal post-training (perception, cross-modal alignment, temporal grounding) that are not apparent in text-only settings.

Method: Constructed MMPostTrainBench across eight multimodal tasks (image, audio, video, joint audio-video, and image-grounded software repair). Agents start from a common base model, operate under fixed budgets, use development feedback, and submit candidate models for independent evaluation; analyses include target and non-target outcomes, iterative improvement, selection behavior, and research integrity. Developed MMResearch to connect media-grounded evidence to interventions, maintain hierarchical memory across rounds, and retain candidates via development evaluation.

Result: Over eight tasks, 52.1% of model–task means are worse than the base; submissions also show non-target regressions. Iterative research did not reliably improve models and agents often failed to pick the best candidate (selection gaps up to 5.38 percentage points). MMResearch improved submitted-model accuracy by up to 7.75 points for Claude Opus 4.8+Claude Code and 2.33 points for GPT-5.6-sol+Codex.

Conclusion: Autonomous LLM agents struggle to sustain improvements when extended from text-only to multimodal domains: many submitted models regress relative to the base model and agents often fail to select their best candidates. The proposed MMResearch framework mitigates some failures and yields measurable gains.

Abstract: Autonomous research seeks sustained model improvements through iterative experimentation and feedback. LLM agents show promise in automating machine learning and language-model post-training, but their ability to sustain multimodal improvement remains unclear. We introduce MMPostTrainBench, a benchmark spanning eight tasks in image, audio, video, and joint audio-video understanding and image-grounded software repair. Agents operate from a common base model within fixed budgets, using development feedback before independent evaluation of their submitted models. Evaluation covers target and non-target model outcomes, iterative model improvement and selection, and research integrity. Across all eight tasks, 52.1% of model--task means fall below the base, and evaluated submissions also exhibit non-target regressions. Model performance does not consistently improve across research iterations, and agents do not reliably select the best evaluated candidate for submission; final submissions trail that candidate by up to 5.38 percentage points. Extending autonomous research from text-only to multimodal tasks introduces additional sources of error in perception, cross-modal alignment, and temporal grounding. The observed regressions and selection gaps highlight the need to balance targeted improvements with non-target capability preservation and to retain gains across research iterations. These requirements motivate MMResearch, a multimodal research framework that connects media-grounded evidence to hypotheses and interventions, carries findings across rounds through hierarchical memory, and retains candidates using development evaluation. Added to existing code-agent runtimes, it improves submitted-model accuracy by up to 7.75 percentage points for Claude Opus 4.8 with Claude Code and 2.33 points for GPT-5.6-sol with Codex.

</details>


### [146] [CASE: Cost-Aware Stopping for Efficient Long-Video Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05400)
*Yiming Du,Chenghao Liu,Zhiyuan Liu,Fangxing Zheng,Zhao Wang,Junnan Nie,Songfang Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CASE 通过学习代价感知的序贯停止决策，为长视频推理代理提供一个轻量且可迁移的终止判断器，在显著降低推理成本的同时保持或提高准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长视频代理能主动收集与问题相关的证据，但缺少明确机制判断“何时已足够”以回答问题；需要平衡额外证据带来的准确性收益与推理成本。

Method: 在每个因果检查点，CASE 将对已收集证据的辅助多项选择评估与宿主代理的执行状态融合，基于完整的原生轨迹构建代价感知目标——比较当前回答与沿相同搜索路径继续停止后的收益（同时考虑答案正确性与全部推理成本）。使用轻量级的岭回归器学习该“决策差值”，输出 STOP/CONTINUE。

Result: 在多种视觉-语言模型与基准（Video-MME, VideoSeek/AVP, LongVideoBench, MLVU）上的评估显示：Video-MME 平均端到端准确率提升 +0.67 个百分点，同时模型 token 使用量减少 53.63%；冻结策略零样本迁移到 LongVideoBench/MLVU 时准确率分别提升 +3.38 / +4.58 个百分点，token 节省分别 58.78% / 51.28%；在比较的停止方法中，CASE 在选定操作点上覆盖最高的准确率-效率帕累托前沿（83.3%）；在线执行还能平均将实际运行时减少 54.1%。

Conclusion: CASE 是一个可插拔的终止决策框架，通过在因果检查点上进行策略条件化的序贯停止判断，使长视频推理代理能够在保证或提升答案准确性的同时大幅减少后续推理开销和运行时间。

Abstract: Long-video agents can actively gather question-relevant evidence, but they typically leave a central decision implicit: when has the agent seen enough to answer? We propose CASE, a plug-in termination framework that frames this decision as policy-conditioned sequential stopping. At each causal checkpoint, CASE combines an auxiliary multiple-choice assessment of accumulated evidence with the host agent's execution state. From complete native trajectories, we construct a cost-aware target that compares answering now with stopping later along the same search path, accounting jointly for answer correctness and the full cost of continued reasoning. A lightweight Ridge regressor learns this decision gap and produces STOP/CONTINUE decisions. We evaluate three vision-language models with VideoSeek and AVP. On Video-MME, end-to-end accuracy changes by +0.67 percentage points on average while CASE reduces model-token use by 53.63%. The same frozen policies then transfer zero-shot to LongVideoBench and MLVU, with end-to-end accuracy changes of +3.38 and +4.58 points while saving 58.78% and 51.28% of model tokens, respectively. Across all agent-model-benchmark combinations, CASE attains the highest accuracy-efficiency Pareto-frontier coverage among the compared stopping methods (83.3%) at the selected operating points. Online execution preserves this favorable accuracy-efficiency trade-off and additionally reduces measured runtime by 54.1% on average. CASE provides a plug-in termination framework for long-video reasoning agents, enabling them to decide when further evidence acquisition is no longer worthwhile.

</details>


### [147] [PharmAgent: Constraint-Aware Search with Frozen Language Models for Molecular Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05431)
*Nihui Shao,Guanxing Chen,Jilong Shi,Zhengyang Bai,Haohuai He,Zhenchao Tang,Qiujie Lv,Yu-An Huang,Zhi-An Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出PharmAgent：用拉格朗日控制器+结构索引重放实现约束感知的分子搜索，在多任务评估中显著超越当前最强基线，兼顾目标提升与约束满足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在有限评估预算下，需要同时提高目标活性并满足多项可开发性约束；现有方法对规则或训练依赖大，冻结语言模型可编辑但缺乏显式约束控制与相关经验积累。

Method: 提出基于拉格朗日乘子的控制器，用于将被接受状态中的约束违规转化为累积的‘约束压力’（历史），并通过结构索引的重放缓冲区引入与当前候选相关的已评估转换；采用逐步激活约束的课程学习，使候选与当前在相同目标下比较，并以被接受状态驱动乘子更新；推导了一个精确恒等式，描述乘子中违规的累积行为。

Result: 在五个任务五次独立运行中，目标专注搜索的目标分数曲线AUC为3.9208，比MOLLEO提升37.3%；在线约束下的属性调整AUC为0.7076，比最强在线基线ExLLM提升53.8%；在目标质量、属性调整表现与Pareto超体积上优于所有消融与基线配置；所有五个分子案例最终均达成可行状态。

Conclusion: PharmAgent 有效地在受限评估预算下进行约束感知的分子搜索，通过自适应外部状态的拉格朗日控制器和结构索引重放机制，将约束违规历史与当前属性测量区分开来，从而在目标提升和可开发性约束方面均显著优于现有基线。

Abstract: Molecular optimization must improve target activity and satisfy developability constraints within limited evaluation budgets. Classical methods require tailored rules or training to incorporate chemical instructions and property feedback. Frozen language models can condition edits on this information, but need explicit constraint control and relevant experience. We therefore present PharmAgent, a constraint-aware molecular search method driven by adaptive external state. Its Lagrangian controller translates violations in accepted states into accumulated constraint pressure, keeping this history separate from current property measurements. Structure-indexed replay complements this feedback with relevant evaluated transitions that guide subsequent proposals. As a curriculum progressively activates constraints, candidates and the incumbent are compared under the same current objective, and the accepted state determines the next multiplier update. We derive an exact identity that characterizes how accepted-state violations accumulate in the controller's multipliers. Across five tasks with five independent runs, PharmAgent achieves a summed area under the target-score curves (AUC) of 3.9208 in target-only search, improving over MOLLEO by 37.3%. With online constraints, it achieves a property-adjusted AUC of 0.7076, improving over the strongest online baseline, ExLLM, by 53.8%. These results rank first among all evaluated methods in both target-only and constraint-aware search. The online comparison covers all five baseline frameworks. The full system leads every ablation variant in target quality, property-adjusted performance, and Pareto hypervolume. All five molecular cases reach feasible final states, documenting target gains and trade-offs.

</details>


### [148] [TeleTune: Evolving Agent Skills From Offline Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05437)
*Justin Chih-Yao Chen,Elias Stengel-Eskin,Yan Chen,Pol Llado,Scott Counts,Mohit Bansal,Benjamin Van Durme,Harsh Jhamtani,Gaurav Verma*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TeleTune 使用动作预测误差与持出验证在离线、无目标、不可回放且交错的用户日志上学习可复用文本技能库，提升检索式演示的效果并节省大量验证成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是让计算机代理从大规模用户遥测日志中抽取可复用的程序化（procedural）技能，但日志存在目标欠定、无法回放评估和任务交错三大挑战，须提出能在离线且无目标注释的条件下安全优化与验证技能的方法。

Method: 提出 TeleTune 框架：基于对已记录轨迹的动作预测误差来生成并筛选技能库编辑；采用“技能引导进步”（在持出集上提升动作预测精度即视为有益编辑）作为离线评估准则；构建工作流（workflow）以检索覆盖新任务子目标的演示；在测试时给定学到的技能库与基于工作流的检索演示用于下游代理。实验在 WorkArena 与 Online-Mind2Web 数据集上与随机检索、Agent Workflow Memory（AWM）及其组合比较。

Result: TeleTune 在两个基准上的平均成功率分别为77.1%与80.6%，分别比最强基线高6.7%与7.7%；在最强扰动下，WorkArena 上仍达68.5%，高出6.3%。分析还表明：技能优化与工作流检索互补；在固定日志上优化比用在线回放验证相同编辑节省5至75倍的 token 成本；技能引导进步与在线成功率呈良好相关。

Conclusion: TeleTune 能在无目标标注、不可回放且可能任务交错的离线操作日志上学习出文本化技能库，有效提高基于检索的演示利用与任务成功率，并在多数据扰动下表现稳健。

Abstract: Computer-use agents need to capture procedural knowledge of how people use software. User telemetry offers a scalable source of this knowledge. However, learning reusable skills from these logs requires addressing three challenges: (1) Goal Underspecification, since logs do not record the goal behind each action; (2) Non-Replayability, since past activity cannot be replayed to evaluate skill updates; and (3) Interleaved Trajectories, since logs may mix several tasks without marking their boundaries. To address these, we introduce TeleTune, a framework for learning a textual skill library from offline logs without recorded goals, cannot be replayed during optimization, and may interleave tasks. TeleTune uses action-prediction errors on logged trajectories to propose library edits and keep only those that improve held-out action-prediction accuracy, which we call skill-guided progress. The learned workflows also enable retrieval of demonstrations that cover the subgoals of a new task. At test time, the agent is provided with the learned library and the workflow-based retrieved demonstrations. Experiments on WorkArena and Online-Mind2Web show that TeleTune outperforms random retrieval, Agent Workflow Memory (AWM), and their combination. We find that the best baseline varies by setting, whereas TeleTune achieves average success rates of 77.1% and 80.6%, respectively, improving over the strongest baseline on each benchmark by 6.7% and 7.7%. Under the heaviest perturbation of the WorkArena training data,TeleTune keeps the highest average success rate at 68.5%, 6.3% above the strongest baseline. Our analyses show (1) skill optimization and workflow-based retrieval are complementary, (2) optimizing on fixed logs costs 5 to 75 times fewer tokens than validating the same edits with live episodes, (3) skill-guided progress tracks the live success rate.

</details>


### [149] [AI Safety via Debate is Compromised by Cognitive Biases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05461)
*Gefei Liu,Sonya Rashkovan,Sophia Lloyd George,Isaac Sheidlower,Serena Booth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LLM debates can be gamed: rhetorical manipulations like anchoring, jargon, exploiting fallacy oversight, or verbosity make human judges favor manipulated positions, threatening the reliability of debate-based model supervision.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Test the core assumption of AI safety via debate that truthful arguments are easier to defend under adversarial scrutiny, by checking whether rhetorical strategies exploiting human judgment biases can overturn that advantage.

Method: Constructed 68 LLM-generated detective-dialogues with known culprits; applied four rhetorical interventions (anchoring, fallacy oversight, pro-jargon, verbosity) to either the true-advocate or innocent-advocate side; ran a human adjudication study with 369 participants to measure judgment shifts.

Result: Across bias types, the interventions significantly shifted adjudicator judgments toward the manipulated side, demonstrating that manipulative rhetoric can undermine debate supervision.

Conclusion: Debate-based supervision is vulnerable: human adjudicators can be systematically swayed by rhetorical strategies, causing judgments to favor the manipulated side even when that side is incorrect.

Abstract: Reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) has played a central role in making large language models responsive to human instructions. However, human evaluators often favor flattering or persuasive responses over truthful ones, creating incentives for models to appeal to evaluators at the expense of accuracy. AI safety via debate has been proposed as a way to improve the supervision of language models: in this paradigm, two agents argue opposing positions and challenge each other's claims, potentially exposing falsehoods to the adjudicator. A central premise of AI safety via debate is that truthful arguments are easier to defend than false ones under adversarial scrutiny. In this work, we investigate whether this advantage persists when debaters use rhetorical strategies that exploit biases in human judgment. Inspired by competitive debate, we construct 68 LLM-generated dialogues about detective mysteries with known culprits, spanning four interventions: anchoring, fallacy oversight, pro-jargon, and verbosity. We apply each intervention to either the side advocating for the true culprit or the side advocating for an innocent suspect, allowing us to distinguish influence on adjudication from correctness. In a study with 369 participants, we find that, pooled across bias types, these interventions significantly shift judgments toward the manipulated side. These findings expose a vulnerability in debate-based supervision: human adjudication is sensitive to manipulative rhetorical strategies.

</details>


### [150] [Hallucination Across the Reasoning Lifecycle: Interface Visibility, Causal Evidence, and Release Control in Large Reasoning Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05472)
*Zhe Yu,Mohan Li,Lei Yu,Ka-Ho Chow,Chengwei Qin,Xingyu Wu,Wenpeng Xing,Shuguang Xiong,Meng Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 综述提出UIPCA五类推理幻觉，指出缺乏证据证明某干预能在保持事实可靠性的同时改进推理，并给出基于诊断—验证—修复—控制的应对方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理错误会级联影响后续决策与系统“记忆”（persistent state），使得基于文本的系统在连贯性与事实可靠性上出现幻觉，需要系统化地诊断与纠正此类错误。

Method: 对312篇论文及第一方报告进行综述，并在58个被详细审阅的来源上聚焦比较证据，提出并组织了UIPCA（Unsupported premises, Invalid inferences, dependent reUse, visible answer-trace Consistency, Action-policy failures）分类框架，梳理了可观测证据、实验设计所能确立的因果关系，以及现有证据支持的纠正策略。

Result: 产出：UIPCA框架、对现有干预效果的批判性合成（未发现改善推理同时提升事实可靠性的对照证据）、并提出将诊断连接到验证、修复、选择性发布和持久态控制的路线图。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：文本推理中的“幻觉”可以分为五类（UIPCA），这些错误会在后续决策和记忆中传播；现有文献尚无证据表明某项干预在配对条件下同时改善推理能力并降低事实不可靠性；应将故障诊断与验证、修复、选择性发布和持久态控制相结合以应对问题。

Abstract: Reasoning errors can propagate into later decisions and memory. This survey synthesizes 312 papers and first-party reports on text-based reasoning hallucinations around three questions: what evidence is observable, what study designs establish, and which corrective actions the evidence supports. UIPCA records unsupported premises (U), invalid inferences (I), dependent reuse (P), visible answer-trace consistency (C), and action-policy failures (A). Across 58 reviewed sources, no comparison establishes that a specified intervention improves reasoning while reducing factual reliability under matched conditions. The synthesis connects diagnosis to verification, repair, selective release, and persistent-state control across memory, tools, and training feedback.

</details>


### [151] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning with Stable Temporal Abstraction for Language Model Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05473)
*Shayan Mohajer Hamidi,Yize Cheng,Yuanda Xu,Zhengze Zhou,Alborz Geramifard*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: STAC通过将不良重规划/持久化作为约束成本并在边界决策上进行拉格朗日优化，有效稳定了分层策略的时间抽象，显著提升了长期交互任务的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管显式的分层结构可以分离子目标规划与动作执行，但并不能保证时间抽象（子目标的持续性）稳定，边界策略可能频繁替换子目标或在不再适宜时仍然保留子目标，导致性能下降。

Method: 提出约束边界策略优化（STAC），在边界决策上引入拉格朗日约束成本，仅作用于采样到的边界决策，而不改变底层算法的奖励、评论器目标、子目标优势和原始动作优势；通过Lagrangian优化动态调整约束权重以惩罚‘过早重规划’与‘过久坚持’两类不良行为。

Result: 在两个基线架构和两个基准（ALFWorld与WebShop）上，STAC较强分层基线分别提升了：使用Qwen3-0.6B时约8.1和7.9个百分点，使用Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct时约23.5和15.8个百分点的成功率。

Conclusion: 本文提出的STAC通过将不当的提前重规划和过时坚持建模为约束成本，从而稳定了分层强化学习中的时间抽象，改进了层次策略的持久性并显著提升了交互任务上的成功率。

Abstract: Hierarchical reinforcement learning improves long-horizon control by organizing primitive actions around persistent subgoals and assigning credit at multiple temporal scales. Recent hierarchical language agents bring these benefits to interactive tasks by explicitly separating subgoal planning from action execution. We observe, however, that an explicit hierarchy does not by itself determine how stable the resulting temporal abstraction is: the learned boundary policy may replace the subgoal almost every turn, making it effectively transient, or retain a subgoal after it has stopped being appropriate. We call this temporal abstraction instability. We propose Stable Temporal Abstraction via Constrained Optimization (STAC), a constrained boundary-policy optimization method that represents premature replanning and stale persistence as constraint costs. STAC applies the resulting Lagrangian costs only to the sampled boundary decision, leaving the underlying algorithm's rewards, critic targets, subgoal advantages, and primitive-action advantages unchanged. Across two backbones and two benchmarks, STAC improves success over a strong hierarchical baseline by $8.1$ and $7.9$ points on ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen3-0.6B, and by $23.5$ and $15.8$ points with Llama-3.2-1B-Instruct.

</details>


### [152] [CodeForge-MA: Execution-Verified Multi-Agent Learning with Language-Conditioned LoRA for Multilingual Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05481)
*Zhizhou Gu,Xianting Wu,Siyu Gu,Tian Zhang,Kejian Tong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CodeForge-MA用多智能体数据锻造、测试驱动的强化调优和语言条件的LoRA稀疏专家路由，在不改动基础模型的前提下提升了代码生成的可执行性和跨语言能力，并加强了对基准泄露的过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在冻结主干限制下，代码生成模型常见的执行失败、跨语言覆盖不足和训练数据泄露/污染问题，同时兼顾推理时保持主干不变的要求。

Method: 提出一个包含四个专责智能体（Composer、Reviewer、Executor、Curator）的多智能体数据锻造框架；训练时结合masked supervised fine-tuning与基于测试的强化学习目标；对大模型采用语言条件的稀疏专家路由管理多个低秩LoRA适配器以保持主干冻结并促进跨语言迁移。

Result: 通过联合设计数据、训练目标和适配器机制，作者声称在多语言代码合成任务上取得了稳健提升（摘要未给出定量细节）。

Conclusion: 本文提出的CodeForge-MA通过数据生成--验证--过滤三阶段多智能体流水线、基于测试的强化微调目标与语言条件的LoRA稀疏专家路由，在冻结主干模型约束下提高了模型的可执行正确性、多语言泛化与数据污染控制能力。

Abstract: Large language models for code generation often fail on execution, multilingual coverage, and contamination control, especially under frozen backbone constraints. We present CodeForge-MA, a unified framework that improves code synthesis through a multi-agent data forge, execution verified reinforced instruction tuning, and a language conditioned mixture of LoRA adapters. Four specialized agents, Composer, Reviewer, Executor, and Curator, iteratively refine instruction code pairs, validate them with tests, and filter duplicates and benchmark leakage. During training, we combine masked supervised fine tuning with a test driven reinforcement objective to align generations with executable correctness. For the larger model, we use sparse expert routing over low rank adapters to improve cross language transfer while keeping the base model unchanged at inference. Experiments show that joint data, objective, and adapter design yields robust gains across programming languages.

</details>


### [153] [SkillGATE: Gate-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search for Skill Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05489)
*Rongchen Zhao,Yu Chen,Yanming Yang,Shijia Xu,Juyuan Wang,Jin Xu,Zibin Zheng,Jingping Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SkillGATE applies a G-PUCT-guided MCTS over a graph-preserving hierarchical index to enable adaptive, uncertainty-aware skill retrieval, delivering strong empirical gains (R@1 +16.3%).


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing SR methods either score skills independently or follow predefined propagation/routing, which are vulnerable to semantic distractors, local traps, and early routing errors. An adaptive, uncertainty-aware search that coordinates region-level navigation and skill-level selection can better explore large diverse libraries.

Method: Construct a graph-preserving hierarchical index; perform Monte Carlo Tree Search with four phases (selection, expansion, simulation, backpropagation); use G-PUCT to guide selection; expansion discovers new regions; simulation evaluates candidate skills; backpropagation updates statistics to inform future actions.

Result: On six SR benchmarks, SkillGATE consistently improves various retrieval and reranking backbones, achieving a 16.3% improvement in overall R@1 over the strongest retriever baseline; code is released.

Conclusion: SkillGATE reframes skill retrieval as an adaptive information-foraging process and implements a graph-guided hierarchical retrieval using Gate-Aware MCTS, yielding substantial empirical gains and more robust retrieval behavior compared to independent ranking or fixed routing methods.

Abstract: Skill Retrieval (SR) aims to identify the most relevant skills from external skill libraries, and becomes increasingly challenging as libraries grow in scale and diversity. Existing methods either rank skills independently or rely on predefined graph propagation and hierarchical routing, making them vulnerable to semantic distractors, local trapping, and early routing errors. We formulate SR as an adaptive information-foraging process that coordinates region-level navigation with skill-level selection according to the utility and uncertainty observed during search. Based on this formulation, we propose SkillGATE, a graph-guided hierarchical retrieval framework with Gate-Aware Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS). SkillGATE constructs a graph-preserving hierarchical index and performs adaptive retrieval through selection, expansion, simulation, and backpropagation. G-PUCT guides action selection, expansion explores new regions, simulation evaluates candidate skills, and backpropagation updates search statistics. Experiments on six SR benchmarks show that SkillGATE consistently improves diverse retrieval and reranking backbones, achieving a 16.3\% improvement in overall R@1 over the strongest retriever-based baseline. Our code is available at https://github.com/Edwinbe/SkillGATE-v1/.

</details>


### [154] [The Functional Structure of Post-Compression Recovery in Low-Rank LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05504)
*Zishan Shao,Liang Tian,Georgiy Zemlevskiy,Kangning Cui,Lixun Zhang,Yixiao Wang,Ting Jiang,Jinhee Kim,Yixuan Chen,Rui-Feng Wang,Fan Yang,Hai Li,Yiran Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Compressed LLMs show reproducible, module-level "recovery pressure" that explains heterogeneity in post-compression recovery; this pressure can be characterized both with backward probes and forward-only methods, predicts recovery behavior, reorganizes non-uniformly during recovery, and provides a functional complement to scalar loss.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Different low-rank compression methods yield compressed LLMs that respond differently to the same recovery procedures, and relative method advantages can change after recovery. The work asks whether such heterogeneity reflects deeper functional structure beyond scalar loss and how that structure evolves during recovery.

Method: Define a standardized within-backbone functional characterization applicable across heterogeneous low-rank compression methods; introduce a primary backward (probe-based, module-wise) characterization and a complementary forward-only characterization that avoids loss/backprop; measure recovery pressure at the compression endpoint; track module-wise structure and responses through staged recovery and localized update experiments.

Result: (1) Heterogeneous recovery outcomes are driven by a reproducible, compression-induced functional structure (recovery pressure). (2) The primary backward probe reveals consistent module-wise structure across independent probes; forward-only probes recover related structure without backprop. (3) Endpoint recovery pressure correlates with subsequent recovery response. (4) During recovery, module-wise structure reorganizes non-uniformly, and updates localized to modules can produce distributed effects beyond the updated modules. (5) Tracking evolving recovery pressure offers a complementary, functional metric of recovery progress beyond scalar loss.

Conclusion: Compression-induced heterogeneity in recovery reflects a reproducible functional structure, formalized as "recovery pressure"; this structure predicts and organizes how different modules respond during post-compression recovery and evolves non-uniformly through the recovery process.

Abstract: Different low-rank compression methods can produce compressed LLMs that respond differently to the same post-compression recovery procedure, and relative advantages observed between methods at the compression endpoint may shrink, grow, or even reverse after recovery. We ask whether this recovery heterogeneity reflects functional structure beyond scalar loss evolution, and how that structure evolves throughout recovery. Our results establish that this heterogeneity reflects a reproducible compression-induced functional structure, which we formalize as recovery pressure.
