Method: 将协议语义与优化策略分离，定义 harness->engine 的意图/需求接口和 engine->harness 的运行时状态/能力/结果回馈。并以两种实例化优化展现：在线缓存感知的运行时协调（cache-aware runtime coordination）与基于负载/角色的执行模式选择（workload-aware execution-mode selection）。在多个会话与研究代理基准上评估性能提升。

Result: 在四个对话与研究代理基准的内存受限并发部署中，HEAR 实现了 1.61× 批处理加速，SCBench 中位首字节时间减少 2.23×；在 BrowseComp-Plus 和 DeepResearchBench 分别得到 1.23× 与 2.45× 端到端加速，且未观测到任务质量下降。Mooncake 实验显示工作流意图与引擎实时状态在不同负载下互补。

Conclusion: 本文提出 HEAR，一种双向的 Harness–Engine 配对协议，通过标准化 harness 表达工作流意图与执行需求，和 engine 返回运行时状态与能力，实现在内存受限并发场景下对代理化 LLM 服务的协调优化，显著提高吞吐与首字节时延，且不改变模型或工作流语义。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly execute complex workflows involving multi-turn reasoning, tool use, and parallel agents. Efficient serving requires decisions that span two layers with complementary information: the agent harness understands workflow dependencies, context lifecycles, and execution objectives, whereas the inference engine observes request queues, KV-cache state, resource pressure, and execution capabilities. Existing interfaces do not systematically connect these views, limiting workflow-aware execution.
  HEAR, a bidirectional Harness--Engine Pairing protocol for agentic LLM serving. HEAR standardizes how the harness communicates workflow intent and execution requirements and how the engine returns runtime state, capabilities, and outcomes. By separating protocol semantics from optimization policies, HEAR supports diverse coordination strategies without changing workflow or model semantics. We instantiate HEAR for online cache-aware runtime coordination and workload-aware execution-mode selection for agent roles.
  Across four conversational and research-agent benchmarks under memory-constrained, concurrent serving, HEAR achieves a $1.61\times$ batch speedup and reduces median time-to-first-token by $2.23\times$ on SCBench. Mooncake shows that workflow intent and live engine state provide complementary benefits across load regimes. On BrowseComp-Plus and DeepResearchBench, workload-specific configurations yield $1.23\times$ and $2.45\times$ end-to-end speedups, respectively, without observed task-quality degradation. These results establish HEAR as a reusable coordination substrate for efficient agentic LLM serving.

</details>


### [223] [FREA: A Multi-Source Expert Benchmark for Reaction Feasibility Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06614)
*Botao Yu,Bo Zhou,Daniel Adu-Ampratwum,Frazier N. Baker,Ziru Chen,Reza Averly,Ye Liu,Wenhao Gao,Xia Ning,Huan Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FREA基准显示没有万能的可行性验证器；LLM与前向模型各有长短，生成负样本可以提升平均表现但不一定改善对模型提案的筛选，说明需要按来源评估并设计可迁移的负样本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成模型和AI代理大规模提出化学反应，必须用可行性验证器筛选进入合成计划的候选。核心问题是这些验证器在不同来源和不同类型候选上的判定是否与化学家一致，以及将生成负样本用于监督能否改善验证器的迁移能力。

Method: 构建并发布FREA基准：751条由化学专家在明确可行性判据下标注的反应，来源包括逆合成模型提案、零产率实验记录、LLM对记录反应的编辑以及五种生成的负样本方法。评价多类验证器（包括前向模型、专用验证器和仅给定判准的LLM），使用AUROC等指标并考察不同操作点及任务细分（如分离不可行替代断裂）。另外发布包含>1400万条记录反应与生成负样本的语料并进行匹配训练比较，分析将生成负样本混入训练的影响及不同生成方法的传递性。

Result: 发现无单一验证器对所有来源领先；LLM在仅给出判据时表现接近专用验证器；前向模型在逆合成提案上表现最佳但在可行编辑与不可行替代断裂任务上表现不佳（甚至低于随机）；在匹配训练实验中，加入混合生成负样本提高了跨源平均AUROC，但未能改善对逆合成提案的性能；对负样本生成方法的不同选择表明，在生成候选上的最大提升往往伴随对真实提案筛选能力的下降，提示负样本设计需考虑与目标提案的分布匹配以实现可迁移性。

Conclusion: 没有任何单一可行性验证器在所有反应来源上都占优。大型语言模型在仅给定判定标准时与专用验证器相当；向前模型在筛选逆合成提案时表现最好，但在评估记录反应的可行编辑时错误地拒绝大部分可行编辑，并且在将不可行的替代断裂与可行生成候选区分时表现低于随机。引入生成的负样本能提高对多源数据的平均AUROC，但这些提升不适用于逆合成提案，而且对生成候选的改进有时伴随对真实提案筛选能力的下降。总体结论是需要对来自不同来源的候选进行专家级评估，并为模型提案设计可迁移的负样本。

Abstract: As generative models and AI agents propose chemical reactions at a scale beyond expert review, feasibility verifiers decide which proposals enter synthesis planning. But do their decisions agree with chemists across different kinds of candidates? We introduce FREA, a benchmark of 751 reactions labeled by expert chemists under an explicit feasibility criterion, drawn from retrosynthesis model proposals, zero-yield experimental records, edits by large language models (LLMs), and five negative candidate generation methods. Our evaluation finds that no verifier leads across all sources: LLMs given only the criterion are competitive with dedicated verifiers, while forward models perform best on retrosynthesis proposals but reject most feasible edits of recorded reactions at the evaluated operating points. Looking beyond aggregate scores, both forward models perform below chance when separating infeasible alternative disconnections from feasible generated candidates. To study whether negative supervision addresses these weaknesses, we also release a corpus of over 14 million recorded reactions and generated negative candidates. In matched training comparisons, adding a mixture of generated negatives to forward training raises mean AUROC across sources, but these gains do not extend to retrosynthesis proposals. Varying the generation method further shows that the largest gain on generated candidates coincides with worse proposal screening. These findings motivate evaluating verifiers against experts across sources and designing negatives for transfer to model proposals.

</details>


### [224] [Collective intelligence through aggregation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06652)
*Franz Dietrich,Christian List*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究在多值且相互关联问题下的判断聚合，证明了关于一致性、抗操纵性与真值追踪性的若干不可能性与可能性定理；结论指出需对问题域作出合理限制才能同时满足三项目标，且中位数方法在实际中是一个稳健的折衷选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实决策场景中（专家小组、委员会、机器集体等）常需对多值变量作出联合判断，且这些变量间有约束或相互影响。想知道如何从个体判断中聚合出“智能”的集体判断，同时应对一致性矛盾、策略操纵与追踪真相的能力三大挑战。

Method: 构建形式化的判断聚合模型，将个体对多值变量的判断视为评价/估计，然后定义三类理性准则（一致性、策略抗性、真值追踪性），证明相应的不可能性与可能性定理。使用数学证明（不可能定理、构造性存在性定理）、对比常见聚合器（如平均、众数、中位数）并分析在不同假设（例如偏好/分布、变量依赖结构、度量空间或单峰性限制）下的性能。

Result: 给出一系列新的不可能性与可能性结果：在一般无约束的情况下，常见逐项聚合（按每个议题平均或中位）可能导致集合不一致且易被策略操纵，也可能无法可靠接近真值；在加上适当域约束或偏好/分布假设（例如指标满足某种单峰性或问题结构可分离）时，可以构造既保持一致又在某种意义下不易被操纵且能追踪真实的聚合规则。中位数方法在许多实际假设下具有较好鲁棒性和策略抵抗性，因此被推荐为实用折中方案。论文还讨论了结论对非人类群体（例如算法或传感器网络）决策的意义。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：在多值问题且题目间可能相互关联的情形下，要同时满足“一致性（collective consistency）”、“不可操控性（non-manipulability）”与“能追踪真实”（truth-tracking）三项理性要求是有严格限制的；在一般情形下存在不可能定理，但在特定限制性条件下存在可行的聚合方法。总体上，中位数（median）方法虽然非完美，但在权衡上述三项挑战时表现较好。

Abstract: Suppose a committee, expert panel, or other group is making judgments on some issues, where these may be not just yes/no-questions, such as whether a defendant is guilty, but also variables with many possible values, such as macroeconomic or meteorological variables or travel directions. Furthermore, there may be interconnections between different issues, as in the case of economic or climate variables. How can the group arrive at "intelligent" collective judgments, based on the group members' individual judgments? We investigate three challenges raised by this judgment-aggregation problem. First, reasonable methods of aggregation (such as defining the collective judgment for each issue as the average or median judgment) can produce inconsistent collective judgments. Secondly, many methods of aggregation are manipulable by strategic voting. Finally, not all methods of aggregation are conducive to tracking the truth on the issues in question. We prove new impossibility or possibility theorems on all three challenges, identifying what it takes to produce collective judgments in a consistent, non-manipulable, and truth-tracking manner and thereby to achieve collective intelligence through aggregation. Overall, the median method, though imperfect, performs reasonably well. We also note the relevance of our analysis for non-human group decisions.

</details>


### [225] [Language models can notice an impossible engineering problem yet still report it as solved](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06668)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 作者用30对力学题检验14款模型在‘解题’与‘拒绝不可解题’上的表现，发现模型会在指出缺陷后仍把题标为已解；引导性提示能提高拒绝率但有时会降低有效题解答率，因而评估应同时考查两类题并区分识别与报告的一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统准确率不能反映模型是否能拒绝不合逻辑或物理不可能的问题；在自动化工程计算场景中，模型若在不可解题上仍给出“已解”的答案会带来真实风险，因此需要专门评估模型的‘拒绝能力’和报告的一致性。

Method: 作者构建了30对力学题（每对包含一个有效题与一个通过更改已知量或假设使其不可解的对应题），用两位独立解题者验证每个答案键并确认不可行性。测试14个模型的回复，每次回复需判定为“solved”或“cannot solve”；还用AI判定和数值检查核验模型是否识别缺陷并报告状态。后续对同一供应商的4个模型进行提示修改（用“flawed”而非“cannot solve”并要求说明缺陷），统计拒绝率与有效题解答率的变化并做显著性检验。

Result: 在三个近期模型中，90次回复中有12次未能拒绝不可解题；其中11次模型实际上指出了题目缺陷并给出修正问题的解，但仍报告原题为“solved”。在后续提示修改实验中，三款模型显著提高了拒绝不可解题的比例，但三款模型对有效题的解答率出现下降，表明拒绝敏感性与求解能力存在权衡。作者据此提出评估应对有效题与被破坏题分别评分并区分缺陷识别与报告状态。

Conclusion: 论文结论是：在工程力学题的判别上，语言模型不仅要能解题，还必须能拒绝物理上不可能的题目；现有模型在“识别并拒绝不可解问题”方面仍有明显欠缺，且在被引导后出现拒绝率提升同时有效题目求解率下降的权衡。评估应同时对有效题和其被破坏的配对题分别打分，并区分模型是否识别缺陷与其报告的“已解/不可解”状态。

Abstract: Language models draft engineering calculations, but answer accuracy does not show whether they reject an impossible problem. We tested 14 models on 30 pairs of mechanics problems, each with a valid version and one made impossible by changing a given value or assumption. Two independent solvers verified every answer key and showed that each flawed problem was physically impossible. We scored solving of valid problems separately from rejection of their flawed counterparts. Each reply required a "solved" or "cannot solve" status; rejection meant "cannot solve" or withholding an answer. The initial prompts did not warn that problems could be flawed. Across three recent models, 12 of 90 replies failed to reject a flawed problem. In 11 of these replies, the model stated the flaw, answered a corrected problem and still reported the original as "solved", according to artificial intelligence raters and numerical checks. We later retested four models from one provider, offering "flawed" instead of "cannot solve" and asking them to name and explain the defect. Three models showed statistically significant increases in rejection, but valid-problem solving fell in three. Evaluations therefore need to score both versions and distinguish flaw recognition from the reported status.

</details>


### [226] [The Pushback Paradox: A Two-Probe Diagnostic for Language Model Compliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06673)
*Stefan Bühler,David Exler,Markus Reischl,Mark Schutera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一个评估模型“服从—抵抗”属性的双探针基准，用κ指数量化，并在12个模型上揭示了各自的安全/可控特征与权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量化语言模型对人类指令的顺从性与抗拒性，凸显在安全、部署与多智能体编排中不同模型的风险/可控性特征。

Method: 设计两个简单场景：主动探针（active probe），用户指示模型去做并接受较低回报以测量可被利用性；被动探针（passive probe），用户指示模型等待并放弃较高回报以测量可停止性。对十二个模型分别计算两类服从率并组合为合规指数 κ。

Result: 在测试的十二个模型中，大多数在两个探针上均倾向服从并以指令为理由。个别模型（如Claude Sonnet-4.6 与 Claude Opus-4.7）能被停止而不易被利用；Claude Opus-4.6与GPT-5-mini在两种探针下都表现出抵抗；没有发现“可被利用但不可停止”的模型。

Conclusion: 本文构建了一个可公开的“双探针”基准，用于将任意语言模型放在“服从—抵抗”光谱上；结论是不同模型在可被利用性（exploitability）与可停止性（stoppability）间存在权衡，且没有模型表现为“可被利用但不可停止”。

Abstract: Are language models compliant with user instructions? A model that always complies can be stopped but also exploited, while one that always resists can be neither exploited nor stopped. We contribute an open two-probe benchmark that can place any language model on this spectrum. In the active probe, a user instructs the model to act and accept a lower payoff, which measures exploitability. In the passive probe, the user instructs it to wait and give up a higher payoff, which measures stoppability. The two compliance rates combine into a compliance index $κ$. Applied to twelve language models, the benchmark shows that seven mostly follow the instruction in both probes and justify their action by pointing to the instruction. Only Claude Sonnet-4.6 and Claude Opus-4.7 can be stopped without being exploitable, Claude Opus-4.6 and GPT-5-mini resist both instructions, and no model is exploitable but unstoppable. Knowing where a language model sits on the compliance index $κ$ matters for human operators and for multi-agent systems, whether distributed or orchestrated.

</details>


### [227] [Conditional Rank Allocation for Taxonomy-Aware Medical Language Model Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06765)
*Guangyuan Dong,Ziwei Hong,Xuehao Zhou,Zidong Yu,Bingchen Liu,Kehan Liu,Chuang Liu,Rong Fu,Yuchao Hou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 ARBOR，通过条件选择共享低秩基底中的秩一原子实现每题定制化适配，在多套医疗QA基准上小幅超越 LoRA 和 MoELoRA，证据支持结构化条件适配，但临床安全性与部署尚未评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗问答覆盖多种专科和临床操作，不同问题可能需要不同的适配方向。固定的单一更新可能受限于有效秩，因而需要一种能按问题条件选择更新子空间的高效方法。

Method: 提出 ARBOR：一种参数高效的条件适配器。核心是从共享低秩基底中为每个问题选择若干秩一原子（rank-one components）；用加性门控将问题表示、专科标签、操作标签及其交互组合以决定选择；并用学习到的系数缩放适配器残差。外加设计的理论示例（正交且等概率的子任务）说明条件选择如何避免与固定更新相同活跃秩时出现的逼近下界。

Result: 在 Qwen3-8B 上、跨 CMB、CMExam、MedQA、MedMCQA 的五次种子实验中，ARBOR 的平均准确率为 69.69%，分别比 LoRA r16 与 MoELoRA 高 1.26 与 1.30 百分点。随着训练专科数量从 1 增至 7，ARBOR 相对于 LoRA r16 的优势从 0.08 点增至 1.94 点。通过标签扰动与原子掩蔽实验验证了临床路由的有用性；原子聚类与提供的专科标签对齐（调整兰德指数 0.62）。此外报告了校准、迁移能力以及测量的成本。局限为未对临床安全和实际部署做检验。

Conclusion: 作者得出结论：在医疗问答场景中，采用结构化的条件适配（通过为每个问题有条件选择共享低秩基底中的秩一分量）能超越固定低秩更新方法，从而提高不同专科与操作情境下的问答性能，但临床安全性与实际部署尚未验证。

Abstract: Medical question answering spans specialties and clinical operations that may benefit from different adaptation directions. We propose ARBOR, a parameter-efficient method that selects rank-one components from a shared low-rank basis for each question. An additive gate combines question representations, specialty tags, operation tags, and their interaction; a learned coefficient scales the adapter residual. An illustrative separation under orthogonal, equiprobable subtasks shows how conditional selection can avoid an approximation floor faced by a fixed update with the same active rank. This result motivates the design without asserting a corresponding bound for medical corpora. On Qwen3-8B across CMB, CMExam, MedQA, and MedMCQA, five-seed experiments yield 69.69% mean accuracy across benchmarks, exceeding LoRA r16 and MoELoRA by 1.26 and 1.30 percentage points, respectively. The reported advantage over LoRA r16 increases from 0.08 to 1.94 points as training expands from one to seven specialties. Tag perturbations and atom masking support the usefulness of clinical routing, while atom clusters align with the supplied specialty labels (adjusted Rand index 0.62). Calibration, transfer, and measured costs further characterize the method. These findings support structured conditional adaptation for medical QA, while leaving clinical safety and broader deployment untested.

</details>


### [228] [Back to the Future: Rethinking EDA Infrastructure for Agentic Systems in Chip Design Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06790)
*Je Yang,Ivan Lobov,Thomas Karpati*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BTTF 是一个将仿真转储结构化为 SQLite 并用多智能体将自然语言查询转成 schema-aware SQL 的端到端代理框架，解决了后仿真验证与波形调试自动化问题，在 150 条查询上达 95.33% 执行准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 在 EDA 领域多集中于静态 RTL 代码生成（约 74.6% 的工作），而后仿真验证与交互式波形调试仍主要依赖人工流程。为实现端到端、自治的验证流水线，需要将仿真数据结构化并让自然语言查询能直接驱动调试与追溯。

Method: 主要方法包括 (1) 将仿真波形与信号转储（unstructured simulation dumps）解析、清洗并归一化为关系型 SQLite 模式；(2) 构建一个多智能体编排引擎，负责将自然语言验证查询解析为库感知（schema-aware）的 SQL，并协调不同子任务（查询生成、异常定位、版本关联）；(3) 将检测到的信号异常与版本化的 RTL 仓库进行关联以定位可能的设计回归或错误版本。

Result: 在构建的 150 查询基准上，BTTF 达到 95.33% 的执行准确率（execution accuracy）。系统还展示了将信号异常与版本化 RTL 代码库关联以辅助定位回归的能力。

Conclusion: BTTF 提供了一个端到端的代理框架，通过将大规模、非结构化仿真转储归一化为关系型 SQLite 数据库，并结合多智能体编排将自然语言验证查询翻译为面向模式的 SQL，从而实现后仿真自动化验证与波形调试的闭环。该方法在 150 条查询基准上达到 95.33% 的执行准确率，表明其在自治 EDA 验证方面具有实用性。

Abstract: The unprecedented computational scale of modern artificial intelligence depends on complex, multi-billion-transistor Systems-on-Chip, yet the workflows that verify these chips remain stubbornly manual. Although Large Language Models (LLMs) have made rapid inroads into Electronic Design Automation (EDA), approximately 74.6% of existing studies target static Register-Transfer Level (RTL) code generation, leaving post-simulation verification and interactive waveform debugging largely untouched. We introduce Back-to-the-Future (BTTF), an end-to-end agentic framework that closes this infrastructural gap. BTTF distills massive, unstructured simulation dumps into a normalized relational SQLite database and couples it with a collaborative multi-agent orchestration engine that translates natural-language verification queries into schema-aware SQL while correlating signal anomalies with versioned RTL repositories. Across a 150-query benchmark, BTTF attains 95.33% execution accuracy, charting a practical path toward autonomous EDA verification.

</details>


### [229] [TasteVal: Measuring the Experimental Research Taste of AI Systems Against Human Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06824)
*Oliver Jaffe,Dane Sherburn*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: TasteVal 用“更少串行实验算力达到专家水平”来量化实验研究品味，结果显示近来模型在此指标上进步很快，但按最终性能衡量未出现类似突破。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一个可量化、可比较的工具，用以衡量模型在前沿 AI 研发中进行实验设计与结论推断的能力，并把这一能力作为影响 AI 进步速度的重要输入（即实验品味作为实验算力的放大器）。

Method: 构建 8 个开放式任务，要求模型作为 Researcher 设计迭代实验、解释结果；由固定的 Coder 代理实现实验并回报结果以隔离编程能力；在 40 H100 小时或 120 小时墙钟预算下运行；招募 24 名人类专家（每个任务至少 2 人），取每任务的最佳一次人类尝试为专家基线；评估 20 个 2023–2026 年发布的模型；使用“达到专家分数所需串行算力”定义算力乘数并计算置信区间。

Result: 最佳模型 Opus 5.5 超过专家基线，算力乘数 2.3×（95% CI 1.15–4.37），且其平均单次运行成本远低于基线；自 2025-12 起算力乘数大约每 3.0 个月翻倍（95% CI 1.7–5.0），而 2023–2025 年间为每 14 个月翻倍；但按归一化最终性能衡量，前沿模型的性能翻倍周期仍约为 14.6 个月。

Conclusion: TasteVal 提供了一个衡量前沿模型“实验研究品味”（experimental research taste）的基准，以计算效率（达到与人类专家相同分数所需的串行实验算力）来量化实验性研究品味。基准显示自 2025 年 12 月以来，前沿模型在 TasteVal 上的“算力乘数”（compute multiplier）增长迅速，最优模型已超过人类专家基线。

Abstract: We introduce TasteVal, a benchmark to evaluate the experimental research taste of frontier models. We define research taste as the ability to pick interesting problems to solve, design experiments, and interpret experimental results. TasteVal measures the experimental component of research taste; given a fixed research problem, we measure how well a model iteratively designs experiments and draws conclusions from their outcomes. We operationalize experimental research taste as compute efficiency; a Researcher who reaches the same score as an expert human using half the serial experimental compute has twice the experimental taste. Experimental taste thus acts as a multiplier on experimental compute, making it a key input to forecasts of AI progress. TasteVal consists of 8 novel, challenging, open-ended tasks representative of frontier AI R&D. To isolate taste from coding ability, the model under evaluation acts as a Researcher that iteratively designs experiments while a fixed Coder agent implements them and reports their results. The Researcher executes until either the 40 H100 hour or 120 wall-clock hour budgets are exhausted. We recruit 24 human experts, at least 2 per task, and take the best expert attempt per task as the expert baseline. We evaluate 20 models released between 2023 and 2026. The best-performing model, Opus 5.5, exceeds our expert baseline, with a compute multiplier of 2.3x (95% CI 1.15-4.37), at roughly 1/30 of our baseliners' average per-run cost. On TasteVal, the compute multiplier of frontier models has doubled approximately every 3.0 months since December 2025 (95% CI 1.7-5.0), up from every 14 months between 2023 and December 2025. Measured by final normalized performance, frontier models show no trend break, doubling every 14.6 months. To keep TasteVal uncontaminated, we do not release the tasks.

</details>


### [230] [BiasFlow: Geometric Monitoring and Backbone Regularization for Spurious Feature Reliance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06846)
*Haojin Deng,Zhiping Lin,Yimin Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BiasFlow提供了度量类-属性质心关系与特征投影敏感性的诊断，并提出BFR正则化以提高冻结骨干在偏置头重训练下的鲁棒性；实验证明可带来明显WGA提升，但诊断指标存在混淆与尺度依赖，属性信息并未被彻底移除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最差组准确率（WGA）只能评价已训练的预测器，不能表征在冻结骨干后重新训练新头时骨干特征空间的行为。需要可监控的几何诊断与可施加的正则化，验证骨干在遭遇偏置头训练时的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一个基于hook的监测工具BiasFlow，用以计算类-属性质心对齐度（IBMI）、类内质心分离度（W-IBMI）和特征投影敏感性；并提出BiasFlow Regularization（BFR），一种监督的、可组合的类条件质心对齐罚项。通过小规模基准（如UrbanCars）、冻结CelebA-Std骨干并在偏置数据上训练新头的压力测试，以及合成水印ImageNet实验评估工具与正则化的效果。

Result: 在报告的基准上加入BFR后，平均WGA被改进或保持，UrbanCars上提升高达+26.0个百分点。在主要压力测试中，冻结CelebA-Std骨干并训练新头时，BFR+GroupDRO将WGA从40.7%提升到64.1%，同时某属性（Male）探测准确率从92.5%降至72.2%；属性信息仍可恢复，跨任务结果参差不齐。合成水印ImageNet实验在匹配训练下将水印迁移准确率提高了+23.0个百分点。

Conclusion: BiasFlow及其正则化（BFR）在测试协议下有助于提升或保持整体和最差组准确率，当通过冻结骨干网络并在偏置数据上重新训练分类头时，能显著提高最差组表现。然而，IBMI与W-IBMI作为诊断指标存在混淆和尺度依赖性，不能单独证明属性信息已被移除；属性信息仍可被恢复，跨任务效果不一致。综上，应将质心几何与对偏置头重训练的抗性作为WGA之外的补充评估指标。

Abstract: Worst-group accuracy (WGA) evaluates a trained predictor but does not characterize how its frozen backbone behaves when a new head is learned. We introduce BiasFlow, a hook-based toolkit for monitoring class-attribute centroid alignment (IBMI), within-class centroid separation (W-IBMI), and feature-projection sensitivity. IBMI is confounded by class-attribute correlation and is not a measure of causal feature reliance. We pair these diagnostics with BiasFlow Regularization (BFR), a supervised, composable class-conditional centroid-alignment penalty. W-IBMI verifies the quantity BFR optimizes; it is scale dependent and does not independently establish attribute removal. Across the reported small-scale benchmarks, adding BFR improves or preserves mean WGA, with gains up to +26.0 pp on UrbanCars. The principal independent stress test freezes CelebA-Std backbones and trains fresh heads on biased data: BFR+GroupDRO improves WGA from 40.7% to 64.1%, while Male probe accuracy decreases from 92.5% to 72.2%. Attribute information remains recoverable, and cross-task results are mixed. A controlled synthetic-watermark ImageNet experiment additionally improves watermark-shift accuracy by +23.0 pp under matched training. These results support evaluating centroid geometry and resistance to biased head retraining alongside WGA, within the tested protocols.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [231] [Smells and Refactorings for Software Environmental Sustainability: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03838)
*Ornela Danushi,Flavio Sampirisi,Jacopo Soldani,Stefano Forti,Antonio Brogi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Systematic review (66 studies to Mar 2026) catalogues 20 "green smells" and maps each to refactorings, offering a practical catalogue and research starting point for sustainable software engineering.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Software often embeds structural inefficiencies that increase environmental impact; framing these as "green smells" enables targeted refactorings to reduce software's ecological footprint.

Method: A systematic literature review of 66 primary studies published up to March 2026, analysing and classifying works that discuss green smells and corresponding refactorings.

Result: Identification of 20 green smells affecting software sustainability, with each smell linked to one or more refactoring techniques claimed to mitigate environmental impact.

Conclusion: The literature shows active interest in addressing green smells; the identified catalogue of 20 smells and mapped refactorings offers pragmatic value for practitioners and a research foundation for sustainable software engineering.

Abstract: Context: The environmental impact of ICT is constantly growing. Enhancing its environmental sustainability requires dealing with energy-hungry software systems, by possibly reducing their footprint. Objective: To better frame the problem and its persistent nature, software can be viewed as carrying structural inefficiencies regardless of how advanced its functionality may be. Identifying these inefficiencies as "green smells" provides a basis for refactoring code to mitigate its ecological impact. We aim at identifying well-known green smells impacting environmental sustainable software and map them to corresponding refactoring aimed at reducing the impact therein. Method: In this article, we conduct a systematic literature review on the articles that discuss green smells and their possible refactorings. We analyse and classify 66 primary studies published up to March 2026. Results: We identify 20 smells impacting environmental sustainability of software. Each smell is associated with one or more refactoring techniques known to mitigate their negative effects on the environmental sustainability of software. Conclusions: The literature witnesses the interest in resolving the green smells affecting software systems. The catalogued green smells and the corresponding refactorings can provide pragmatic value for practitioners in their daily development workflows, while also offering researchers a starting point to establish new research directions in sustainable software engineering.

</details>


### [232] [Characterizing Open-Source Video Games from a Software Engineering Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03953)
*Henrique Mandelli Canella,Yohan Duarte,Cristiano Politowski,Vinicius Durelli,Andre Takeshi Endo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对 491 个 itch.io 上的开源游戏在 GitHub 上的元数据分析显示：题材集中、评分偏好、仓库多半低维护、以 Godot/Unity 为主、缺乏测试文档，结果对独立游戏开发者的工程实践改进具参考价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 独立与个人开发者在开源平台发布游戏并公开源码，这为实证软件工程提供了难得的可视化开发工件集合；理解这些项目的特征有助于改进面向小团队或个体的工程实践与工具支持。

Method: 从 itch.io 上筛选并匹配到 GitHub 的 491 个开源游戏项目，提取并分析其元数据（如题材、玩家评分、仓库活动日志、实现技术栈、测试与文档存在性等），对这些维度进行描述性统计与定性归纳。

Result: 主要发现包括：题材分布高度集中；玩家评分总体偏高但并非普适；大多数仓库维护稀少；绝大多数项目使用 Godot 或 Unity；测试相关文档/实践罕见。研究还指出了开源游戏作为研究语料的价值与局限。

Conclusion: 本研究表明：开源视频游戏在题材上集中在少数类型（益智、动作、平台类），当玩家评分存在时通常较高；仓库多数停滞或仅有最低维护；主流引擎为 Godot 或 Unity；与测试相关的文档与实践很少被记录。研究为为小团队/独立开发者定制的软件工程实践与工具设计提供了实证依据。

Abstract: Video games hold a prominent role in the entertainment industry, which constitutes a multibillion-dollar global market. Within this ecosystem, independent studios, small teams, and solo developers operate with constrained budgets while making their games available through platforms such as itch.io, where many projects are also available as open-source. Where even if the project is fully released, other developers can still have access to the development process through the source code, helping other developers to develop their own games. These open-source games also constitute a valuable corpus for empirical software engineering research, offering transparent access to development artifacts that are typically inaccessible in commercial projects. This study characterizes open-source video games from a Software Engineering standpoint, focusing on their genres, popularity, repository activity, implementation characteristics, and testing practices. To this end, we extracted metadata from 491 video games from itch.io, published as open-source projects in GitHub. The results indicate that open-source games predominantly fall within limited genres (Puzzle, Action, and Platformer), that player ratings, when available, tend to be high, bringing a perception that the community receives well those kind of games, that repositories are largely dormant or minimally maintained, that the majority of these projects adopt either Godot or Unity as their game engine, and that documentation of testing-related practices is seldom included. The results offer insights into the development of open-source video games and may contribute to advancing software engineering practices and tools designed for small teams and independent game developers.

</details>


### [233] [How Do Coding Agents Optimize Software and Report Performance Validation? A Large-Scale Empirical Study of Open-Source Pull Requests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.03969)
*Huiyun Peng,Ricardo Calvo,Kelechi G. Kalu,James C. Davis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 代理生成的性能PR在策略上愈发接近人类，但证据（定量、多维、可复现）仍不足，合并率较低；需要面向效果与成本的严格验证与评审机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鉴别自治AI编码代理在开源性能优化中的实践是否遵循既有优化方法，以及其所声称的性能提升是否由严格、可量化的证据支撑，从而评估代理在真实软件工程流程中的可靠性与价值。

Method: 基于MSR 2026 挖掘挑战的试点扩展，收集并分析截至2026年6月的1,130份代理（agentic）与1,130份人类作者的性能相关PR。比较两组的合并结果、补丁特征、采用的优化策略及验证行为，结合定量统计和定性编码分析时间趋势与实践差异。

Result: 主要发现：1) 代理PR合并率较低（54.4% vs. 73.3%）；2) 两组采用的优化策略相似，但代理更依赖静态推理、较少使用基准测试（代理46.1% vs. 人类57.2%），且基准使用率随时间接近一致；3) 代理在代码气味类重构上报告验证的频率与针对资源的改动相当，而人类作者对重构的验证频率较低；4) 在两组中，约半数标称“已验证”的PR并未提供定量性能指标；且相关权衡（如内存/延迟/吞吐）很少被量化；5) 总体上代理优化实践正趋向人类，但支撑性证据仍不充分，需改进评估基础设施与审查标准。

Conclusion: 代理生成的性能PR在优化策略上与人类开发者已高度趋同，但实证支持仍不充分——代理PR合并率明显低于人类（54.4% vs. 73.3%），验证往往缺乏量化和多维权衡的证据。为保证优化有效性，需要以量化、多维、可复现的验证作为评审与接受标准。

Abstract: Software performance optimization requires identifying bottlenecks and improvement opportunities, and empirically evaluating the effects and trade-offs across performance objectives. Autonomous AI coding agents submit performance-oriented pull requests (PRs) to open-source projects, yet it remains unclear whether these changes reflect established optimization practices and adequately substantiate their claimed value. Extending our MSR 2026 Mining Challenge pilot study of 407 performance PRs, we analyze a sample of 1,130 agentic and 1,130 human-authored performance PRs collected through June 2026. We compare adoption outcomes and patch characteristics between the two groups, and examine their optimization practices and validation behaviors. The results show that agentic PRs are merged less often than human-authored PRs (54.4% vs. 73.3%), but the two groups use similar optimization strategies and report validation at comparable rates. Agentic PRs rely more on static reasoning and less on benchmarks (46.1% vs. 57.2%), although benchmark use converges by the end of the study period. Agentic PRs also report validation for code-smell refactorings as often as for resource-targeting changes, whereas human-authored PRs do so less often. Across both groups, about half of validated PRs report no quantitative performance metric, and relevant trade-offs are rarely quantified. Agentic optimization practice has become increasingly similar to human practice, but the supporting evidence remains incomplete. Establishing whether agent-proposed optimizations are supported by rigorous, quantitative, and multidimensional evidence remains an important challenge. These findings motivate evaluation infrastructure and review criteria that measure intended effects and relevant costs rather than treating the presence of validation alone as sufficient.

</details>


### [234] [World Requirement Model: Learning Requirement-Change Consequences from Typed Artifact Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04445)
*Yuanpeng He,Lijian Li,Dongming Jin,Huanyao Zhang,Fangjing Li,Linyu Li,Chung-ju Huang,Tianxiang Zhan,Qingsong Wen,Wenpin Jiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: WRM: a typed artifact-graph world model with relation-aware attention improves consequence scoring for requirement changes versus MLP baselines, with consistent MAP gains across multiple comparisons, but has selection and architectural limitations.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Requirement changes affect connected stakeholders, constraints, components, and tests. Predicting these consequences across linked artifacts is needed to manage impact, conflicts, violations, and defect risk.

Method: Represent engineering context as a typed artifact graph; use relation-aware attention and typed propagation to contextualize nodes; learn world and decision representations to model dynamics; use shared readouts to score impact, conflict, violation, and defect risk; supervise additionally with successor adjacency and latent prediction objectives.

Result: On 28 scored cases from 282 synthetic cases in six domains, WRM achieves impact MAP 0.724 vs 0.623 for a hashed-text MLP (16.2% relative gain, paired difference 0.101, 95% CI [0.044,0.159]). Lowest-quarter mean AP improves by 32.4%, equal-domain MAP by 13.3%. Four 47-case comparisons on an expanded corpus show MAP gains of 15.6–29.1% and better means on all five reported metrics. Caveats: checkpoints selected on scored cases and unmatched backbones; linear impact head cannot rerank artifacts across decisions.

Conclusion: WRM shows that encoding a requirements engineering context as a typed artifact graph with relation-aware attention and typed propagation yields substantially better consequence prediction than a text-hashed MLP baseline, and it contributes a world-model formulation and evaluation conditions for requirement-world prediction.

Abstract: Requirement changes can affect connected stakeholders, constraints, components, and tests. We present World Requirement Model (WRM), which encodes this engineering context as a typed artifact graph and predicts consequences at shared artifact identifiers. Relation-aware attention and typed propagation contextualize nodes; world and decision representations support learned dynamics. Shared readouts score impact, conflict, violation, and defect risk; auxiliary objectives supervise successor adjacency and latent prediction. On 28 scored cases from 282 synthetic cases in six domains, WRM obtains impact mean average precision (MAP) of 0.724 versus 0.623 for a hashed-text multilayer perceptron (MLP), a 16.2\% relative gain and paired difference of 0.101 (conditional 95\% interval [0.044,0.159]). Lowest-quarter mean AP improves by 32.4\%, and equal-domain MAP by 13.3\%. Four 47-case comparisons on an expanded corpus show MAP gains of 15.6--29.1\% and higher means on all five reported metrics. The recorded advantage thus extends across score summaries and annotation/training settings. Checkpoints were selected on scored cases, and backbones are unmatched, so these results characterize selected systems. Our analysis establishes candidate-coverage bounds and shows that the current linear impact head cannot rerank a fixed world's artifacts across decisions. WRM contributes an artifact-addressed world-model formulation, comparative evidence for contextual consequence scoring, and explicit conditions for evaluating requirement-world prediction.

</details>


### [235] [Noise-Resilient Testing of Quantum Programs Based on Output Dominance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04461)
*Yuechen Li,Yige Yang,Tao Yue*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Qomino 无需单独噪声估计，通过比较期望输出与最强竞争观测并采用有界重试，在模拟 NISQ 噪声下实现高准确率、低延迟的量子软件测试判定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 NISQ 设备上，门错误、退相干与测量误差会扭曲输出，导致难以将程序缺陷与硬件噪声区分开来；现有噪声感知测试方法多依赖昂贵或后端特定的校准/建模，降低适用性，因此需要一种无需额外噪声估计且成本更低的判定方法。

Method: 提出基于分解感知的逆构造（decomposition-aware inverse construction），将期望输出与观测到的最强竞争者进行比较，并对不确定判定采用有界重试（bounded retries），以在不事先估计或缓解噪声的情况下直接从有噪声样本中判断测试结果。

Result: 在 6 个 Qiskit 程序的 30 个受控缺陷变体和 6 个模拟有噪声后端上评估，Qomino 在所有六个程序上总体准确率为 85.65%，在受限的两程序评估中达到 99.95%；与六种基线方法比较时，在给定设置下准确率与速度均显著优于量子特定与学习型方法；消融与重试分析支持主要组件的贡献。

Conclusion: Qomino 在无需单独噪声估计或消除阶段的前提下，通过优势（dominance）引导的判定策略，在有噪声观测样本上能有效区分程序缺陷与噪声，整体上比若干统计、量子特定与基于学习的方法表现更好，并在给定设置下达到更高的准确率和更快的运行速度。

Abstract: Quantum software testing (QST) is a central quality-assurance activity for checking whether quantum programs conform to their specifications. In the noisy intermediate-scale quantum (NISQ) era, gate, decoherence, and measurement errors distort observed outputs, making it difficult to distinguish program defects from noise. The existing noise-aware QST literature typically mitigates or models noise before applying a test oracle. However, these strategies can incur substantial cost and depend on calibration data, model training, or backend-specific information, limiting their applicability. We present Qomino, a dominance-guided QST approach for judging tests from noisy samples without a separate noise-estimation or mitigation phase. The approach combines decomposition-aware inverse construction, comparison of the expected outcome with the strongest observed competitor, and bounded retries for inconclusive decisions. We evaluate Qomino on 30 controlled buggy variants from six Qiskit programs and six simulated noisy backends, comparing it with six baselines spanning statistical, quantum-specific, and learning-based methods. Qomino achieves 85.65% accuracy over all six programs and 99.95% in the restricted two-program evaluation, significantly outperforming the corresponding baselines. Across the evaluated programs and backends under the stated settings, Qomino is faster than the compared quantum-specific and learning-based methods. Ablation and retry analyses support the contributions of its main components.

</details>


### [236] [DreamTest: World-Model Surrogates for Search-Based Testing of Deep Reinforcement Learning Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04494)
*Qinghua Xu,Guancheng Wang,Boxi Yu,Liting Lin,Lionel Briand*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: DreamTest用世界模型在模型内“想象”试验轨迹以预测失败并指导搜索，比黑箱替代器更能高效且多样地发现DRL代理在CPS中的失败。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有替代模型将系统视为黑箱并直接预测通过/失败，缺乏对轨迹级行为的建模，且在测试代价高昂的CPS中需要更样本高效且能捕捉失败多样性的测试方法。DreamTest旨在通过模拟“想象的”试验过程来提高失败预测准确性和发现不同类别失败的能力。

Method: 提出DreamTest：基于循环状态空间模型（recurrent state-space model, RSSM）从代理训练日志中学习代理行为与环境动态；对于候选测试配置，在模型中进行想象式回放（imagined rollouts）以生成失败评分，并用该评分引导搜索以优先验证高风险配置。

Result: 在Parking、Humanoid和DonkeyCar三项任务上，DreamTest在失败预测（AUPRC）上分别比最强基线提升97%、12%、39%，并在五个分布外测试集上分别取得最高145%、29%、44%的增益。在相同模拟器验证预算下，最佳DreamTest+搜索组合平均发现更多新颖失败（分别为29%、22%、79%）。在k=2–40的行为聚类范围内，DreamTest生成的失败在几乎所有k值下覆盖最多的行为簇，表明其能持续生成行为多样性的失败。

Conclusion: DreamTest通过学习代理行为与环境动力学的世界模型，在无需每次在模拟器或实系统执行的情况下，通过想象式轨 rollout 估计失败，从而更高效且更具多样性地发现DRL代理在网络物理系统（CPS）中的失败。

Abstract: Testing deep reinforcement learning (DRL) agents in cyber-physical systems aims to uncover diverse failures before deployment, but each execution can be expensive. Surrogate-assisted testing reduces this cost by learning to predict which test configurations are likely to fail. Prior surrogates treat the system as a black box and predict pass or fail outcomes directly; we instead model how a test unfolds and estimate failure from an imagined episode.
  We introduce DreamTest, a world-model surrogate for testing DRL agents. DreamTest adapts a recurrent state-space model to learn agent behaviour and environment dynamics from the agent's training log. Given a candidate configuration, imagined rollouts produce a failure score that guides search without executing every candidate in a simulator or real system.
  We evaluate DreamTest for failure prediction, test generation, and failure diversity on Parking, Humanoid, and DonkeyCar. Mean area under the precision-recall curve (AUPRC) exceeds the strongest baseline by 97%, 12%, and 39%, respectively, and gains on five out-of-distribution test sets reach 145%, 29%, and 44%. Under the same simulator-validation budget, the best "DreamTest + search" combinations find 29%, 22%, and 79% more novel failures on average. Across clusterings with k = 2-40, failures generated with DreamTest cover the most behavioural clusters for almost all k, indicating that DreamTest consistently discovers behaviourally diverse failures.

</details>


### [237] [Practical Runtime Enforcement of First-Order Temporal Requirements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04611)
*François Hublet,David Basin,Srđan Krstić*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: EnfFlash compiles a useful MFOTL fragment to imperative programs and interprets them, providing order-of-magnitude faster runtime enforcement for complex temporal policies, enabling practical enforcement of large regulations like the GDPR.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Existing enforcement mechanisms for rich temporal requirements are too slow for production use, forcing developers to use non-temporal or ad-hoc solutions that do not scale. There is a need for a practical enforcer that supports obligations and other temporal constructs while keeping runtime overhead low enough for interactive systems.

Method: (1) Identify a computationally tractable fragment of MFOTL sufficient for many real-world temporal requirements; (2) design an enforcement algorithm that compiles formulas in this fragment into imperative programs; (3) implement the algorithm in the EnfFlash tool that interprets those programs efficiently; (4) evaluate performance against EnfGuard on standard benchmarks and on security policies for LLM agents, and integrate EnfFlash into web applications for end-to-end measurement.

Result: EnfFlash achieves up to 44x lower latency than EnfGuard on standard benchmarks and up to three orders of magnitude lower latency on LLM-agent security policies. It can enforce a 400-line MFOTL GDPR specification on a social network with <15 ms added latency per page view, demonstrating feasibility for real-world interactive applications.

Conclusion: The paper introduces EnfFlash, an efficient runtime enforcement approach that compiles a practically expressive fragment of Metric First-Order Temporal Logic (MFOTL) into imperative programs and interprets them, enabling low-latency enforcement of complex temporal policies (including obligations). Empirically, EnfFlash outperforms prior work (EnfGuard) by large margins and can enforce large policies such as a 400-line GDPR formula within latency bounds suitable for interactive web applications.

Abstract: Runtime enforcers observe a system's behavior and exert control over it to ensure that the system always adheres to its requirements. Many natural requirements not only formulate restrictions on the system's present behavior, but also impose obligations on its future behavior; for instance, agentic security may require personal data collected during a run to be erased by some deadline. In an ever-growing compliance landscape, real-world systems may be subject to hundreds of such requirements. However, existing enforcement mechanisms supporting complex policies are often too slow for production software; instead, developers may resort to non-temporal access control mechanisms and ad-hoc instrumentation, but these scale poorly with requirement complexity.
  To address this problem, we introduce an efficient algorithm and tool for enforcing complex temporal requirements and demonstrate its performance. Specifically, we identify a fragment of Metric First-Order Temporal Logic (MFOTL) that can be enforced with low runtime complexity while supporting a rich family of practically relevant requirements, including obligations. We then design an enforcement algorithm for this fragment and implement it in EnfFlash, a novel tool that enforces requirements with latency up to 44x lower than EnfGuard, the previous state-of-the-art enforcer, on standard benchmarks, and up to three orders of magnitude lower on security policies for LLM agents. This performance is achieved by compiling requirements into imperative programs that we efficiently interpret. We evaluate EnfFlash both standalone, on existing benchmarks, and integrated in web applications as an enforcement backend. We demonstrate that it can enforce a substantial 400-line MFOTL formula specifying the GDPR on a social network while adding under 15 ms to each page view, which is sufficient for most interactive and real-time applications.

</details>


### [238] [When Talk Isn't Code: Comparing LLM Agents That Simulate and Develop Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04639)
*Xiang Li,Aichun Huang,Mingming Zhang,Zheli Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次将知识探测、模拟对话（S-mode）与生成并测试代码（D-mode）放在同一框架与攻击套件下比较：S-mode能快速筛查多数漏洞但不能代替对生成程序的实际攻击测试；模型能背诵检查但常不在生成代码中实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作分别从模型回答/对话和从模型生成代码两方面评估LLM在网络协议实现与蜜罐场景中的安全性，但没有把三者（探测、模拟、生成）对比起来；作者认为单独的口头或对话评估可能会高估安全性，因此需要统一比较。

Method: 设计并实现一个统一的评估框架（harness），使用同一攻击套件和同一oracle来分别评估三种模式：知识探测（问模型需要哪些安全检查）、模拟对话模式（S-mode，模型在会话中扮演实现）和开发模式（D-mode，模型生成程序并对程序实施攻击）。在四种协议实现上对15个LLM进行评测。

Result: 在包含完整安全规范时，中位模型在重组、HTTP、和防火墙实现上的攻击成功率在两种模式下均不超过4%；但在DNS上中位攻击成功率为S-mode 13%、D-mode 15%。在D-mode下，向规范中添加缺失的DNS检查能把某攻击从100%降到34%，而删去一个规则会使程序在其它检查上也变弱。提前询问模型不能预测代码是否实现了检查（例如14个会写DNS解析器的模型中有13个能口头列出该检查，但所有42个程序都缺失）。S-mode能标记82%的真实DNS漏洞并在96%的安全情况不误报，因而可作第一道筛查，但存在过报与漏报。总体结论是S-mode可筛查但不能替代D-mode；应把安全需求成文。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：让大模型在对话中‘扮演’实现（S-mode）可以作为快速筛查工具，但不能替代让模型实际生成并测试程序（D-mode）；模型口头列出的安全检查并不能可靠预测其生成的代码是否实现了这些检查，因而即便模型能复述安全要求，依然需要把安全规范写下来并对生成代码做实际攻击测试。

Abstract: LLMs are used both to simulate network protocol implementations, as honeypots do, and to write them. Prior work evaluates the two uses separately, from a model's answers or conversations in one case and from its generated code in the other. We observe that a knowledge probe (asking the model which security checks an implementation needs) and a conversation can credit security checks that the generated program lacks, but no study has compared them with the code the same model writes. To fill this gap, we present the first such comparison between simulation mode (S-mode), where the model plays the implementation in a conversation, and development mode (D-mode), where the model writes the program and the program is attacked, with a knowledge probe as a baseline. We design and implement a harness that judges all three with one attack suite and one oracle, and evaluate 15 LLMs on four protocol implementations. We find that with the full security specification, the median model's attack success is at most 4\% on reassembly, HTTP, and firewall implementations in both modes, but 13\% in S-mode and 15\% in D-mode on DNS. In D-mode, adding the missing DNS requirement to the specification cuts that attack from 100\% to 34\% and changes only that check, whereas deleting a stated rule weakens the program on other checks too. Asking the model beforehand does not predict which checks a program has, since 13 of the 14 models that write a DNS resolver name the check, yet all 42 programs lack it. S-mode is a useful first filter, flagging 82\% of the real DNS vulnerabilities and leaving 96\% of the safe cases unflagged. It errs in both directions, over-reporting security checks that D-mode does not implement and under-reporting checks that D-mode does. S-mode can screen but not replace D-mode testing, and security requirements should be written down even when a model can recite them.

</details>


### [239] [Back to the Future: Regressing Readability Features from LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04641)
*Tairan Wang,Earl T. Barr*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: BTTF用信息论+语义+传统度量的可解释特征配合线性回归，在可读性评分上兼顾性能、变换鲁棒性与解释性，适合低资源或需可解释性的场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可读性模型在不同数据集上与人类评价不一致，需要一种既可解释又对变换鲁棒的模型，尤其在AI编码代理生成或维护代码时，能帮助人类与代理理解与审查输出。

Method: 构建混合特征集（传统代码度量、基于嵌入的语义组织、基于因果语言模型的可预测性/惊讶度），用线性回归学习可读性分数；在五个公开基准与一个手工标注的开发集（共1,100样本）以及13种可读性削弱变换上评估，与多种基线（本地与云LLM、Dorn）比较。

Result: 在基准上平均Spearman相关较最强基线提升最多0.101；在13种变换上对生产代码的响应率Java为93.8%、Python为87.6%，分别比Dorn高14%和19.2%；在本地模型中最佳，仅比最强云LLM落后1.7%（Java）和5.1%（Python）；特征系数方向与理论一致；直接LLM基线提示成本高且不可解释。

Conclusion: BTTF是一种将线性回归与信息论特征结合的方法，用语言模型作为测量工具而非黑盒评判器，从而在可解释性与鲁棒性上提升代码可读性评估性能。

Abstract: Code readability supports software understanding, review, and maintenance. As AI coding agents become more widely used, readable code helps both humans review agent-generated output and agents maintain code within limited context windows. Yet existing readability models do not consistently agree with human judgements across datasets.
  We introduce BTTF (Back To The Future), pairing linear regression with information-theoretic features. Rather than using language models as black-box judges, BTTF uses them as measurement instruments. It combines traditional code measurements, embedding-based semantic organisation, and causal-LM predictability to capture source structure, semantic coherence, and contextual surprise.
  We evaluate BTTF on five publicly available human-rated readability benchmarks and our manually annotated MBJP development set, totalling 1,100 samples. BTTF improves average Spearman correlation over the strongest baseline by up to 0.101. Across 13 controlled readability-reducing transformations on production code, it achieves response rates of 93.8% in Java and 87.6% in Python, outperforming Dorn by 14% and 19.2%. It performs best among local models and trails the strongest cloud-LLM response by 1.7% in Java and 5.1% in Python. The direct-LLM baselines require an average of 1,083 prompt tokens per evaluation while remaining opaque. BTTF combines state-of-the-art benchmark performance and strong transformation robustness with feature-granular interpretability: every feature coefficient matches its theoretical directional effect across all evaluated model configurations.

</details>


### [240] [RETRACE: From Entangled Repair Histories to Reusable Experience for CI Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04658)
*Rabeya Khatun Muna,Muhammad Ahasanuzzaman,Nakhla Rafi,Yisen Xu,Jinqiu Yang,Tse-Hsun Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过恢复问题—更改对齐，RETRACE 将混乱的 CI 历史转化为可检索的修复经验，显著提升 LLM 驱动的自动修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实 PR 常含多重失败、撤销和无关改动，缺乏问题—改动对齐，阻碍从历史中学习可复用修复经验。

Method: 结合端点视角（自通过修订向后推理）与开发视角（沿提交历史前向追踪），用 CI 执行证据调和两视角，并将经验抽象为三层（具体修复—抽象模式）以便检索和重用。

Result: 在 CI-REPAIR-BENCH（565 个 PR，101 个仓库，12 类失败）上，使 mini-SWE-agent Pass@1 从 19.6% 提升至 31.9%（MiniMax-M2.5），从 23.3% 提升至 32.8%（DeepSeek-V4-Flash）；Codex 在子集上从 15.5% 提升至 27.5%。

Conclusion: RETRACE 能从混杂的软件历史中恢复问题到更改的对齐，从而使历史 CI 修复成为可复用经验并显著提高 LLM agent 的修复成功率。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents increasingly reuse prior experience, but most approaches assume that problems and solutions are already aligned. Software histories rarely provide this alignment: a pull request (PR) may contain multiple continuous integration (CI) problems, failed attempts, reverted edits, and unrelated changes, obscuring which changes resolve each problem. We present RETRACE, a framework for reconstructing problem-level repair experience from such histories. RETRACE combines an endpoint view that reasons backward from changes retained in the passing revision with a development view that traces repair evolution forward through commit history. CI execution evidence reconciles the two views, and the recovered experience is represented at three abstraction levels, from concrete fixes to transferable repair patterns. For new failures, RETRACE retrieves relevant problem-level experience to guide repair. On CI-REPAIR-BENCH, comprising 565 PR-level repairs from 101 repositories across 12 failure categories, RETRACE improves mini-SWE-agent Pass@1 from 19.6% to 31.9% with MiniMax-M2.5 and from 23.3% to 32.8% with DeepSeek-V4-Flash. On a matched subset, Codex improves from 15.5% to 27.5%. Combining both views consistently outperforms either alone, showing that recovering problem-change alignment enables historical CI repairs to serve as reusable repair experience.

</details>


### [241] [Towards Automatically Pruning Logging Code with Coding Agents: How Far Are We?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04716)
*He Yang Yuan,Haonan Zhang,Xin Wang,An Ran Chen,Kisub Kim,Zishuo Ding,Zhenhao Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: First systematic study of logging-code removal: created LogRem (387 cases), catalogued 10 patterns/11 reasons, and found current coding agents often produce valid edits but infrequently match developers’ exact removals; contextual cues (commit messages, discussions) help, and accurate scope detection is key for reliable automation.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Excessive logging creates noise, runtime overhead, and diagnostic obstruction; prior work focused on logging generation/modification, while removal is underexplored. The paper aims to characterize developer removal practices and assess whether coding agents can perform this maintenance task reliably.

Method: Extracted and manually validated real-world logging-removal cases from Python and Java repositories; derived 10 removal patterns and 11 removal reasons; constructed the LogRem dataset with 387 cases covering direct logging statement removal, logging infrastructure removal, and logging replacement; evaluated four coding agents under multiple model settings; compared agent outputs to accepted real-world changes and analyzed validity, alignment, error types, runtime cost, and effects of additional context (commit messages, developer discussions, taxonomy guidance).

Result: Agent outputs are mostly syntactically/compilationally valid (95.6%–100.0%), but only 11.1%–19.6% remove the same logging code as real changes while preserving unrelated code. Agents exhibit missed removals, extra removals, and unrelated edits with variation across removal categories and trajectories. Execution cost varies widely without consistent correlation to alignment. Commit messages and developer discussions yield the largest alignment improvements; providing taxonomy guidance consistently reduces runtime.

Conclusion: Logging removal is a distinct and nontrivial software-maintenance task; automated coding agents can produce syntactically valid edits but rarely match developers’ exact removals while preserving unrelated code. Reliable automation requires accurately determining the removal scope and preserving necessary code. The study is the first to characterize logging removal patterns and evaluate coding agents on this task.

Abstract: Logging code supports debugging, monitoring, and software maintenance, but excessive logging can add noise, impose runtime overhead, and obscure diagnostic information. While prior research has extensively studied logging code generation and modification, logging removal remains comparatively underexplored. In this paper, we study developer logging removal practices and explore the use of coding agents for this task. We extract and manually validate logging removal cases from Python and Java repositories and derive 10 removal patterns and 11 removal reasons that characterize how and why logging code was removed in real-world software changes. We further construct LogRem, a dataset of 387 real-world cases covering direct logging statement removal, logging infrastructure removal, and logging replacement. We evaluate four coding agents with multiple model settings and compare their outputs with accepted real-world changes. Although 95.6% to 100.0% of outputs pass validity checks, only 11.1% to 19.6% remove the same logging code as the corresponding real-world change while preserving unrelated code. Agents differ through missed removals, extra removals, and unrelated code edits, with substantial variation across logging removal categories and trajectories. Execution cost varies widely, but higher cost does not consistently yield closer alignment. Commit messages and developer discussions provide the largest alignment gains, while taxonomy guidance consistently reduces runtime. Overall, our study establishes logging removal as a distinct software maintenance task and shows that reliable automation depends on accurately determining removal scope while preserving necessary code. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first study to examine logging code removal from this perspective.

</details>


### [242] [Asymmetric Repository Lineage Modeling and Verifier-Guided Coordination in Concurrent AI Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04779)
*Arjun Subramanian,George Xu,Nithilan Karthik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 MERGEGYM 以及基于谱系/决策时特征的轻量 triage 流程，在文本合并冲突检测与有限回放预算下能高效恢复大部分冲突；补丁重构证实很多零重叠案例源于三方合并基线不一致，但结论局限于文本可合并性与冻结标签的调度目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发 AI 代理会生成相互冲突的更改，廉价信号（如作者声称某属性成立）可能误导优先级决策，而唯一可靠的判定需要可执行的检查器/回放。论文旨在研究如何在成本受限的情形下用轻量判别器或规则去三角化、决定何时启动昂贵的可执行检查器，从而在工程上实现可行的冲突筛选与调度策略。

Method: 构建并公开 MERGEGYM 三轨基准：开放时间范围预测（T1）、回放-条件冲突解决（T2）、调度（T3）；在分层抽样的 715 对谱系数据（其中 167 个文本冲突）上评估。方法包括：1) 简单的谱系并集规则；2) 基于 12 个决策时特征的逻辑回归与随机森林；3) 零-shot 与加元数据的 LLM 方案；4) 补丁重构以验证零重叠冲突的三方合并解释；通过保留集计算 AUROC/PR-AUC、在限定回放预算下的冲突恢复率与调度上的 de-overlap 与 makespan 代价来衡量效果。

Result: 关键数值：在 715 对谱系（167 文本冲突）中，有 79 个（47.3%）冲突发生于作者改动文件集合不重叠的情况。谱系并集规则在 held-out AUROC=0.877；12 特征逻辑回归 AUROC=0.882（置信区间 [0.845,0.917]），PR-AUC=0.661；未调参随机森林 AUROC=0.902（[0.875,0.928]），PR-AUC=0.701。以 33.3% 的回放预算，逻辑回归与随机森林恢复冲突分别为 81.2% 与 82.9%。在 79 个零重叠冲突中，补丁重构对 48 个成功并全部变为可合并，剩余 31 个结论不定。T1：零-shot LLM AUROC=0.704，LLM+元数据 0.740。T3：决策时 gate 在冻结标签回放下中位数去重叠率 91.7%，相应中位 makespan 膨胀 65.0%。回放本身在日志中很便宜（中位 0.02s），因此当回放足够便宜时应尽量全部验证。论文限定所有保证仅针对文本可合并性或明确的冻结标签调度目标。

Conclusion: 本文表明：在并发 AI 编码代理的场景下，针对属性的验证合约与三轨基准（MERGEGYM）可以有效划分并取样冲突、并通过决策时间特征和简单规则对是否需要可执行检查器进行三方合并式回放做出高效三角化。线谱并集（lineage-union）规则即为强基线；基于决策时特征的传统模型（逻辑回归、随机森林）能在 AUROC/PR-AUC 上进一步提升；补丁重构实验证实了许多“零重叠”冲突确实由三方合并基线不一致引起。论文也明确限定其结论仅适用于文本可合并性或冻结标签的调度目标。

Abstract: Concurrent AI coding agents create a coordination problem in which cheap signals may prioritize work, but only an executable checker can establish the property being claimed. We study this separation through a property-scoped verification contract and MERGEGYM, a three-track benchmark for open-time scope forecasting, replay- conditioned conflict resolution, and scheduling. On a stratified 715-pair lineage set (167 textual conflicts), 79 conflicts (47.3%) occur despite disjoint authored file sets. This is an operationally important proxy mismatch expected from three-way merge: authored PR diffs are measured against PR-specific bases, whereas the checker compares both heads to their common merge base. A standalone lineage-union rule reaches held-out AUROC 0.877; a 12-feature logistic model reaches 0.882 [0.845, 0.917] with PR-AUC 0.661, and an untuned random forest on the same decision-time features reaches 0.902 [0.875, 0.928] with PR-AUC 0.701. At a 33.3% held-out replay budget, the logistic and random-forest models recover 81.2% and 82.9% of conflicts, respectively. Patch reconstruction succeeds for 48/79 zero- overlap conflicts and all 48 become clean; the other 31 cases are inconclusive, so this check validates the expected three-way-merge explanation rather than claiming a new Git mechanism. In T1, a zero-shot LLM reaches AUC 0.704 and LLM-plus- metadata fusion 0.740. In T3, a decision-time gate de-overlaps a median 91.7% of labeled scope collisions at 65.0% makespan inflation under frozen-label replay. Because local git merge-tree replay is already cheap in our logs (median 0.02 s), we do not claim that lineage triage saves this checker alone: when exact replay is cheap, verify everything. All empirical guarantees in this paper remain limited to textual mergeability or the explicitly stated frozen-label scheduling target.

</details>


### [243] [Agent Skill Evolution: How Revisions Affect Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04832)
*Jiajie Wang,Yutong Zhao,Tianlin Li,Huashan Chen,Jinfu Chen,Kebin Peng,Sen He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Revising SKILL.md rules—especially by naming commands/paths—meaningfully improves LLM agent compliance and correctness with modest token overhead; lazy loading saves tokens while preserving a substantial fraction of behavioral gains.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SKILL.md files guide LLM coding agents and are revised like code, but the causal effect of those revisions on agent behavior and operational cost is unknown. The paper aims to quantify how Skill revisions affect compliance, actions, correctness, and token cost.

Method: Assemble 2,608 first/last revision pairs from 3,159 SKILL.md files; characterize evolution and co-changes with harness files. Identify ‘‘rule changes’’ (automatically checkable additions/removals). Measure impact on 21 models for single answers and on four agents in a sandbox; assess cost in tokens on 20 models and four agents. Blind-judge assessments used for final correctness on three agents.

Result: 55% of revisions change a rule/procedure. Commits that edit Skills more often modify harness files (e.g., CLAUDE.md). For 16 open-weight models, adding a rule increases single-answer compliance by +0.41 on average. Across four agents, required-action rate rises by +0.23 on average (+0.16 to +0.36); for three agents judged blind, final correctness increases by +0.10 on average (+0.06 to +0.14). With real-tool lazy loading, agents retain ~51% of action gains and open models ~38%. A revision adds 18–19% input tokens to a single answer; no detectable token cost per agent episode from revision alone, but loading a Skill body increases episode tokens by ~50% on average.

Conclusion: Skill revisions that add or remove checkable rules substantially change agent behavior: most revisions alter rules or procedures, and adding a named command/path in a Skill raises model compliance and agent action/correctness rates, while incurring modest token costs. Lazy loading of Skill bodies preserves part of the benefit while reducing token overhead.

Abstract: Agent Skills, the SKILL.md files that tell an LLM coding agent how a project works, are revised like code, yet what a revision does to the agent is unknown. From 2,608 first/last revision pairs of 3,159 Skills, we characterize how Skills evolve and how they change together with the configuration of the agent's harness. We then focus on rule changes, revisions that add or remove a rule we can check automatically, such as "run allium check". We measure their effect on 21 models in single answers and on four agents in a sandbox, and their cost on 20 of these models and the four agents. Most revisions (55%) change a rule or procedure, and commits that revise a Skill change harness files such as CLAUDE.md more often than other commits of the same size. Across 16 open-weight models, an added rule raises compliance in a single answer by +0.41 on average. Across the four agents, the rate at which the agent takes the required action rises by +0.23 on average (+0.16 to +0.36), and for the three agents that blind judges assessed, final correctness rises by +0.10 on average (+0.06 to +0.14). The gain comes mainly from rules that name a command or path the old Skill did not mention. Real tools load a Skill's body only when the agent decides it needs it. In that setting the four agents keep about half of the action gain on average (51%), and the three open models about 38%. A revision adds 18-19% input tokens to a single answer and no detectable cost to an agent episode, while loading a Skill's body raises the tokens of an episode by 50% on average.

</details>


### [244] [CARET: Training-Free Test-Time Scaling for Repository-Level Code Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04837)
*Jiajie Wang,Yutong Zhao,Kebin Peng,Sen He,Qing Guo,Tianlin Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: CARET 是一个无需训练的 RAG 改进方案，利用样本一致性做检索路由和反向似然做候选打分，在接近单次生成的预算下显著提升跨文件代码补全的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 RAG（检索-生成）方法多将重点放在改进检索，生成阶段常用一次性贪心解码，但生成在测试时尺度扩展（采样多个候选并自评分）能带来额外收益；探索在预算接近单次生成的条件下同时改进检索与生成以提升补全质量。

Method: CARET 在检索阶段根据模型自采样的一致性在检索上下文间进行路由，当样本散布时级联到备选上下文；在生成阶段使用缓存的提示前缀对候选进行采样以避免重复编码长检索上下文，并通过反向上下文似然（reverse-context likelihood）对候选进行打分，选择能让游标之后代码更可能的完成。

Result: 在多个跨文件代码补全基准（CrossCodeEval、RepoEval-Line、RepoEval-API）和六个不同规模/族的代码模型上，CARET 在 18 个组合上均优于贪心解码，平均 exact match 提升 10.8 个百分点，相较 self-consistency@10 平均提升 5.3 个百分点；额外的 token 级别计算约为 1.45 倍（实际墙钟 1.3–2.9 倍，随模型增大而增大）。

Conclusion: 本文提出了训练零样本的 CARET 方法，通过在检索与生成两个阶段做协同优化，在不大幅增加计算预算的情况下显著提高库级代码补全的准确率。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) dominates repository-level code completion: it retrieves cross-file context (R), then decodes one greedy completion (G). Existing work mainly focuses on retrieval and stops there. We argue both stages can be improved together, with generation in particular gaining from test-time scaling. We present CARET, a training-free method. For R, CARET routes among retrieval contexts using the agreement among its own samples, cascading to an alternative context when the samples scatter. For G, it samples candidates over a cached prompt prefix, so the long retrieved context is encoded once rather than once per sample. It then selects the final completion by reverse-context likelihood: a correct completion makes the code after the cursor more probable, so the same model grades its own candidates by reading ahead. Across CrossCodeEval, RepoEval-Line, and RepoEval-API with six code models (1.1B to 7B, four families), CARET improves exact match in all 18 combinations by 10.8 points on average over greedy decoding and 5.3 over self-consistency@10. Token-level compute stays near 1.45 times one generation (measured wall-clock 1.3-2.9 times, growing with generator size). Improving retrieval and generation together yields more accurate code than improving retrieval alone, at a budget that stays close to a single pass.

</details>


### [245] [Complex Agents, Shallow Tests: Demystifying and Enhancing Test Adequacy of Agent Harness in the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04921)
*Yifan Xiong,Jingyi Ge,Zhenpeng Chen,Yiling Lou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Agent harness code is under-tested; introducing contract-aware test generation (HarnessTester) yields large coverage and mutation-score gains and uncovers many real harness bugs, improving agent system reliability.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: As agent harnesses become more complex and central to agent behavior, implementation bugs in the harness threaten agent reliability. Despite this, LDH code that interprets LLM outputs and drives agent logic appears to receive limited testing. The paper aims to quantify this gap and provide a targeted testing solution.

Method: First, an empirical study measured test coverage (line/branch) and testing adequacy of real-world agent harnesses, focusing on LDH code. Then, the paper proposes HarnessTester, a test-generation technique that encodes agent–harness contracts to create contract-faithful test setups and systematically exercise LDH code. Evaluation compares HarnessTester against state-of-the-art general-purpose test generators using metrics such as line/branch coverage, mutation score, and real bug detection in popular agentic systems.

Result: HarnessTester achieves substantially higher coverage and mutation-score improvements than general-purpose generators (75.95%/84.76% larger line/branch coverage gains; 69.89% larger mutation-score gains). It detected 122 real-world harness bugs across widely used agentic systems, 88 previously unknown and 69 confirmed by developers, demonstrating practical effectiveness in improving reliability.

Conclusion: Agent harnesses in LLM-based agentic systems are substantially undertested, particularly LLM-dependent harness (LDH) code, and a harness-oriented test generation approach (HarnessTester) that enforces agent-harness contracts can greatly improve test adequacy and find real-world bugs.

Abstract: LLM-based agentic systems are emerging as a new software paradigm. Modern agents are typically composed of backbone LLMs and a surrounding harness that serves as the operational software infrastructure for agent execution. As agent harnesses grow increasingly complex, agents suffer from diverse harness implementation bugs, raising substantial reliability concerns. In this work, we conduct the first empirical study to systematically investigate the test adequacy of harness in real-world agentic systems. Our analysis reveals that agent harness remains substantially undertested. In particular, LLM-dependent harness (LDH) code, despite its critical role in processing LLM outputs and governing agent behavior, receives limited testing attention, with less than half of its lines and branches covered by existing tests. Motivated by these findings, we further propose HarnessTester, the first harness-oriented test generation technique that incorporates explicit agent-harness contract support to construct contract-faithful test setups and extensively exercise LDH code. Our evaluation shows that HarnessTester substantially outperforms state-of-the-art general-purpose test generation techniques in achieving 75.95%/84.76% larger line/branch coverage gains and 69.89% larger mutation-score gains. Furthermore, HarnessTester detects 122 real-world harness bugs in widely-used agentic systems (e.g., OpenClaw), among which, 88 bugs are previously-unknown bugs and 69 bugs have been confirmed by agent developers. These results highlight the practical effectiveness of HarnessTester in improving test adequacy and assuring the reliability of real-world agentic systems.

</details>


### [246] [Assembling Insights for Agentic Machine Learning Engineering Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04927)
*Bihui Jin,Yinxi Li,Kaiyuan Wang,Pengyu Nie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: A systematic benchmark and insight-assembly agent quantify how curated insights from related Kaggle competitions boost agentic MLE; careful leakage controls and controlled experiments reveal complementary insight types and practical guidance on injection modes.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Agentic MLE systems reuse knowledge from other MLE tasks but prior insight collection/injection is ad hoc and evaluations risk data leakage; need systematic study of insight effects, types, and injection methods under controlled conditions.

Method: Construct MLE-InsightBench (160 Kaggle competitions, 12 domains, one holdout per domain), build MLE-InsightForge to extract three insight types (domain, competition, improvement) from sources while avoiding leakage, and inject insights as on-demand skills or upfront playbooks; evaluate performance improvements and run controlled experiments across LLM backends.

Result: Insights improve normalized private-test scores by 123.9% over no-insight baseline; three insight types are complementary; injection mode effectiveness is task-dependent; skills are generally more cost-effective; results hold across different LLM backends.

Conclusion: Paper shows that curated insights from related MLE tasks significantly improve agentic MLE performance and that insight type and injection method both matter; skills are more cost-effective overall, and gains generalize across back-end LLMs.

Abstract: Agentic machine learning engineering (MLE) is an emerging AI4SE application for complex ML tasks and a step toward recursive self-improvement of AI systems. Recent agentic MLE systems show the value of leveraging insights from related MLE tasks: some systems condition code generation on expert domain knowledge, which is implicitly curated from peer MLE tasks; some systems have a loop of solving an MLE task, gathering memory to benefit future tasks. However, insight collection and injection remain ad hoc, and existing evaluations often do not control insight sources, making data leakage a threat to validity.
  To close these gaps, we systematically study how insights from related MLE tasks affect agentic MLE systems, and how their impact depends on insight type and injection method. We introduce MLE-InsightBench, a benchmark of 160 Kaggle competitions across 12 domains. To prevent leakage, one competition per domain is held out as the target, while the remaining 148 serve as sources only when they cannot have been influenced by the target task, such as through later editions or reused solutions. We also develop MLE-InsightForge, an insight assembly agent that explores source competitions, top human solutions, and writeups to construct three insight types: 12 domain insights summarizing best practices, 148 competition insights describing winning solutions, and 16 improvement insights capturing generic debugging and optimization strategies. These insights are injected either as on-demand skills or as an upfront playbook. In our evaluation, the extracted insights improve the normalized private-test score by 123.9% over a no-insight baseline. Controlled experiments further show that the three insight types are complementary; the better injection mode (skill vs. playbook) varies by tasks, with skills more cost-effective overall; and the gains generalize across backend LLMs.

</details>


### [247] [TeleGen: Improving LLM-Based Web Application Generation via Runtime Telemetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.04981)
*Yujia Luo,Haonan Zhang,Jiasi Shen,Zishuo Ding,Weiyi Shang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过埋点-收集-压缩运行时遥测并将简明遥测摘要反馈给 LLM，TeleGen 在诊断并修复交互级失败方面显著优于传统基于结果/错误信息的修复流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的生成-执行-修复流程仅依赖任务结果或错误信息作为反馈，缺乏对浏览器动作到最终任务结果之间运行行为的可观察性，导致难以定位交互级别的失败（如隐藏路径、导航或前后端协同问题）。

Method: 对由 LLM 生成的前端应用进行自动化埋点，执行任务时收集原始运行时日志（包括 DOM/网络/导航/事件序列等），将长日志压缩为简明的遥测摘要（telemetry briefs），将摘要与错误/测试结果一并反馈给 LLM 进行迭代修复；在 WebGen-Bench 和 Web-Bench 上进行对比实验与消融分析。

Result: 在 WebGen-Bench 上，将不带遥测的修复成功率从 67.7% 提升到 76.2%（+8.5% 点）；在 Web-Bench 上将累计 Pass@2 从 21.7% 提升到 29.8%；消融表明原始遥测能提供诊断信号，而压缩后的遥测摘要在效果与成本之间达成更好的平衡；对导航、表单工作流与前后端协调失败改进最大。

Conclusion: TeleGen 通过在生成的 web 应用中埋点并收集运行时遥测，将原本不可见的交互流程暴露给 LLM，用以指导代码修复，从而显著提升交互任务的成功率。

Abstract: Large language models can generate runnable web applications from natural-language requirements, but many generated applications still fail interactive tasks. Existing generate-execute-repair pipelines execute the generated application and use task outcomes or error messages to guide code revision. However, this feedback often misses the runtime behavior between a browser action and the final task outcome, making interaction-level failures difficult to diagnose. Therefore, we propose TeleGen, an observability-enhanced framework for LLM-based web application generation. TeleGen instruments generated applications, collects runtime telemetry during task execution, and compresses raw telemetry logs into concise briefs for repair. We evaluate TeleGen on WebGen-Bench and Web-Bench. On WebGen-Bench, TeleGen improves task success from 67.7% with repair without telemetry to 76.2%, an increase of 8.5 percentage points. On Web-Bench, it improves cumulative Pass@2 from 21.7% to 29.8%. Ablation results show that runtime telemetry provides a useful diagnostic signal, while telemetry briefs make this signal more effective and less costly to use. Further analysis shows that telemetry is especially helpful for failures involving hidden execution paths, such as navigation, form workflows, and frontend-backend coordination.

</details>


### [248] [LLM-Based Test Generation: Information Sources, Generation Strategies, and Quality Evidence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05001)
*Yunhao Liang,Chengguang Gan,Ruixuan Ying,Hanjun Wei,Zhe Cui,Shiwen Ni*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文基于95篇文献构建了一个连接测试生成过程与质量证据的四维框架，系统化地解释了执行反馈、规范溯源、训练监督与评价证据之间的关系，并提出具体的基准与研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大模型在生成测试场景、可执行测试套件、断言、交互序列与模糊测试基础设施中的广泛应用，不同测试产物依赖不同的正确性信息来源，因而需要一个能把生成流程与用于证明测试质量的证据连接起来的统一框架，澄清评价与比较中的关键变量。

Method: 对95条经筛选的文献进行叙事性综述，按四个维度组织：测试目标与制品、训练与生成时可用信息、生成与学习机制、评估证据；综合单元测试、基于需求的测试、测试判定器（oracle）、API/GUI测试、模糊测试与学习型测试生成器的工作。

Result: 给出一套解释性框架与分类体系，阐明执行反馈如何提高可执行性但影响判定器独立性，说明规范来源与训练时参考监督对比较的重要性，区分下游代码选择收益与测试本身正确性的差别；并基于这些区分组织基准集合，提出未来研究方向。

Conclusion: 本文提出了一个将生成过程与测试质量证据相连的结构化叙事性综述框架，基于95篇文献（截止2026-09-29）沿四个维度组织该领域，解释了执行反馈、规范溯源与训练监督、以及下游代码选择收益与测试正确性之间的相互影响，并据此整理基准与提出研究议程（独立判定器评估、预算感知生成、仓库级评估、测试维护等）。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly used to generate test scenarios, executable test suites, assertions, interaction sequences, and fuzzing infrastructure. These artifacts serve different purposes and rely on different sources of information about correct behavior. A test that increases implementation coverage, a test that agrees with a reference program, and a test that detects a requirements violation therefore provide distinct kinds of evidence. We present a structured narrative survey that connects the generation process to the evidence used to justify test quality. Drawing on 95 curated source records through 29 September 2026, we organize the field along four dimensions: testing objectives and artifacts, information available during training and generation, generation and learning mechanisms, and evaluation evidence. We synthesize work on unit and requirements-based testing, test oracles, API and GUI testing, fuzzing, and learned test generators. The resulting framework explains how execution feedback can improve executability while also affecting the independence of an oracle, why specification provenance and training-time reference supervision matter for comparisons, and how downstream code-selection gains differ from test correctness. We use these distinctions to organize benchmarks and derive a research agenda for independent oracle assessment, budget-aware generation, repository-scale evaluation, and test maintenance.

</details>


### [249] [Rethinking Tool Design for Agentic RCA: A Controlled Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05009)
*Yu Luo,Rongchen Gao,Zhenhui Zhou,Changchang Liu,Yuliang You,Yongqian Sun,Shenglin Zhang,Qiuai Fu,Shijie Wang,Dan Pei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 工具层级与组合会在准确率、类型识别与耗时间带来权衡，且其收益依赖模型与系统，应按模型、诊断目标与系统定制工具组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLM代理被用于微服务的根因分析，但关于如何设计与组合其工具缺乏系统的实证指导；需要理解工具抽象层与组合对诊断效果和效率的影响。

Method: 构建24个结构化工具，分为L0（Python参考）、L1（指标访问）、L2（证据分析）、L3（诊断）四个层级。使用来自三套微服务系统的375个故障用例，评估8种工具配置并用Qwen3.7-Plus进行主实验，另外在共享子集上比较四个Qwen模型。度量包括top-1定位准确率、故障类型识别、处理时长与轨迹分析。

Result: 以Qwen3.7-Plus为例，L3在定位top-1上达82.8%（参考L0为85.4%），但单案耗时不足L0的一半；增加工具层级有助于故障类型识别却可能降低定位精度；轨迹分析发现代理在获取更多证据后可能覆盖先前正确的建议；不同模型（四个Qwen）对工具的响应不同，且工具配置在不同微服务系统间排名会变化。

Conclusion: 工具抽象与组合显著影响基于大模型的微服务根因分析性能：不同层级与组合在定位精度、故障类型识别与耗时之间存在权衡；模型选择与目标系统改变工具收益和最优配置。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents are increasingly explored for root cause analysis (RCA) in microservice systems, yet empirical guidance on how to design and combine their tools remains limited. We conduct a controlled empirical study of tool abstraction and composition across models and microservice environments. We implement 24 structured tools for metric access (L1), evidence analysis (L2), and diagnosis (L3), alongside a Python-based reference setting (L0). Using 375 failure cases from three microservice systems, we evaluate eight configurations with Qwen3.7-Plus and compare four Qwen models on a shared subset of four configurations. Our results show that tool configurations affect root cause localization and failure type identification differently. With Qwen3.7-Plus, L3 achieves 82.8% top-1 localization accuracy compared with 85.4% for L0, while requiring less than half the time per case. Adding tool levels can improve type identification while reducing localization accuracy. Trajectory analysis reveals cases in which agents override correct diagnostic recommendations after consulting additional evidence. Model choice also changes tool benefits: adding L3 to L1+L2 improves diagnosis for three models but hurts Qwen3-8B, which rarely invokes L3. Tool configuration rankings further change across microservice systems. These findings provide an empirical foundation for designing and using RCA tools, guiding tool selection and composition according to the model, diagnostic objective, and target system.

</details>


### [250] [Characterizing Security Effects of OSS Vulnerabilities in Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05036)
*Yu Ji,Yang Wei,Yutao Hu,Haojun Zhao,Yueming Wu,Deqing Zou*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过差分执行与跨层运行时溯源，本文能判断 OSS 漏洞在代理工作流中是否被触发并定位其传播边界，从而支持基于执行后果的风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件代理越来越依赖开源组件，组件中的安全缺陷可能超出软件开发过程本身，影响代理的输出、内部状态及后续暴露给模型的信息。需要一种能在具体执行中判断漏洞是否被触发并识别其影响止点的分析方法。

Method: 作者在代理工作流中演练已知 OSS 漏洞，观察其在运行时各层（工具输出、代理状态、模型输入等）出现与传播的模式，基于此提出一种结合漏洞感知语义信息、对比执行（有漏洞与修复版本）和多层运行时溯源的数据流/控制流跟踪方法，用以检测漏洞是否导致可观测的安全效应并定位其传播边界。

Result: 实验表明该方法能准确区分实际发生的漏洞效应并确定其在不同场景中的表现边界；在真实代理框架的已记录工作流上应用时，发现多个源自已知 OSS 依赖的安全效应，强调了按执行后果而非仅按漏洞存在来评估风险的重要性。

Conclusion: 本文结论是：仅知道开源依赖中存在漏洞并不足以判定安全风险，必须基于执行时的语义信息、差分执行与跨层运行时溯源来判断漏洞是否在具体代理工作流中实际产生可观测的安全影响，并确定该影响在运行时各层的最远传播边界。

Abstract: Software agents increasingly depend on open-source components when executing tools and interacting with external systems. Security flaws in these dependencies may therefore influence more than the software process in which they occur: their consequences can be carried through tool outputs, agent state, and information subsequently exposed to the model. Determining whether such a consequence is actually realized in a particular execution, and where its influence stops within the agent system, remains challenging.
  We investigate how known OSS vulnerabilities behave when exercised as part of agent workflows. Our study reveals recurring patterns in the way security-relevant consequences emerge and propagate across runtime layers. Building on these observations, we use vulnerability-aware semantic information, differential executions of vulnerable and corrected software, and runtime provenance spanning multiple layers to determine whether a vulnerability produces an observable security effect and to identify the furthest layer at which that effect remains manifested.
