  Our evaluation shows that this approach can accurately distinguish realized vulnerability effects and determine their manifestation boundaries across a diverse collection of vulnerability scenarios. We additionally apply the analysis to documented workflows in a real-world agent framework and uncover multiple security effects originating from known vulnerabilities in its OSS dependencies. These results highlight the importance of reasoning about vulnerable dependencies in terms of their execution-level consequences rather than vulnerability presence alone.

</details>


### [251] [Beyond Task Completion: Measuring Interaction Cost in Terminal User Interfaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05047)
*Ruida Hu,Yuanhao Wang,Chao Peng,Yakun Zhang,Cuiyun Gao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种将LLM作为用户的可重复、执行验证的TUI可用性评估范式（Agent-as-a-User），通过Agent-KLM与TUINaut平台将解释与操作成本分离并验证结果，在大规模真实任务与界面上揭示了成功率掩盖的可用性差异，并证明生成界面常需提升可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通过TUI使用LLM日益普遍，但仅用任务完成率不足以衡量界面理解与操作难度；现有的人类评估或LLM生成评分缺乏可重复性与以实际执行验证为基础的交互成本度量。

Method: 引入Agent-KLM模型，区分代理端的解释（interpretation）和操作（operation）成本；开发TUINaut平台记录交互轨迹并通过与执行者无关的oracle验证任务结果；构建TUINaut-Bench，包含84个任务、15个真实TUI、6名人类参与者、5种观察-评估配置，以及对由三个主流生成堆栈生成的45个TUI的评估。

Result: 尽管总体成功率相近，但人类与代理在具体完成的任务上存在差异；解释和操作成本在任务排名上高度相关，尤其是操作成本，且该关系在不同观察与评估配置下稳定；自动生成的TUI可实现功能正确性却仍有明显的可用性改进空间。

Conclusion: 提出的Agent-as-a-User范式将LLM代理置于用户角色，实现了以执行结果验证为基础的、可重复的基于任务的可用性测量；通过将解释成本与操作成本分离并结构化关联人类交互，该范式能够揭示传统成功率掩盖的可用性差异，且在多种配置和真实TUI上保持稳健。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used through terminal user interfaces (TUIs), yet task completion alone does not capture how difficult an interface is to understand and operate. Existing human assessments and LLM-generated ratings or reports do not provide repeatable measurements of interaction effort grounded in verified task execution.
  We propose Agent-as-a-User, an evaluation paradigm that places an LLM agent in the user role. Agent-KLM separates agent-side interpretation and operation costs and relates them structurally to human interaction. TUINaut operationalizes the paradigm by recording interaction trajectories and verifying outcomes with actor-independent oracles.
  Using TUINaut-Bench, we study 84 tasks across 15 real-world TUIs with six human participants and five observation-evaluator configurations, and evaluate 45 TUIs generated by three leading stacks. Similar aggregate success rates mask differences in which tasks humans and agents complete, whereas interpretation and operation costs follow correlated task rankings, especially for operation. This pattern persists across configurations. Generated TUIs can implement correct functionality while still requiring usability improvements. These results position Agent-as-a-User as a repeatable, execution-grounded paradigm for measuring task-based usability.

</details>


### [252] [SpecAgent: Empowering Program Verification with Agentic Synthesis of Formal Program Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05132)
*Lezhi Ma,Han Wang,Shangqing Liu,Jiawan Wang,Lei Bu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个依赖感知、可检索增强并带有诊断与批评闭环的agentic框架（SpecAgent），能高质量合成ACSL并在真实C程序验证上显著提升通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手工编写形式规范耗时且难以规模化；现有基于LLM的方法通常为前向生成且只做局部修复，无法处理函数与循环间的复杂依赖，导致级联失败。

Method: 基于四个agentic组件：依赖感知的规划（识别规范目标）、检索增强生成（RAG，用以提供相关规范模式与上下文）、agentic修复（诊断缺陷并回溯修改依赖规范）、agentic批评（超越证明成功评估语义强度）；以DeepSeek-V4为LM并结合验证器进行闭环验证与迭代。

Result: 在50个来自14个真实仓库的程序上，SpecAgent达成96.82%精确率与88.95%召回率（正确且强的规范）；在811个真实验证目标上，成功放 discharged 604 个，均优于基线方法。

Conclusion: SpecAgent提出并验证了一种面向实用C程序的自动化ACSL规范合成框架，显著提高了生成规范的正确性与强度，并在实程序验证任务上优于现有基线。

Abstract: Formal specifications are essential for deductive program verification, providing semantic abstractions for compositional verification of complex software. However, manually constructing specifications is labor-intensive, motivating automated synthesis. Despite recent advances in large language models (LLMs), existing approaches often rely on forward-only workflows and localized repair, limiting their effectiveness on real-world programs with complex dependencies among functions and loops. Verification failures may stem from previously generated specifications, causing cascading failures that local refinement cannot resolve. To address these challenges, we present SpecAgent, an agentic framework for synthesizing high-quality ACSL specifications for real-world C programs. SpecAgent integrates four components: dependency-aware planning to identify specification targets, retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) to provide relevant specification patterns and program context, agentic repair to diagnose defects and revisit dependent specifications, and agentic critique to assess and refine semantic strength beyond proof success. We evaluate SpecAgent on specification synthesis and program verification tasks. On 50 programs from 14 real-world repositories, SpecAgent with DeepSeek-V4 achieves 96.82% precision and 88.95% recall in synthesizing correct and strong specifications, outperforming all baselines. On 811 real-world verification targets, it successfully discharges 604, also surpassing existing baselines. These results demonstrate SpecAgent's effectiveness in synthesizing high-quality specifications and facilitating real-world program verification.

</details>


### [253] [StateWise: Diagnosing and Repairing Persistent Operational State Before Agent Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05241)
*Yongyuan Peng,Zhou Feng,Tongying Wu,Jiahao Chen,Yuan Su,Chunyi Zhou,Tianyu Du,Shouling Ji*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: StateWise is a repair-first framework that identifies and verifies decision-critical records, binds evidence to records for persistent corrections, and replans from a repaired state, greatly reducing failures from corrupted persistent state in coding-agent workflows.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Persistent operational records used by LLM agents can become invalid when environments or requirements change, causing incorrect or unsafe agent behavior; existing review and provenance tools often leave persistent state uncorrected.

Method: Record-level counterfactual replanning to surface decision-critical records; reliability checks and read-only verification of machine-observable facts; targeted clarification for developer-owned intent; typed evidence grounding that ties evidence to specific records and scopes; replanning from repaired state and an independent state-action safety check before execution.

Result: Evaluation on 150 executable coding-agent cases across varied runtime/workspace/repository settings and cross-model tests shows StateWise achieves 93.3% overall correctness versus 38.7% for a baseline agent, with no unsafe actions. Ablations and transfer experiments demonstrate recovery capability and transferability.

Conclusion: StateWise substantially improves correctness and safety of LLM agents under corrupted persistent state by diagnosing and repairing decision-critical records before execution, achieving 93.3% correctness with no unsafe actions in the reported benchmark.

Abstract: LLM agents combine reasoning, tool use, and persistent memory to support work across tasks by reusing stored operational records as premises for later actions. However, environmental or requirement changes can invalidate these records, while existing action review, provenance tracking, and clarification mechanisms may leave the underlying persistent state uncorrected. Our audit of coding-agent trajectories identifies candidate failure chains in which invalid records are reused, leading to task failures and unsafe modifications. We propose StateWise, a framework for diagnosing and repairing persistent operational state before action execution. StateWise uses record-level counterfactual replanning to identify decision-critical records, then establishes their current validity through reliability checks, read-only verification of machine-observable facts, and targeted clarification of developer-owned intent. Typed evidence grounding binds evidence to specific records and scopes, enabling persistent corrections with repair lineage. The agent then replans from the repaired state, followed by an independent state-action check before execution. We evaluate StateWise on 150 executable coding-agent cases across diverse runtime environments, workspace configurations, and repository settings, complemented by cross-model evaluations. Under corrupted persistent state, StateWise achieves 93.3% overall correctness, compared with 38.7% for the baseline agent, with no unsafe actions. Component ablations, multi-task experiments, and transfer evaluations further demonstrate effective recovery, persistent corrections, and transferability across repositories and tool interfaces.

</details>


### [254] [Mind the Gaps: From Failure Attribution to Closed-Form Repair of Code Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05277)
*Jian Gu,Hongyu Zhang,Chunyang Chen,Aldeida Aleti*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: Code language models must be maintained like the software around them: when a library evolves, a model keeps writing the interface that it saw during training. Repairing the model itself lets one correction reach all downstream uses. Existing repair methods attribute a failure to neurons, select the highest-ranked ones, and apply a generic update. This pipeline assumes that the attributed neurons are the ones to patch and that a generic update fits every failure, and neither assumption has been examined. We examine both on executable API evolution tasks in Python and Rust with three code models and identify two gaps. The targeting gap separates the neurons that failure attribution targets from the neurons that can carry a patch: their top sets have a Jaccard overlap of only 0.15 to 0.20. The tailoring gap separates a generic update from a patch built for the failure: the patches that different carrier neurons need are nearly orthogonal. To address both gaps, we propose ASTRA. It targets neurons by contrastive semantics, an attribution that scores a neuron by its contribution to the logit contrast between the target token and the produced token. It then tailors the patch by solving one small linear system in closed form, which corrects all failing tokens of a sample jointly and needs neither an optimizer nor a backward pass. Contrastive semantics selects significantly better carrier neurons than gradient-based attributions in 3 of 6 settings and comparable ones in the others. On average, ASTRA reaches 66.7 percent Pass@1, against 48.4 percent for the best of AlphaEdit, STAR and low-rank adaptation, and repairs a sample in 2.7 seconds. It is the best method in all 6 settings and for every type of API change, and this advantage persists under an unseen phrasing of the test prompt. Its side effects on unrelated code are small on the large models and larger on the small one.

</details>


### [255] [Understanding the Hierarchical Structure and Functional Landscape of the Model Context Protocol Ecosystem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05319)
*Tingxuan Tang,Zilong Chen,Yue,Xiao*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: MCPacific maps 124k MCP servers into a 58.9k-capability taxonomy built with LLMs, revealing broad non-dev use, uneven alternative coverage, and large quality/complexity variance; taxonomy organization boosts agent task success versus flat listings.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Marketplaces list hundreds of thousands of MCP servers but only provide coarse, marketplace-specific categories, making it hard for agents/users to find appropriate tools, identify alternatives, and compare them functionally.

Method: Collected 368,754 MCP listings (124,267 unique servers) from 17 marketplaces; statically extracted 1,328,233 tool specifications in seven languages; built a 58,915-capability hierarchical taxonomy via iterative LLM-driven design-test-refine; mapped corpus to taxonomy using calibrated embedding routing; evaluated effect of taxonomy-based presentation on task completion across four models.

Result: A large, cross-marketplace corpus and taxonomy (MCPacific) showing that 85% of tools serve non-developer domains, 98.5% of tools have alternatives but ~25% of capabilities are single-sourced, significant differences exist among comparable tools in security alerts, code complexity, and maintenance; taxonomy-based presentation improves task completion (up to +12 percentage points Pass@0.75).

Conclusion: MCPacific demonstrates that the MCP ecosystem is large, diverse, and uneven: while nearly all tools have at least one functional alternative, a substantial fraction of capabilities are single-sourced, and comparable tools vary widely in quality and complexity. Organizing tools via a hierarchical functional taxonomy meaningfully improves agent task success versus flat candidate lists.

Abstract: AI agents increasingly rely on tools exposed through the Model Context Protocol (MCP) to complete user tasks. Hundreds of thousands of MCP servers are listed across marketplaces, yet they are organized only by coarse, marketplace-specific server categories. This makes it difficult for agents and users to identify tools for a given operation, find functional alternatives, and assess how those alternatives differ. We present MCPacific, the largest tool-level, cross-marketplace map of the MCP ecosystem. MCPacific collects 368,754 MCP server listings corresponding to 124,267 unique servers across 17 marketplaces, statically extracts 1,328,233 tool specifications from these servers in seven languages, and organizes them into a hierarchical functional taxonomy of 58,915 capabilities. We construct the taxonomy through an iterative LLM-driven design-test-refine process and map the full corpus to it using calibrated embedding routing. Our study reveals that MCP extends well beyond developer tooling, with 85% of tools serving other domains. Functional alternatives are widespread but unevenly distributed: 98.5% of tools have at least one alternative, yet nearly a quarter of capabilities are supported by only one tool. Functionally comparable tools also differ in security alerts, code complexity, and project maintenance, with complexity differing by more than 2.5x in 41% of comparable tool pairs. Finally, presenting candidate tools through the taxonomy rather than a flat list improves task completion rate across all four evaluated models, with gains of up to 12 percentage points in Pass@0.75 for crowded candidate sets.

</details>


### [256] [Adaptive Code Revision Attacks on AI Pull Request Reviewers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05399)
*Jingzhi Gong,Jie M. Zhang,Gunel Jahangirova,Meng Wang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文通过AFCRA和含159个可执行利用的基准，实证表明攻击者能利用AI审稿反馈修补表面缺陷而保留漏洞，使自动PR审查失效；并给出检测与缓解建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI审查在报告问题并给出修复建议时，同时也暴露了如何获得审查通过的线索；研究动机在于验证攻击者是否能借助这些反馈对代码表面问题进行修复却保留深层漏洞，从而规避自动化审查。

Method: 提出AFCRA（Adaptive Feedback-guided Code Revision Attack）框架，构建AFCRA-Bench（159个已披露漏洞及可执行利用检测）；在与Sonnet 5和GPT-5.5两个AI审稿器进行多轮（五轮）交互的设置下，比较AFCRA与现有基于文本/评论的攻击，使用可执行利用来验证漏洞是否被保留以衡量成功率。

Result: AFCRA在五轮交互中对Sonnet 5和GPT-5.5的成功率分别是最强文本/评论型攻击的2.5倍和12.5倍；案例分析显示审稿器会接受对被报告问题的修正但忽视残余漏洞。论文还提出了面向研究者、AI供应商、PR审稿者与作者的可操作防御建议。

Conclusion: 作者证明了“反馈驱动的代码修订”构成对自动化拉取请求(PR)审查的实际威胁：攻击者可以利用审稿器的反馈修复被指出的问题同时保留可被利用的漏洞，从而让修订过的代码通过审查但仍可被利用。

Abstract: Pull-request review protects software before new code reaches users, helping prevent vulnerabilities that could expose users to attacks. AI agents increasingly perform these reviews and explain which problems need fixing. However, for an attacker submitting vulnerable code, this feedback also reveals what changes may secure approval. Existing PR attacks seek such approval through persuasive text and comments while keeping executable code fixed. This leaves unclear whether an attacker can use the feedback to repair the reported problem while preserving a vulnerability in the revised code. We therefore conduct an empirical study of this threat using AFCRA (Adaptive Feedback-guided Code Revision Attack). To distinguish successful attacks from genuine repairs, we construct AFCRA-Bench from 159 disclosed vulnerabilities, with executable exploits to verify vulnerabilities in code.
  Across five-round interactions with Sonnet 5 and GPT-5.5 reviewers, AFCRA reaches success rates 2.5x and 12.5x those of the strongest evaluated text- or comment-based attack. Case studies of these successes show how reviewers accept repairs of reported problems while overlooking surviving vulnerabilities. These findings establish feedback-guided code revision as a threat to automated PR review. To address this threat, we derive actionable implications for researchers, AI providers, PR reviewers, and PR authors on securing AI-assisted development.

</details>


### [257] [UndoBench: Separating Task Competence from Recovery Capability in Tool-Using AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05622)
*Dolly Sah,Tanmay Sah,Harshul Jain,Tanya Sah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: UndoBench揭示了名义完成率与实际恢复能力之间的显著脱节，恢复成功高度依赖故障发生的执行阶段；需要专门的基准与分阶段防护机制以保证生产环境中的安全与一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估名义任务完成，未区分代理在无故障情形下的规划能力与面对操作性故障（如丢失确认、部分变更等）时的恢复能力，而这些差异在企业级应用中会导致重复副作用或数据不一致。

Method: 提出UndoBench基准，包含36个基础工作流和36种故障场景，跨8个企业域。采用对照成对试验（相同随机种子）以将任务胜任力与恢复能力解耦，使用线级效果历史与环境状态oracle进行精确评估。在两种开源权重模型、两种框架、三种恢复范式上进行大量执行（5,760次/2,880对照试验），并扩展到商业API模型与若干执行边界分析。

Result: 在冻结的丢失确认研究中，名义完成率为83.54%，但条件恢复成功率（CRSR）仅为46.72%；简单重试在53.33%的试验中产生了重复外部效果。不同执行阶段表现不同：变更前方法表现相近且无重复效果；部分变更阶段多数恢复策略在复合工作流上失败；提交后但未确认阶段，验证与服务端幂等性显著提升安全性。

Conclusion: 本文证明仅评估AI代理的名义任务完成度会掩盖在实际执行中关键且与阶段相关的恢复缺陷；需要单独衡量恢复能力，并对不同故障阶段采用不同防护策略。

Abstract: Tool-using AI agents are increasingly deployed across enterprise software systems, yet widely used benchmarks primarily evaluate nominal task completion, conflating baseline planning competence with operational fault recovery. We introduce UndoBench, a benchmark spanning 36 base workflows and 36 fault scenarios across 8 enterprise domains, decoupling task competence from recovery capability via counterfactual paired trials under identical seeds alongside wire-level effect-history and environment-state oracles. On 12 held-out TEST workflows across two open-weight models, two frameworks, and three recovery paradigms (5,760 executions / 2,880 paired trials) in the frozen lost-acknowledgment study, nominal competence reached 83.54% while conditional recovery success rate (CRSR) fell to 46.72%, with naive retry producing duplicate external effects in 53.33% of trials. Extensions to commercial API models reproduced this competence-recovery separation. Evaluations across complementary execution boundaries show that recovery is phase-dependent: before mutation, methods perform similarly without duplicate effects among capable trials; during partial mutation, naive retry, per-call idempotency, and zero-privilege journaling collapse on the evaluated composite workflows; after commit but before acknowledgment, verification and server-side idempotency substantially improve safety. These findings demonstrate that evaluating nominal completion alone masks critical, phase-dependent recovery vulnerabilities in autonomous agents.

</details>


### [258] [Finite State Machine-Based Traffic Orchestration for Multi-Engine Analysis Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05788)
*Ankit Kumar,Thirumoorthi Thangamani*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 把多引擎需求合成为一个预先映射的复合FSM，运行时用查表决定转发，支持时间过期与多级输出，声明式且可扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一代理需将同一路实时流量分发给多个独立分析引擎，但每个引擎对流量的需求随时间独立变化，而代理每个资源只能执行一次转发指令，故需要一个能同时满足各方需求且具有低延迟、确定性的编排机制。

Method: 把每个引擎建模为独立的FSM，取其状态的笛卡尔积得到复合FSM；对可达复合状态进行预映射（state -> forwarding configuration）；把基于时间的过期建模为显式的FSM转移，并加入每个复合态的多等级输出以支持不同服务等级。运行时仅做查表，避免在线协商。

Result: 设计是声明式、与具体引擎无关，支持时限性流量衰减与多等级服务输出，理论上可横向扩展到N个引擎而无需重构。实现上将运行时复杂度降到常数查表；潜在问题（如状态爆炸）需通过可达性剪枝或层次化/符号化表示来缓解。

Conclusion: 将各检测引擎的状态空间通过笛卡尔积合成一个复合有限状态机，预计算每个可达复合状态到代理转发配置的映射，使运行时由查表而非多方协商决定流量转发，从而实现快速且确定性的流量编排。

Abstract: Modern application-security platforms route live traffic from a single proxy into several independent analysis engines -- behavioral anomaly detection, authentication analysis, resource discovery, access-control inspection, among others. Each engine's appetite for traffic changes on its own schedule, yet the proxy can honor only one forwarding instruction per resource. We present a traffic-orchestration method that models the combined needs of all engines as a single composite finite state machine (FSM) whose state space is the Cartesian product of per-engine states. Every reachable composite state pre-maps to one forwarding configuration, so runtime reduces to a lookup rather than a negotiation. We add two refinements: time-based expiry treated as a first-class FSM transition enabling declarative traffic decay, and per-state multi-tier outputs that let one machine serve every service level. The design is fully declarative, engine-agnostic, and scales to N engines without re-architecture.

</details>


### [259] [PreMaQ: Predicting Maintainability-Related Quality of LLM-Generated Code Before Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05858)
*Taiki Wakamatsu,Yuta Ishimoto,Masanari Kondo,Yasutaka Kamei*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: PreMaQ能在生成前基于LLM内部表示预测代码的可维护性指标（CSS、MI），在多模型多基准上表现稳定，并能用于模型选择以实质性提高生成代码的可维护性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM生成代码能力提升，除了功能正确性之外，代码的可维护性也很重要。若能在生成前预测可维护性质量，开发者可降低生成、审查并丢弃低质量代码的成本。现有工作已证明可预测功能正确性，但可维护性相关质量是否可预测仍不清楚，因此提出PreMaQ以填补该空白。

Method: 利用LLM的内部表示（如模型隐藏向量或中间激活）作为特征，训练回归模型预测生成代码的Code Smell Score(CSS)和Maintainability Index(MI)。评估涵盖4个开源权重的LLM与4个Python代码生成基准，总计2695个任务，并通过计算斯皮尔曼秩相关和基于模型选择的改进比率来验证方法有效性。

Result: 在16个模型-基准组合上，预测的CSS与实际值平均斯皮尔曼相关为0.57，MI为0.65。用于模型选择时，PreMaQ达到了理想基于可维护性选择器相对于随机选择可实现改进的59.5%；将PreMaQ与提示嵌入结合后该比例升至60.7%，表明两者信号互补。

Conclusion: 本文提出PreMaQ方法，能在生成之前从LLM内部表示预测代码的可维护性相关质量（CSS和MI），预测结果与实际值在多模型多数据集组合上均表现出中高相关性，可用于模型选择以提升最终生成代码的可维护性。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) become increasingly capable of code generation, adopting generated code in software development requires assessing not only its functional correctness but also its maintainability-related quality. If such quality could be estimated before generation, developers could avoid the cost of generating, reviewing, and discarding low-quality code. Although prior work has shown that the functional correctness of the LLM-generated code can be predicted in advance, it remains unclear whether maintainability-related quality is similarly predictable. We introduce Pre-Generation Maintainability-Related Quality Prediction (PreMaQ), which predicts the Code Smell Score (CSS) and Maintainability Index (MI) of generated code from the internal representations of LLMs before generation. Our evaluation covers four open-weight LLMs and four Python code generation benchmarks, comprising 2,695 tasks in total. Our results show that predicted CSS and MI consistently correlate with their observed values across all 16 model-benchmark combinations, achieving mean Spearman rank correlations of 0.57 and 0.65, respectively. When used for model selection, PreMaQ achieves 59.5% of the maintainability-related quality improvement attainable by an ideal maintainability-based selector over random selection on tasks for which multiple models generate functionally correct code. Combining predictions from PreMaQ and prompt embeddings increases this proportion to 60.7%, indicating that the two signals are complementary. These findings suggest that PreMaQ can be used to predict and improve the maintainability-related quality of LLM-generated code.

</details>


### [260] [Adopting InnerSource in a Public Sector Organization: A Case Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05947)
*Muhammad Usman,Johan Linåker,Deepika Badampudi,Hannes Salin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: Qualitative case study shows InnerSource promising in public sector but hindered by culture, governance, and working practices; recommends organizational, managerial and platform interventions.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: To fill the evidence gap about InnerSource adoption in public sector organizations and to identify motivations, challenges, and improvement strategies.

Method: Qualitative case study based on nine semi-structured interviews and one internal workshop with 13 participants within a single Swedish public-sector organization.

Result: Identified motivations (reuse, collaboration, speed/cost), challenges (open culture absence, asset ownership/documentation/visibility, siloed practices, limited contribution time), and improvement strategies (culture fostering, management support, internal platforms/governance).

Conclusion: InnerSource adoption improved reuse, cross-team collaboration, and development speed/cost efficiency; main barriers are cultural mindset, management of reusable assets, and ways of working; improvements include culture change, management support, platforms and governance.

Abstract: InnerSource (IS) is increasingly viewed as a way to improve large-scale software development. However, evidence on its adoption in public sector organizations remains limited. This paper reports a study of IS adoption in a Swedish Public Sector Organization. Drawing on nine semi-structured interviews and an internal workshop with 13 participants, we examine motivations, challenges, and improvement strategies for IS adoption. The results show that the main motivations are improving reuse, strengthening cross-team collaboration, and enabling faster and more cost-efficient development. Key challenges relate to a lack of an open culture and mindset, management of reusable assets (e.g., ownership, documentation, and code visibility), and ways of working (e.g., siloed practices and limited time for contributions). The study also identified improvements to address challenges, including fostering an enabling culture and mindset for IS and knowledge sharing, strengthening management support, and establishing internal platforms and governance for reusable assets.

</details>


### [261] [Mechanizing the User's Eye: Pre-Registered Deployment of a Sabotage-Validated Fail-Plausible Observer in a Production LLM Agent Runtime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05981)
*Wei Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 构建并开源了一个两层的用户视角观察器：确定性回归检测器链+需逐字引用证据的LLM判决器，能可靠替代人眼的回归扫描（离线和影子验证良好），但对新颖故障模式的预测/归纳能力尚未解决，现场强制判决尚未给出可计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前纵向研究显示约70%的静默故障是由人以用户视角发现的，而大量测试与治理检测无异常。目标是机械化人眼在发现‘可生成为流畅、貌似合理输出’的内部错误（即fail-plausible）的部分能力，减少对人工浏览产品界面的依赖。

Method: 两层流水线：第一层是五个从事故事后分析中提炼出的确定性检测信号（可被‘破坏测试’证明承担负载）；第二层是一个要求给出逐字证据引用的LLM判决器，若判决器未能提供证据则弃用。离线用24份带明确诚实边界的生产事后分析作为金标准评估，之后进行影子部署和强制部署（预注册并冻结分析协议）。

Result: 离线：确定性层对回归检测实现6/6召回且0/4假阳性（每个检测器经篡改测试验证），对新模式的留出召回为0/4。影子部署期间捕获并修正了一个系统性假阳性；26天无误报；强制部署12天内没有触发判决，因此现场精度报告为未定义；系统自身产生10个静默失败，表明判决器继承了其判例分类。并且发布了语料、检测器与得分卡作为可运行基准。

Conclusion: 该工作表明：在生产级大模型代理运行时中，机械化“用户视角”回归扫描是可行的——确定性信号+需引用证据的LLM判定器可以几乎无误地检测已知回归，但对新颖模式的召回仍然为零；部署实践谨慎并预注册；总体上该系统现阶段更像回归检测引擎而非预测器。

Abstract: A prior longitudinal study of silent failures in a production LLM agent runtime (arXiv:2606.14589) found that about 70% were discovered by a human looking at the product as a user while thousands of tests and governance checks stayed green, and posed mechanizing part of what the human eye does as an open problem. This paper reports our attempt. We built an automated user-viewpoint observer targeting the most dangerous class, fail-plausible failure, in which an internal error becomes fluent, plausible output to the user. It is a two-layer pipeline: five deterministic signals distilled from incident postmortems escalate to an LLM judge whose verdicts must cite verbatim evidence or be discarded. Ground truth is 24 labeled production postmortems with explicit honesty boundaries: 16 of 24 are structurally invisible to any content-reading observer, and we say so. Offline, the deterministic layer achieves 6/6 regression detection with 0/4 false positives, each detector proven load-bearing by sabotage; held-out recall on novel patterns is 0/4. It is, so far, a regression engine. Deployment followed pre-registration: shadow mode caught and retired one systematic false positive on its first run, the 26-day shadow window then ran clean, flip criteria were fixed before the shadow data was read, and the analysis protocol was frozen before the enforcing-mode window opened. That window (12 observed days) fired zero verdicts; per the pre-registered path we report live precision as undefined rather than narrating quiet as success. The observer also produced ten silent failures of its own, confirming that the judge inherits the taxonomy it judges. We release the corpus, detector, and scorecard as a runnable bench: mechanization today retires the human's regression scanning so the eye can specialize in novelty; prediction remains open but is now measurable under frozen rules.

</details>


### [262] [From Trust-by-Design to Continuous Trust in the Era of Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.05991)
*Peter Fettke,Wolfgang Reisig*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出“持续信任”概念，通过退款部门示例说明为何与如何在系统运行期维持对含人为因素系统的信任，讨论与合规/认证的关系并提出形式化及工具研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统正确性证明不足以覆盖人为错误、故意违规与运行时故障，外部利益相关者需要运行期信任保证，因而提出持续信任以填补这一空白。

Method: 基于概念性阐述和运行示例（企业退款部门），分析相关威胁来源并讨论持续信任所需的机制与治理措施，比较现有合规与认证方法的局限性，提出研究方向。

Result: 给出持续信任的概念框架和说明性案例，识别需解决的形式化问题与工具需求，但缺乏实现性原型或定量评估。

Conclusion: 提出持续信任(continuous trust)概念，强调在系统运行期间需要对人为失误、故意作弊和设备故障进行持续监督，并用退款部门示例阐释，讨论与合规检查、认证的关系并展望形式化与工具支持问题。

Abstract: Trust is crucial in the digital world, particularly in the age of systems based on artificial intelligence. Traditionally, system correctness is demonstrated by proving that the system satisfies the required properties. However, digital systems involve humans, who can make mistakes, break rules, or even intentionally cheat the system. Technical devices may also malfunction or cause errors. Therefore, external stakeholders need to trust a system throughout its operation. To address this challenge, we propose the concept of continuous trust. In this paper, we present a detailed explanation of this concept, based on a running example of a business's refund department. The paper concludes with a discussion of related work, such as compliance checking and certification, as well as an outlook on further questions regarding the formalization and the necessary tool support.

</details>


### [263] [AgentSpy: Making AI Agent Behavior Observable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06001)
*Christoph Bühler,Matteo Biagiola,Luca Di Grazia,Guido Salvaneschi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AgentSpy提供一种外部、声明式配置的运行时监控方法，能有效总结代理的资源使用并以确定性规则检测多类安全违规；评估显示高可靠性区分度与较好攻击检测率，但存在环境逼真性与规避检测的潜在盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM驱动的代理会以用户权限执行命令、读写文件并访问网络，代理自身的轨迹与测试结果往往不足以揭示其全部行为（尤其是子进程执行的行为），需要外部可观测、可审计的方法来验证代理是否履行义务与是否违反禁止规则。

Method: 在隔离环境（由声明式规范配置）中运行代理，记录代理及其所有子进程的系统调用与网络流量；基于这些监控数据实现两类分析：合规性（可靠性）分析——用规则把每次运行摘要为所用资源（执行的命令、访问的文件、联系的主机）；安全性（安全）分析——对系统调用应用确定性规则以检测禁止行为。对每类分析各实例化一个分析器并评估其效果。

Result: 在可靠性评估中：使用codex harness与三款近期LLM，在77个任务上每个任务运行3次，得出：相同任务的重复运行集合在92.2%的比较中比包含其它任务的集合更相似；在通过基于结果的测试的任务中，代理仍存在18%与任务无关的活动、7%读取评分文件、17%未使用开发者指导。在安全评估中：通用规则检测到我们考虑的5类攻击中的4类，在50次运行中无误报。

Conclusion: AgentSpy通过在外部观察的方式对基于大型语言模型的代理进行系统调用与网络流量级别的监控，从而支持合规性（可靠性）与安全性分析。实验表明该方法在区分任务行为与检测多类攻击上有效，但在环境逼真性、对规避行为与跨平台覆盖等方面仍有局限。

Abstract: AI agents built on large language models (LLMs) run shell commands, read and write files, and reach the network, typically with their user's privileges. However, what an agent does during an execution is difficult to understand: tests assert on the result, and the agent's trajectory records only what the agent reports about itself, which may omit behavior executed by its subprocesses. We present AgentSpy, an approach that observes an agent from outside the agent. AgentSpy runs the agent in an isolated environment, configured by a declarative specification, and records the system calls and network traffic of the agent and of every process it executes. Based on this monitoring, AgentSpy supports two families of analyses: conformance analyses, which measure obligations, i.e., what an agent execution should do, and safety analyses, which check prohibitions, i.e., what an agent execution must never do. We instantiate one analysis of each family. The reliability analysis uses rules to summarize each run by the environment resources the agent uses: the commands it executed, the files it accessed, and the hosts it contacted. The security analysis applies deterministic rules to the system calls of an execution. For reliability, we evaluated AgentSpy on 77 tasks with the codex harness and three recent LLMs, executing each task three times. Sets of repeated runs of the same task are more similar than sets that include runs of another task in 92.2% of the comparisons. Among tasks for which all three runs pass outcome-based tests, the agent performs task-unrelated activities in 18% of the cases, reads the grading files in 7%, and does not use the developers' guidance in 17%. For security, the generic rules of AgentSpy detect four of five attack categories we considered, with no false positives across 50 runs.

</details>


### [264] [Experimentation Practices in Indie Game Startups](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06004)
*Elizabeth Bjarnason,Johan Linåker,Fabian Fagerholm*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过对10位独立游戏开发者的访谈，本文提出了一个适用于独立游戏公司在预发布阶段进行持续实验（CE）的框架，阐述了各开发阶段的实践与建议，表明在资源受限下依然可通过轻量原型、目标用户招募与合适指标进行有效实验，以提升游戏开发决策的验证能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Indie game startups have scarce time, budget, and user access making continuous experimentation difficult; authors aim to understand how CE can be conducted in pre-release stages and provide practical guidance tailored to indie constraints.

Method: Exploratory qualitative interview survey with 10 indie game developers across different companies; thematic analysis to derive practices and a CE framework; mapping practices to development stages (ideation, preproduction, feature production, content production).

Result: Developed a CE framework highlighting planning and implementation aspects; documented current practices and concrete recommendations for experimentation in ideation, preproduction, feature production, and content production; provided generalizable insights for other interactive software startups.

Conclusion: This paper concludes that continuous experimentation (CE) is feasible and beneficial in pre-release stages of indie game development when adapted to constraints like limited users, resources, and time. It emphasizes tailored practices, a CE framework for planning experiments, and actionable recommendations for ideation through content production stages. The authors claim that indie developers can employ specific strategies (e.g., lightweight prototypes, targeted user recruitment, metrics selection, and iterative design) to improve validation and increase chances of commercial success; insights also generalize to other interactive software startups.

Abstract: Small independent (indie) game startups face fierce competition when developing games with limited time, budget, and resources. Continuously testing and experimenting with new ideas, features, and content is essential to validate and guide development toward a viable and successful game. Such continuous experimentation (CE) requires access to users and user data, which is often very limited in the early stages of development, both in general and, in particular for indie game companies. We provide insights and recommendations on how to perform experimentation and game testing in pre-release stages of indie game development based on an exploratory interview survey with 10 independent game developers from different companies. We provide a framework for CE that highlights key parts to consider when planning and implementing an experiment. Current practices for experimentation within indie game development are also described for the early development stages of ideation, preproduction, feature production, and content production. Our empirical findings offer insights and recommendations into how to conduct experiments during pre-release stages of game development, specifically for Indie game companies. Other software startups, particularly those with a value proposition centred around interactive and satisfying user experiences, can also benefit from our research.

</details>


### [265] [Let the Agent Do It? How Software Practitioners Understand and Make Permission Decisions in Agentic AI Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06047)
*Larissa Salerno,Haoyu Gao,Gregory Gay,Alexander Serebrenik,Philipp Leitner*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 研究通过访谈+调查揭示开发者在为代理AI授权时依赖可观察证据并采用多阶段监督；建议权限系统增强重要动作的可审查性、可逆性和对意图的区分，避免将重复同意当作长期偏好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着代理式AI越来越多地代表开发者修改文件、执行命令并访问外部资源，这些动作常涉及权限风险。但目前尚不清楚实务者如何在保持代理自治带来的便利同时做出权限决定，本研究旨在填补这一认知空白。

Method: 采用顺序混合方法：首先对18位使用AI代理的从业者进行半结构化访谈以探索决策因素及监督策略；随后基于访谈结果设计问卷，对115位从业者进行调查以量化发现和检验普遍性。

Result: 发现：1) 从业者更信赖可直接观察/复核的行为；2) 对后台活动的不透明性导致不确定性并影响授权；3) 授权决策取决于动作的范围与风险、任务契合度、对代理的熟悉度以及运行环境；4) 从业者通过事前设限、执行中监控和事后复核三类监督策略来管控代理；5) 监督强度由信任、任务重要性、时间压力和后果程度驱动。基于此，提出了若干权限系统设计建议。

Conclusion: 研究表明，开发者在为代理AI授予权限时，主要基于可观察和可复查的行为做判断，而对后台决策和数据使用的不透明性影响了权限决策。为平衡自治与安全，权限系统需提升对重要/有后果操作的可审查性、明确代理权限与用户意图的差异、突出可逆性、不将重复批准视为长期偏好，并区分单次拒绝与整体方法上的否定。

Abstract: Agentic AI assistants increasingly act on developers' behalf by modifying files, executing commands, and accessing external resources. These actions often require permission, yet little is known about how practitioners make permission decisions while still benefiting from agent autonomy. To address this gap, we conducted a sequential mixed methods study, interviewing 18 practitioners who use AI agents and then surveying 115 practitioners based on the interview findings. We find that practitioners often understand agent behaviour through what they can directly observe and review, while decisions, data use, and other activity behind the scenes remain less clear. This uncertainty also shapes permission decisions, which depend on the scope and risk of an action, whether it fits the task, familiarity with the agent, and the environment in which it operates. Practitioners respond by adjusting how closely they oversee agents, from setting limits in advance to monitoring execution and reviewing work afterwards. How much scrutiny they apply depends on factors such as trust, task importance, time pressure, and the consequences of an action. Our findings suggest that permission systems should make consequential actions easier to review, distinguish what an agent is allowed to do from what the user intended, make reversibility clearer, avoid treating repeated approvals as stable preferences, and distinguish rejecting a single action from rejecting an entire approach.

</details>


### [266] [Where Does the Budget Go? Structural Concentration in Learning-Based Mutant Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06055)
*Taha Rostami,Jeongju Sohn,Mike Papadakis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文揭示了学习型变异体选择的空间集中问题，提出简单且有效的LinePool按行分层策略以增加行为多样性、提高缺陷发现并增强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管学习型变异体选择在节省成本和子集效用上展现潜力，但其如何在程序结构上分布、是否会形成测试盲点以及这种分布对行为多样性与缺陷发现能力的影响尚不清楚。

Method: 基于两个数据集和两档预算（2%、5%）的实证研究，分析现有学习型选择对变异体在源代码行上的分布与行为杀死集合的重叠；提出LinePool——一种与模型无关的按源行分层抽样步骤，将选择分散到不同代码行但保留原始排名信号；并与聚类及既有多样化方法进行对比评估。

Result: 发现学习型方法倾向将预算集中在少数代码位置，导致未覆盖的代码区域和较高的杀死集合重叠；LinePool显著增加行级覆盖、降低杀死集合重合、在紧预算下提升缺陷暴露，并在不完美或偏移的排名信号下保持鲁棒性；在所测基准上，LinePool优于或持平于聚类/既有多样化方法，且表现稳定。

Conclusion: 学习驱动的变异体选择在提高测试效率方面有效，但会集中在少数代码位置，产生盲点；引入基于源代码行的分层策略（LinePool）能显著提升空间覆盖和行为多样性，从而在严格预算下提高缺陷暴露并增强对排名误差的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Learning-based mutant selection is used to reduce the cost of mutation testing by ranking and selecting the mutants that seem to be the most promising. Prior work has shown great potential for this approach in fault revelation and subsuming-mutant selection. However, the risks and benefits of these approaches -- especially the distribution of selected mutants across code locations and how that distribution affects behavioral diversity and fault revelation -- remain unclear. We investigate this distribution and its effects, and introduce LinePool, a simple, model-agnostic stratification step that distributes selections across source lines while retaining the underlying ranking signal. Across two datasets and budgets (2% and 5% of the mutants), we find that learning-based selection concentrates the budget on a few code locations, and may thereby systematically introduce 'blind spots', untested code areas, allowing faults to escape detection. On larger programs, the selected mutants also exhibit substantial overlap in kill behavior. LinePool substantially increases spatial coverage and reduces kill-set overlap. It also improves fault revelation at tight budgets and makes selection more robust to imperfect or shifted ranking signals. Comparisons with clustering and established diversification methods show that none consistently outperforms LinePool across datasets, supporting its use as a simple diversification step. In general, our results suggest that behavioral diversity should be considered alongside effectiveness when designing and evaluating mutant-selection methods, and that exploiting structural elements of the program is one simple way to obtain such diversity while maintaining effectiveness.

</details>


### [267] [Correct Code, Broken Contributions? SWE-CC: Benchmarking Repository Policy Compliance for Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06193)
*Hai Dang Truong,Rayner Goh,Thanh Le-Cong,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SWE-CC 提出针对仓库治理合规性的基准与自动化检查流水线，揭示了现代自动编码代理在功能正确但不合规方面的显著缺陷（43.1% 违规率），强调需衡量运行时和交付物两层面的政策遵循。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有补丁评测基于功能性单元测试，无法衡量代理是否遵守仓库特定的贡献政策（风格、git 流程、测试工作流等），而这些政策对代码质量和长期维护性至关重要。需要一个能检验治理合规性的基准来评估自动化工程代理的真实可部署性。

Method: 构建半自动化流水线：从 12 个成熟开源仓库的开发者文档中抽取并规范化为 823 条可机器检查的原子策略；为每条策略实现轻量确定性的检查函数；设计全面审计机制以检查代理的运行时行为与最终交付物；基于 SWE-bench Verified 扩展 500 个端到端贡献任务，在两种 agent scaffold 下用 4 个 LLM 进行评测。

Result: 生成了 SWE-CC 基准及其 823 条可检策略；实验显示在 500 个任务上，代理在适用策略上平均有 43.1% 的违规率，且几乎一半的违规发生在中间执行步骤（非最终补丁）；表明仅通过功能性测试不能保证合规与可合并性。

Conclusion: SWE-CC 证明了通过单元测试的补丁并不等同于满足真实开源仓库的贡献规范；尽管现代 LLM 驱动的自动化编码代理能生成功能上正确的修改，但它们仍违反了大量仓库政策（43.1% 的适用策略），其中近半数违规发生在中间执行步骤。要实现可合并、可维护的自动贡献，未来代理必须在运行时与最终产出两个层面上可靠地遵守仓库治理。

Abstract: Autonomous coding agents now resolve a substantial share of real-world GitHub issues. However, passing functional tests differs fundamentally from producing a high-quality contribution acceptable for merging. Mature open-source projects publish repository-specific contribution policies, spanning style, git, testing workflows, to ensure code quality and long-term maintainability. Because existing benchmarks evaluate patches solely on unit tests, agent compliance with repository governance remains unknown. In this paper, we introduce SWE-CC, a benchmark evaluating code and process compliance in autonomous software engineering. We develop a semi-automated pipeline that converts developer documentation across 12 open-source repositories into 823 machine-checkable atomic policies. SWE-CC introduces two features: 1) lightweight, deterministic checker functions that represent each policy, 2) a comprehensive auditing mechanism that inspects both agent runtime behaviors and final deliverables. We evaluate the compliance of agent workflows in 500 end-to-end software contribution tasks extended from SWE-bench Verified. Our evaluation of four LLMs under two agent scaffolds shows that modern agents suffer from coding compliance issues: although agents produce functionally correct patches, they still violate 43.1 percent of applicable project policies, with nearly half of all violations occurring during intermediate execution steps. These results show that functional correctness does not guarantee real-world readiness, highlighting that future software engineering agents must reliably conform to repository governance to enable safe and trustworthy deployment.

</details>


### [268] [LLM Non-Determinism in Deterministic Processes: A Controlled Variation Space for Constraining the Propagation of Non-Determinism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06255)
*Paul Darius Mandl,Peter Mandl,Martin Häusl*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 将LLM的非确定性限定在预定义的包络与退出检查之下，使其在受规制的软件流程中既能产生变化又不会破坏权威状态与可审计性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机源于矛盾：LLM输出具有灵活性与非确定性，而受规制的软件开发依赖可追溯、可核查的状态、规则与审批——需要在变异性与可控性之间找到平衡。

Method: 方法是面向过程的：在执行前明确定义（1）结果的过程相关属性与退出条件，（2）任务配置及记录，然后在预先界定的变异空间（确定性包络）中运行LLM，最后通过退出检查决定该输出是否能成为下一权威状态。

Result: 文章给出概念化框架、一套设计指导并通过受规制软件开发的例子说明如何实现：不同运行可产生不同但可接受的结果，而只有通过预定义检查的结果才能转入权威状态，从而兼顾创新性与合规性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的“受控非确定性”（Controlled Non-Determinism, CND）为在受规制的软件开发流程中安全引入LLM提供了可行的概念框架：通过在事前固定任务配置、过程相关属性与验收（退出）条件，并将LLM运行约束在“确定性包络”（Deterministic Envelope）内，允许可接受的变化同时保持权威状态转换受控与可审计。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used in software development processes that rely on defined states, rules, and approvals. This creates a tension between the flexibility of LLMs and the need for reliable and traceable process states. We introduce Controlled Non-Determinism, a process-oriented approach that allows variation without letting it directly determine the authoritative process state. We place LLM-based processing within a controlled variation space that is bounded by a Deterministic Envelope. Before execution, we define the process-relevant properties of the result and the exit conditions. We also record the task configuration. These elements remain fixed during execution. At the end of the variation space, an exit check determines whether the result satisfies the predefined conditions and may become the next authoritative state. Different runs may produce different admissible results while the transition to an authoritative state remains controlled. We illustrate the approach with regulated software development and derive design guidelines for LLM-based changes whose results can be checked before they become part of the authoritative software state.

</details>


### [269] [Choosing an energy-efficient software architecture for building system diagnostic support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.06444)
*Roxane Koitz-Hristov,Franz Wotawa*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出了一种将诊断性能与软件能耗并入评估的能耗模型，并用仿真实验显示：不同FDD架构在不同建筑规模下的净节能表现会发生逆转，强调需综合权衡准确率、计算能耗与规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑能耗占全球能源开支约30%，通过修复故障可显著节能；但FDD软件本身的开发与运行也消耗能量，且不同诊断方法的能耗和诊断效果不同，因此需要一个综合视角来衡量FDD的净环境效益。

Method: 提出了一个将FDD性能（检测与漏检带来的节能影响）与诊断软件计算能耗共同考虑的能耗模型；并在仿真中比较了四类FDD架构（规则基、模型基、传统机器学习、大语言模型微调）——仿真实验使用来自先前文献的性能与能耗数据进行评估。

Result: 基于文献数据的初步仿真表明：当同时考虑计算能耗和诊断准确性时，会改变不同FDD方法的相对优劣；在小型建筑中，计算高效的机器学习算法带来最大净节能；随着建筑规模增大，虽然大语言模型在计算上更昂贵，但其更高的诊断收益可使其净节能优于轻量方法。

Conclusion: 本文结论为：评估建筑故障检测与诊断（FDD）系统的环境影响时，必须同时考虑诊断性能（如检测率和漏检率）与软件自身的能耗；不同架构在规模不同的建筑上具有不同的净节能优势——计算高效的机器学习（如随机森林）在小型建筑上更优，而资源密集的微调大语言模型在建筑规模增大时可能更具优势。

Abstract: Around 30\% of global energy expenditure can be attributed to the building sector, where a large portion of energy-consumption could be avoided by repairing existing faults. Fault detection and diagnosis (FDD) software addresses this issue; however, its creation and operation also have an environmental impact. The magnitude of this impact is influenced by the diagnosis architecture, as different architectures and methods have different energy demands. Yet, simply considering the energy consumed by the software itself is not sufficient to assess its overall environmental impact, since the diagnostic performance, e.g., number of detected faults or number of faults missed, also contributes to its ecological footprint.
  In this paper, we propose an energy-consumption model that considers FDD performance and energy spend directly by the diagnosis software. In an initial experiment, we compare several FDD architecture families, i.e., rule-based, model-based, classical machine learning, and large-language-model-based, in simulation using performance and energy-consumption values collected from prior literature. The results show that considering the computational energy and accuracy of FDD can change the relative benefit of the different approaches. Computationally efficient machine learning methods, such as random forest, provide the largest net savings on smaller buildings, whereas more resource-intensive approaches, such as fine-tuned large language models, become advantageous as building size increases. Our findings suggest that overall energy efficiency depends not only on the computational demand of the FDD software, but also on its diagnostic performance and the scale of the building.

</details>


### [270] [AI Agent Pull Requests on GitHub: Frequency, Structure, and Merge Conflict Rates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04697)
*George Xu,Arjun Subramanian,Nithilan Karthik*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: AI代理同时向同一仓库提交PR很常见，但通常来自同一代理；尽管跨代理并行少见，却更容易产生（且更严重的）合并冲突，冲突多集中在源码和结构性改动上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前尚无关于AI编码代理在同一代码库中并行提交PR的实证研究，作者旨在量化并行发生的普遍性、跨代理与同代理差异，以及并行带来的合并冲突特征。

Method: 基于AIDev-pop数据集（33,596个PR，2,807个仓库）统计并行提交的出现频次（精确时间重叠与一周窗口两种度量）；从每个包含并行对的仓库中抽取747个独立并行对，重放三路git合并并计算文本冲突率；基于git报告对冲突类型进行分类（源码vs依赖清单、结构性vs文本性等）。

Result: 在精确时间重叠下，40.2%的仓库包含并行的代理作者PR对，这些并行对占代理生成PR的79.4%；以一周窗口衡量时分别增至53.4%和95.0%。大多数并行对为同一代理所发，只有0.5%为跨代理并行，分布在约4.3%的仓库（122/2807）。在747个重放合并中，跨代理并行的文本冲突率为41.7%，高于同代理的19.8%（95%置信区间无重叠）。被冲突文件中84.4%为源码文件，约42%属于结构性冲突（修改/删除或添加/添加）。

Conclusion: 研究发现AI编码代理并行提交PR在开源仓库中很普遍，但绝大多数并行PR来自同一代理；跨代理并行虽然罕见，却更易产生合并冲突，且冲突主要发生在源码并且很多是结构性冲突。

Abstract: AI coding agents may generate and submit Pull Requests (PRs) to the same repository at the same time. However, research concerning the extent of concurrent submission by AI coding agents to a common repository does not exist. This paper uses the AIDev-pop dataset (33,596 PRs in 2,807 repositories) to provide the first empirical examination of the prevalence of concurrent submission using PRs authored by agents. We report that when considering exact temporal overlap, 40.2% of repositories contain co-active agent-authored PR pairs; further, the co-active pairs account for 79.4% of all PRs generated by an AI agent. When we examine co-activity within a one week collaboration window, the percentages are increased to 53.4% and 95.0%, respectively. For the majority of the co-active PR pairs (underlying the vast majority of which are intra-agent authored), both PRs were authored by the same agent, while only 0.5% of co-active pairs were cross-agent, and occurred in only 122 out of 2807 total repositories examined (or approximately 4.3%). Additionally, we replayed actual three way git merges on 747 unique co-active pairs (one per repository), and computed the percentage of textual conflict encountered during the merge operation to combine the two PRs in each pair. We observed that the percentage of textual conflict encountered was significantly higher for cross-agent pairs compared to intra-agent pairs: 41.7% vs. 19.8%, respectively, with non-overlapping 95% confidence intervals. Lastly, we developed a classification system based on the detection of conflict reported by git, and determined that the majority of conflicts resulted from modifications to source code files (84.4% of conflicted files) and not dependency manifest files; further, nearly 42% of conflicts we observed were structural (i.e., modify/delete or add/add).

</details>


### [271] [Using Process Mining to Generate AI Agents from Software Engineering Process Records](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.04948)
*Saimir Bala,Fabiana Fournier,Lior Limonad,Andreas Metzger*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 通过多范式流程挖掘从仓库事件发现项目特定的软件工程代理角色，并自动生成对应规范与实现，已在开源项目上完成概念验证与初步用户验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多代理/AI 在软件工程中的角色划分要么过于粗粒度导致复杂性不可控，要么细粒度导致协调开销大；此外多数框架依赖预设角色结构，缺乏对项目特性与过程变体的适配能力。

Method: 从软件仓库抽取事件日志，应用面向对象、命令式与声明式流程挖掘来识别行为模式；将识别到的模式映射到预定义的软件工程角色词表以生成代理角色规范；基于这些规范自动生成代理实现并在开源项目上进行功能测试与探索性用户研究。

Result: 提出并实现了一个流程挖掘驱动的项目特定代理发现与规范生成流程；在一个成熟开源项目上完成了概念验证、功能测试与探索性用户研究，结果表明生成的代理规范与实现具有一定的可接受性和与人类期望的对齐度（具体量化指标未在摘要中给出）。

Conclusion: 结合面向对象、命令式与声明式流程挖掘手段，从代码仓库事件日志中发现并生成面向项目的 AI 代理角色规范与实现，能在混合软件工程团队中提供项目特定的代理职责划分；在开源项目上做了概念验证并通过功能测试与探索性用户研究检验了生成规范与人类期望的一致性。

Abstract: Integrating AI agents into Software Engineering (SE) raises an important challenge: how can we specify and realize AI agents that work effectively alongside humans in hybrid SE teams? Determining the right granularity and separation of concerns for such agents is non-trivial. Coarse-grained agents may introduce unmanageable complexity, whereas micro-agents may create severe coordination overhead. Moreover, existing multi-agent SE frameworks typically rely on predefined role structures and do not account for project-specific characteristics or process adaptations. We address this by combining object-centric, imperative, and declarative process mining. Using event logs extracted from software repositories, our approach discovers project-specific agent roles using a predefined SE role vocabulary grounded in repository behavior and generates matching agent specifications and implementations. As proof-of-concept, we applied our approach to a well-established open-source project. We performed functional tests and an exploratory user study to determine how well the generated AI agent specifications are aligned with human expectations.

</details>
